Gaze Heads: How VLMs Look at What They Describe
Authors: Rohit Gandikota, David Bau
First: 2026-06-12T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:59:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How a vision-language model internally solves the task of describing an image is far from obvious. We find that the model develops a specific mechanism for this: a small set of attention heads in its language-model backbone, which we call gaze heads, whose attention tracks the image region the model is currently describing. We find them with a simple correlation score from a few forward passes, using comic strips as a controlled testbed where narrative order is laid out spatially. These gaze heads do not just track the image tokens being described: redirecting their attention to a chosen region forces the VLM to describe that region instead. A single attention-mask intervention on the top-100 gaze heads, fewer than 9% of all heads, steers the model's answer to any chosen comic panel at 83.1% accuracy, while the same intervention on random heads fails to redirect the answer, and intervening on all heads destroys generation. The same lever also extends to continuous control: switching the gaze target mid-generation makes the model wrap up its current panel description and move to the new one within a few tokens. Beyond comics, the same intervention redirects answers to chosen regions in natural COCO images. The mechanism further recurs across model sizes from 2B to 32B parameters and across other VLM architectures, although some frozen-encoder families show no comparable head set. More broadly, this shows that targeted edits identified through mechanistic analysis can serve as practical inference-time levers for steering multimodal model behavior, without any retraining. Our code, interactive demo, and datasets are available at https://gaze.baulab.info/
中文标题/摘要
标题：凝视头：视觉-语言模型如何“注视”其所描述的内容
视觉-语言模型内部如何解决图像描述任务远非显而易见。我们发现模型为此发展出一种特定机制：其语言模型主干中的少量注意力头，我们称之为“凝视头”，其注意力会追踪模型当前正在描述的图像区域。我们通过在几轮前向传播中计算简单的相关性得分，在漫画条作为控制测试平台（其中叙事顺序以空间形式呈现）中发现了这些凝视头。这些凝视头不仅追踪正在被描述的图像标记：将它们的注意力引导至选定区域会迫使模型转而描述该区域。对前100个凝视头（占所有头不足9%）施加单次注意力掩码干预，即可将模型对任意选定漫画面板的回答准确率提升至83.1%；而对随机头施加相同干预则无法引导回答，对所有头干预则会破坏生成。同一机制还可实现连续控制：在生成过程中切换凝视目标，模型会在数个标记内完成当前面板描述并转向新面板。除漫画外，该干预机制同样可引导模型在自然COCO图像中回答选定区域。该机制在2B至32B参数规模的模型及多种其他VLM架构中普遍存在，尽管部分冻结编码器家族未表现出类似头集合。更广泛地说，这表明通过机制分析识别的定向编辑可作为无需重训练的推理时杠杆，用于引导多模态模型行为。我们的代码、交互式演示及数据集见 https://gaze.baulab.info/
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how vision-language models (VLMs) internally describe images by identifying a specialized subset of attention heads—called 'gaze heads'—that selectively focus on image regions being described. Using comic strips as a controlled environment, the authors detect these heads via a simple correlation metric across forward passes. Experiments show that redirecting gaze heads’ attention to a specific region forces the model to describe that region, with a single intervention on top-100 gaze heads (less than 9% of all heads) achieving 83.1% accuracy in steering output to a chosen comic panel, while random or full-head interventions fail or break generation. The mechanism also enables continuous control during generation and generalizes to natural images and multiple model sizes, demonstrating that targeted mechanistic edits can effectively steer VLM behavior at inference time without retraining.
该研究探索视觉语言模型（VLM）如何通过内部机制描述图像，发现其语言模型部分存在一组特殊注意力头——称为‘注视头’——专门跟踪当前正在描述的图像区域。研究利用漫画条作为控制环境，通过少量前向传播计算相关性得分识别这些头。实验表明，仅干预排名前100的注视头（占全部头不足9%），即可将模型输出引导至指定漫画格，准确率达83.1%，而干预随机头或全部头则无效或破坏生成。该机制还支持生成过程中的连续控制，允许中途切换目标区域，模型会在几token内完成当前格描述并转向新区域。此机制在自然图像（如COCO）及多种VLM架构和规模（2B至32B参数）中均具可迁移性，证明通过机制分析识别的靶向干预可在推理时有效引导模型行为，无需重新训练。
OmniVideo-100K: A Dataset for Audio-Visual Reasoning through Structured Scripts and Evidence Chains
Authors: Xinyue Cai, Chaoyou Fu, Yi-Fan Zhang, Ran He, Caifeng Shan
First: 2026-06-12T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://github.com/MiG-NJU/OmniVideo-100K
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current automated pipelines for audio-visual Question Answering (QA) generally adopt a ``video-caption-QA'' paradigm. However, these methods typically segment videos into short clips and generate separate descriptions for audio and visual modalities. This decoupled processing severs inherent associations between sounds and their visual sources, while independent clip processing often causes inconsistent descriptions of the same entity across segments. Furthermore, coupling long-text comprehension and QA synthesis into a single step often restricts models to localized events, yielding questions lacking long-term temporal connections and deep cross-modal reasoning. To address these issues, we propose an automated data engine featuring two mechanisms: (1) \textbf{Entity-Anchored Video Scripting} transforms videos into structured scripts, comprising summaries, main entity lists, and segment-wise audio-visual descriptions. The entity list serves as a global prior to ensure cross-segment referential consistency and reconstruct audio-visual associations. (2) \textbf{Clue-Guided QA Generation} prompts models to first mine cross-segment, multimodal clues from the script, and subsequently generate QA pairs based on these high-value clues. Leveraging this pipeline, we construct the instruction-tuning dataset \textbf{OmniVideo-100K} and a human-verified test set, \textbf{OmniVideo-Test}. Fine-tuning VITA-1.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B and Qwen3-Omni-30B on OmniVideo-100K yields performance gains of up to 20.59% on OmniVideo-Test, demonstrating strong generalization (up to 12.64% improvements) across established benchmarks like Daily-Omni and JointAVBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniVideo-100K：通过结构化脚本与证据链进行视听推理的数据集
当前的视听问答（QA）自动化流程通常采用“视频-字幕-QA”范式。然而，这些方法通常将视频分割为短片段，并为音频和视觉模态分别生成独立描述。这种解耦处理切断了声音与其视觉来源之间的固有联系，而独立片段处理常导致同一实体在不同片段中的描述不一致。此外，将长文本理解与QA合成耦合为单步操作，常限制模型仅关注局部事件，生成缺乏长期时序关联和深度跨模态推理的问题。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种自动化数据引擎，包含两种机制：（1）\textbf{实体锚定视频脚本化}将视频转换为结构化脚本，包含摘要、主要实体列表及分段视听描述。实体列表作为全局先验，确保跨片段指代一致性并重建视听关联。（2）\textbf{线索引导的QA生成}促使模型首先从脚本中挖掘跨片段、多模态线索，再基于这些高价值线索生成QA对。利用该流程，我们构建了指令调优数据集\textbf{OmniVideo-100K}及人工验证测试集\textbf{OmniVideo-Test}。在OmniVideo-100K上微调VITA-1.5、Qwen2.5-Omni-7B和Qwen3-Omni-30B，在OmniVideo-Test上性能提升最高达20.59%，并在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等基准测试中展现出强泛化能力（提升最高达12.64%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current audio-visual QA systems that decouple audio and visual processing and lack long-term reasoning, this work introduces OmniVideo-100K, a dataset built using an automated pipeline with two key mechanisms: Entity-Anchored Video Scripting, which structures videos into scripts with entity lists to maintain cross-segment consistency and audio-visual associations, and Clue-Guided QA Generation, which prompts models to mine multimodal clues before generating QA pairs. Experimental results show that fine-tuning models like VITA-1.5, Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, and Qwen3-Omni-30B on this dataset improves performance by up to 20.59% on the human-verified OmniVideo-Test set and achieves up to 12.64% gains across benchmarks like Daily-Omni and JointAVBench, demonstrating strong generalization.
为解决当前音视频问答系统中音频与视觉解耦、缺乏长期推理的问题，作者提出OmniVideo-100K数据集，通过自动化流程构建：首先采用实体锚定视频脚本机制生成含实体列表的结构化脚本以确保跨片段一致性，再通过线索引导问答生成机制挖掘多模态线索后生成问答对。在该数据集上微调模型在OmniVideo-Test测试集上最高提升20.59%，并在Daily-Omni和JointAVBench等基准上最高提升12.64%，展现出优异的泛化能力。
RATS! Patches Talk Through Registers: Emergent Parts in Register Attention Transformers
Authors: Timing Yang, Predrag Neskovic, Jansen Seheult, Wenchao Han, Anand Bhattad, Alan Yuille, Feng Wang
First: 2026-06-12T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:59:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When humans see a bird, they recognize far more than just "bird" -- they see a head, wings, and talons, a structured assembly of reusable parts that can be identified across every bird they have ever seen. We ask whether a self-supervised visual model can discover the same compositional structure on its own. To this end, we propose RATS (Register Attention Transformers), which decomposes the classification token into N learnable register tokens that route patch information through an L->N->N->L bottleneck via a three-step compress-communicate-broadcast attention. The N registers are partitioned across the H attention heads, so that registers assigned to different heads do not interact with each other. Without auxiliary losses or part annotations, each register spontaneously specializes into a proto-semantic region whose emerging structure resembles object parts. RATS surpasses all baselines by +12 mIoU on average across five segmentation benchmarks, with consistent gains on ADE20K (+1.11 mIoU) and COCO (+0.2 AP^m). Its register dictionary further exhibits part-level consistency and semantic proximity across related categories. Our results suggest that RATS may provide a useful architectural prior for structured and interpretable visual representation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RATS！补丁通过寄存器交谈：寄存器注意力变换器中的涌现部件
当人类看到一只鸟时，他们识别的远不止“鸟”——他们看到头部、翅膀和爪子，这些是可复用的结构化部件，能在见过的每只鸟中被识别出来。我们问，自监督视觉模型能否自行发现类似的组合结构。为此，我们提出了RATS（寄存器注意力变换器），它将分类标记分解为N个可学习的寄存器标记，通过三步压缩-通信-广播注意力机制，以L->N->N->L瓶颈路由补丁信息。N个寄存器分布在H个注意力头中，使得分配给不同头的寄存器互不交互。无需辅助损失或部件标注，每个寄存器自发专业化为一个原型语义区域，其涌现结构类似于物体部件。RATS在五个分割基准测试中平均超越所有基线12 mIoU，尤其在ADE20K（+1.11 mIoU）和COCO（+0.2 AP^m）上表现稳定。其寄存器字典进一步展现出相关类别间部件级一致性和语义相近性。我们的结果表明，RATS可能为结构化和可解释的视觉表征学习提供有用的架构先验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the human ability to recognize compositional object parts, this work investigates whether self-supervised visual models can autonomously discover structured part representations. RATS introduces a transformer architecture that decomposes the classification token into N learnable register tokens, routing patch information through a compress-communicate-broadcast attention bottleneck across L->N->N->L layers, with registers distributed across attention heads to prevent cross-head interaction. Without auxiliary losses or part annotations, each register spontaneously specializes into a proto-semantic region resembling object parts. Experiments show RATS outperforms all baselines by +12 mIoU on average across five segmentation benchmarks, with notable gains on ADE20K (+1.11 mIoU) and COCO (+0.2 AP^m), and its register dictionary demonstrates consistent part-level semantics across related categories.
受人类识别物体组成部分能力的启发，该研究探索自监督视觉模型是否能自主发现类似结构化表示。作者提出RATS（寄存器注意力变换器），将分类标记分解为N个可学习的寄存器标记，并通过L->N->N->L的压缩-通信-广播注意力瓶颈路由图像块信息。寄存器分布在不同注意力头中以避免跨头交互，使每个寄存器在无辅助监督下自发演化为类似物体部分的原型语义区域。实验表明，RATS在五个分割基准上平均提升12 mIoU，其中在ADE20K上提升1.11 mIoU、COCO上提升0.2 AP^m，且其寄存器字典在相关类别间展现出一致的部件级语义关联。
RepFusion: Leveraging Multimodal Priors for Denoising in Representation Space
Authors: Xichen Pan, Aashu Singh, Satya Narayan Shukla, Xiangjun Fan, Shlok Kumar Mishra, Saining Xie
First: 2026-06-12T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://xichenpan.com/repfusion
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used in text-to-image (T2I) systems, but they are typically limited to text encoding, while denoising is handled by newly trained generative backbones. The emergence of representation autoencoders (RAEs) shifts the generation target toward semantically structured visual representations, creating a latent space that is more compatible with pretrained LLM priors. Inspired by multimodal LLMs (MLLMs), where an MLP projector is sufficient to align clean visual representations with a pretrained LLM, we repurpose the MLLM itself as a noisy representation encoder, extending this mechanism from clean to noisy inputs. We present RepFusion, which uses the resulting MLLM outputs as the conditioning signal for a diffusion transformer. In controlled comparisons at similar inference budgets, RepFusion outperforms baselines that devote comparable capacity to newly initialized denoisers. These results demonstrate that MLLMs provide strong priors for denoising visual representations and that, by conditioning on evolving noisy representations, test-time compute can be productively spent on repeated MLLM conditioning in modern T2I systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RepFusion：利用多模态先验在表示空间中进行去噪
大型语言模型（LLMs）广泛应用于文本到图像（T2I）系统，但通常仅限于文本编码，而去噪则由新训练的生成骨干网络处理。表示自编码器（RAEs）的出现将生成目标转向语义结构化的视觉表示，构建出更兼容预训练LLM先验的潜在空间。受多模态LLM（MLLMs）启发，其中MLP投影器足以对齐干净视觉表示与预训练LLM，我们重新利用MLLM本身作为噪声表示编码器，将该机制从干净输入扩展至噪声输入。我们提出RepFusion，利用所得MLLM输出作为扩散Transformer的条件信号。在相似推理预算的受控比较中，RepFusion优于将相当容量分配给新初始化去噪器的基线方法。这些结果表明，MLLMs为去噪视觉表示提供了强大先验，且通过在演进的噪声表示上进行条件化，现代T2I系统可在测试时高效利用重复MLLM条件化计算。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better integrate pretrained language models into text-to-image generation, this work proposes RepFusion, a method that repurposes multimodal LLMs as noisy representation encoders to guide diffusion transformers. Instead of training separate denoisers, it leverages the MLLM’s ability to align visual representations with language priors, even under noisy conditions, and uses its outputs as conditioning signals during denoising. Experiments show that RepFusion achieves superior image quality compared to baselines with similar computational budgets, demonstrating that MLLMs can effectively serve as strong priors for denoising in representation space, enabling efficient test-time inference through repeated MLLM conditioning.
为减少文本到图像系统中训练独立去噪器的开销，该研究利用预训练的多模态大语言模型作为视觉表示空间的去噪先验。RepFusion将MLLM的投影器改造为噪声表示编码器，并将其输出作为扩散Transformer的条件信号。实验表明，在相似推理预算下，RepFusion优于同等容量的基线去噪器，证明MLLM能通过在测试时反复对演化中的噪声表示进行条件引导，有效提升去噪性能。
Instruct-Particulate: Scaling Feed-Forward 3D Object Articulation with Kinematic Control
Authors: Ruining Li, Yuxin Yao, Matt Zhou, Chuanxia Zheng, Christian Rupprecht, Joan Lasenby, Shangzhe Wu, Andrea Vedaldi
First: 2026-06-12T17:59:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:59:36+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://instruct-particulate.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing articulated 3D objects is important for animation, gaming, and robotic simulations. Recent neural networks can estimate the articulated structure of 3D objects, but their generalization remains limited by the scarcity of annotated data for this task. To address this gap, we introduce Instruct-Particulate, a model that takes a 3D mesh together with a target kinematic specification, including part descriptions, connectivity, joint types, and optional point prompts, and predicts the corresponding kinematic part segmentation and joint motion parameters. The kinematic specification disambiguates the task and allows the model to target annotations of different granularity, thereby making it possible to use more abundant heterogeneous training data. At test time, the kinematic specification can be obtained automatically from large-scale vision-language models, so the model can be applied to any input mesh. To train our model at scale, we construct a heterogeneous dataset of more than 150,000 articulated 3D objects, extending existing publicly available collections with data obtained by partially labelling other 3D models (monolithic or already decomposed into parts) with kinematic labels by means of vision-language models. Experiments show that our model generalizes better across categories and to AI-generated meshes, enabling articulated asset reconstruction from real-world images via image-to-3D models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Instruct-Particulate：通过运动学控制扩展前馈3D物体运动建模
重建可动3D物体对动画、游戏和机器人仿真至关重要。近期神经网络可估计3D物体的运动结构，但其泛化能力受限于该任务标注数据的稀缺。为解决此问题，我们提出Instruct-Particulate模型，输入为3D网格与目标运动学规范（含部件描述、连接关系、关节类型及可选点提示），输出对应运动学部件分割与关节运动参数。运动学规范消除了任务歧义，使模型可处理不同粒度的标注，从而能利用更丰富的异构训练数据。测试时，运动学规范可由大规模视觉-语言模型自动获取，使模型适用于任意输入网格。为大规模训练模型，我们构建了包含15万多个可动3D物体的异构数据集，通过视觉-语言模型对其他3D模型（整体或已分解部件）进行部分标注，扩展了现有公开数据集。实验表明，该模型在类别间及AI生成网格上的泛化能力更强，可借助图像到3D模型技术，从真实图像重建可动资产。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable 3D object articulation reconstruction in animation and robotics, the authors propose Instruct-Particulate, a model that predicts kinematic part segmentation and joint motion from a 3D mesh and a target kinematic specification. This specification, including part descriptions, connectivity, and joint types, disambiguates the task and enables training with heterogeneous, partially labeled data. The model leverages vision-language models to automatically generate kinematic specifications at test time, allowing deployment on any input mesh. Trained on a large, diverse dataset of over 150,000 articulated objects, the model demonstrates superior generalization across object categories and AI-generated meshes, enabling reconstruction of articulated 3D assets from real-world images via image-to-3D pipelines.
为解决3D物体姿态重建在动画与机器人领域中的规模化需求，作者提出Instruct-Particulate模型，该模型根据3D网格与目标运动学规范（含部件描述、关节类型等）预测运动学分割与关节参数。该规范引导模型利用异构训练数据，通过视觉-语言模型对单体或已分解的3D模型进行部分标注以扩充数据集。实验表明，该模型在跨类别泛化与AI生成网格处理上优于基线方法，可结合图像到3D模型流程，实现从真实图像重建可动3D资产。
ClinHallu: A Benchmark for Diagnosing Stage-Wise Hallucinations in Medical MLLM Reasoning
Authors: Sicheng Yang, Hangjie Yuan, Wenjun Zhang, Jinwang Wang, Yichen Qian, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang, Lei Zhu
First: 2026-06-12T17:58:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:58:38+00:00
Comments: Code and datasets: https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Building trustworthy medical multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is critical for reliable clinical decision support. Existing medical hallucination benchmarks mainly focus on data collection, but often ignore where hallucinations originate within the reasoning process. We find that hallucination sources vary across samples: errors may arise from visual misrecognition, incorrect medical knowledge recall, or flawed reasoning integration. To enable source-level hallucination diagnosis, we introduce ClinHallu, a benchmark for stage-wise hallucination diagnosis in medical MLLM reasoning. ClinHallu contains 7,031 validated instances, where each instance is augmented with a structured reasoning trace decomposed into Visual Recognition, Knowledge Recall, and Reasoning Integration. We also use stage-replacement interventions to measure how correcting specific stages affects the final answer. Beyond evaluation, we show that trace-supervised fine-tuning reduces stage-wise hallucinations. ClinHallu provides a fine-grained hallucination testbed for diagnosing and mitigating reasoning failures in medical MLLMs. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClinHallu：用于诊断医学多模态大语言模型推理中分阶段幻觉的基准
构建可信的医学多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对可靠的临床决策支持至关重要。现有医学幻觉基准主要关注数据收集，但常忽略幻觉在推理过程中的具体来源。我们发现，不同样本的幻觉来源各异：错误可能源于视觉误识别、医学知识回忆错误或推理整合缺陷。为实现按来源诊断幻觉，我们提出ClinHallu，一个用于医学MLLM推理中分阶段幻觉诊断的基准。ClinHallu包含7031个经验证实例，每个实例均附加结构化推理轨迹，分解为视觉识别、知识回忆和推理整合三个阶段。我们还采用阶段替换干预方法，评估修正特定阶段对最终答案的影响。除评估外，我们表明轨迹监督微调可减少分阶段幻觉。ClinHallu为诊断和缓解医学MLLM推理失败提供了细粒度的幻觉测试平台。该基准公开于https://github.com/alibaba-damo-academy/ClinHallu。
Summary / 总结
To address the lack of fine-grained diagnosis of hallucinations in medical multimodal large language models, the authors introduce ClinHallu, a benchmark that decomposes reasoning into three stages—Visual Recognition, Knowledge Recall, and Reasoning Integration—and annotates 7,031 instances with structured traces. By applying stage-replacement interventions, they quantify how correcting each stage impacts final output accuracy. Their experiments show that trace-supervised fine-tuning significantly reduces hallucinations across stages, demonstrating the value of stage-wise diagnosis for improving model reliability in clinical reasoning.
为解决医疗多模态大语言模型中幻觉缺乏细粒度诊断的问题，该研究提出ClinHallu基准，将推理过程分解为视觉识别、知识回忆和推理整合三个阶段，并对7031个验证病例标注结构化推理轨迹。通过阶段替换干预实验，量化各阶段修正对最终输出准确性的影响，发现幻觉来源因样本而异。实验表明，基于轨迹的监督微调可有效降低各阶段幻觉，为提升模型临床推理可靠性提供针对性改进路径。
Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing
Authors: Jinsu Kim, Jihoon Tack, Noah Lee, Jongheon Jeong
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-12T17:58:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:58:08+00:00
Comments: 25 pages; ICML 2026; Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Persona-Pruner：为角色扮演雕刻轻量级模型
语言模型（LMs）在作为角色扮演聊天机器人时展现出巨大潜力，当给定角色或用户人格描述时，能提供一致且风格化的交互。然而，将其应用于现实场景（如多个NPC同时交互的生态系统）时，因计算成本过高而暴露出关键效率问题。本文质疑是否必须为单个人格分配完整通用模型，假设特定角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分。我们观察到，盲目剪枝LM会严重损害特定角色的扮演表现，因为它无法区分冗余知识与关键角色特质。我们提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单个描述中分离人格专属子网络，雕刻轻量级角色扮演模型。实验表明，Persona-Pruner在保持角色扮演性能方面显著优于现有最先进的LLM剪枝技术，在RoleBench的LLM-as-a-judge评分中，性能下降较最强大基线减少高达93.8%，同时仍保留通用LLM能力。代码见https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of deploying large language models for role-playing in real-world applications, this paper investigates whether a full generalist model is necessary for a single persona. The authors propose Persona-Pruner, a method that isolates persona-specific sub-networks from a single character description to sculpt lightweight, role-playing-optimized models. Experimental results demonstrate that Persona-Pruner significantly outperforms existing pruning techniques, preserving role-playing performance with up to 93.8% less degradation on RoleBench compared to the strongest baseline, while retaining general language model capabilities.
为应对大型语言模型在角色扮演场景中部署时的高计算开销，本文质疑是否必须使用完整模型处理单一角色，假设仅需模型容量的一小部分即可支撑角色特性。Persona-Pruner通过从单一角色描述中隔离角色专属子网络来实现轻量化，避免了盲目剪枝导致性能下降的问题。实验表明，该方法显著优于现有剪枝技术，在RoleBench评测中角色扮演性能下降幅度比最强基线低93.8%，同时保持了通用语言模型能力。
AdaSR: Adaptive Streaming Reasoning with Hierarchical Relative Policy Optimization
Authors: Junlong Tong, Wenqi Xu, Yingqi Fan, Anhao Zhao, Xuan Lu, Yang Tan, Xiaoyu Shen
First: 2026-06-12T17:56:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:56:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large reasoning models typically follow a read-then-think paradigm: they observe the complete input, reason over a static context, and then produce the answer. Yet many real-world scenarios are inherently dynamic, such as audio and video stream, where information arrives as a continuous stream and models must reason, update, and respond under partial observations. Recent streaming reasoning methods allow models to think while reading, but they largely rely on supervised imitation of pre-constructed trajectories, which limits their flexibility. In this paper, we propose AdaSR, an adaptive streaming reasoning framework that enables models to reason during input streaming and perform final deliberation once the stream is complete, learning when to think, and how much computation to allocate across different stages. To optimize this hierarchical reasoning process, we introduce Hierarchical Relative Policy Optimization (HRPO), which decomposes policy optimization into streaming reasoning and deep reasoning phases, providing more fine-grained advantage assignment instead of uniformly distributing a single sequence-level advantage over all tokens. HRPO integrates format, accuracy, and adaptive thinking rewards to enforce valid reasoning protocols, preserve final task performance, and encourage latency-aware computation allocation. Experiments show that AdaSR achieves a better balance among reasoning accuracy, computational efficiency, and streaming latency compared with supervised fine-tuning baseline. We release our code at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/StreamingLLM/tree/main/AdaSR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaSR：基于分层相对策略优化的自适应流式推理
大型推理模型通常遵循“先读再思”的范式：它们观察完整输入，在静态上下文中进行推理，然后生成答案。然而，许多现实场景本质上是动态的，例如音频和视频流，其中信息持续到达，模型必须在部分观测下进行推理、更新和响应。近期的流式推理方法允许模型边读边思考，但它们主要依赖于对预构建轨迹的监督模仿，限制了其灵活性。本文提出AdaSR，一种自适应流式推理框架，使模型在输入流式传输期间进行推理，并在流结束时进行最终决策，学习何时思考以及如何在不同阶段分配计算资源。为优化这一分层推理过程，我们引入分层相对策略优化（HRPO），将策略优化分解为流式推理和深度推理阶段，提供更精细的奖励分配，而非将单个序列级优势均匀分配给所有token。HRPO整合格式、准确性和自适应思考奖励，以强制执行有效的推理协议，保持最终任务性能，并鼓励延迟感知的计算分配。实验表明，AdaSR在推理准确性、计算效率和流式延迟之间取得了比监督微调基线更好的平衡。我们的代码已发布于https://github.com/EIT-NLP/StreamingLLM/tree/main/AdaSR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for models to handle dynamic, streaming inputs like audio or video where partial observations require real-time reasoning, AdaSR introduces an adaptive streaming reasoning framework that allows models to reason incrementally during input arrival and perform final deliberation after the stream ends. It employs Hierarchical Relative Policy Optimization (HRPO), which splits policy learning into streaming and deep reasoning phases, enabling fine-grained reward assignment based on format, accuracy, and latency-aware computation allocation. Experimental results demonstrate that AdaSR outperforms supervised imitation baselines by achieving superior balance among reasoning accuracy, computational efficiency, and streaming latency.
为应对音频、视频等动态流式输入场景中模型需在部分观察下进行自适应推理的需求，AdaSR提出了一种在输入流式传输中逐步推理、待流结束时进行最终决策的框架。其核心方法是采用分层相对策略优化（HRPO），将策略优化分解为流式推理与深度推理两个阶段，实现基于格式、准确率和延迟感知的精细化奖励分配。实验表明，AdaSR相较监督微调基线，在推理准确率、计算效率与流式延迟之间取得了更优的平衡。
Learning Coordinated Preference for Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Pengxin Wang, Lihao Guo, Yi Xie, Bo Liu, Siyang Cao, Jingdi Chen
First: 2026-06-12T17:55:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:55:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cooperative multi-objective multi-agent reinforcement learning (MOMARL) models team decision making under multiple, potentially conflicting objectives. In this setting, conflicts arise not only across objectives but also across agents with different observations, roles, and contributions. We propose Preference Coordinated Multi-agent Policy Optimization (PCMA), which learns coordinated agent-specific preferences to enable complementary trade-offs among agents. Theoretically, we formulate cooperative MOMARL as a team-optimal game and show that, under suitable conditions, preference diversity can induce team improvement through a first-order improvement decomposition. Experiments on multiple cooperative MOMA environments and a practical traffic-control scenario show that PCMA improves both performance and trade-off coordination.

标题：多目标多智能体强化学习中的协调偏好学习
协作型多目标多智能体强化学习（MOMARL）模型用于在多个潜在冲突的目标下进行团队决策。在此设定中，冲突不仅存在于目标之间，也存在于具有不同观测、角色和贡献的智能体之间。我们提出偏好协调多智能体策略优化（PCMA），通过学习协调的智能体特定偏好，实现智能体间的互补权衡。理论上，我们将协作型MOMARL形式化为团队最优博弈，并证明在适当条件下，偏好多样性可通过一阶改进分解促进团队性能提升。在多个协作型MOMA环境及实际交通控制场景中的实验表明，PCMA同时提升了性能与权衡协调性。

Theoretically, it frames the problem as a team-optimal game and demonstrates that preference diversity can drive team improvement via first-order improvement decomposition. Empirical results across multiple cooperative MOMA environments and a traffic-control scenario confirm that PCMA enhances both overall performance and coordination of trade-offs among agents.
为解决合作多目标多智能体强化学习中智能体因目标冲突与角色异质导致的协调难题，本文提出偏好协调多智能体策略优化（PCMA）方法，通过学习智能体特异性偏好以实现互补权衡。该方法将问题建模为团队最优博弈，并理论证明在特定条件下偏好多样性可通过一阶改进分解促进团队性能提升。实验在多个合作MOMA环境及交通控制场景中验证，表明PCMA在任务表现与智能体间权衡协调方面均优于基线方法。
CORA: Analyzing and bridging thinking-answer gap in Multimodal RLVR via Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment
Authors: Jiayue Cao, Zhicong Lu, Xuehan Sun, Wei Jia, Hongling Zheng, Changyuan Tian, Zichuan Lin, Wenqian Lv, Nayu Liu
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-12T17:54:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:54:59+00:00
Comments: Submitted to EMNLP 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has successfully elicited the reasoning capabilities of large language models, motivating its extension to multimodal scenarios. Existing methods primarily focus on improving the visual coverage of reasoning traces and mitigating visual hallucinations, but underestimate the semantic inconsistency between the reasoning process and the final answer. In this paper, we delve into thinking-answer inconsistency in RLVR for large vision-language models (LVLMs), showing thorough analyses of rollouts collected throughout Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) training process and post-RLVR evaluation outputs that this issue persists during training and remains present during inference. Motivated by the analysis, we propose Consistency-Oriented Reasoning Alignment (CORA), which introduces thinking-answer semantic consistency into RLVR through a lightweight plug-and-play consistency reward model, and further incorporates Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting (HRAS) to stably coordinate task and consistency optimization. Extensive experiments across representative multimodal reasoning benchmarks and mainstream LVLMs show that CORA improves task performance while effectively mitigating thinking-answer inconsistency, leading to more faithful reasoning traces.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CORA：通过一致性导向推理对齐分析并弥合多模态RLVR中的思维-答案差距
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已成功激发大型语言模型的推理能力，推动其扩展至多模态场景。现有方法主要关注提升推理轨迹的视觉覆盖度并缓解视觉幻觉，但低估了推理过程与最终答案之间的语义不一致。本文深入研究大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在RLVR中的思维-答案不一致问题，通过对Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）训练全过程收集的rollouts及RLVR评估输出的全面分析，表明该问题在训练和推理阶段均持续存在。受此启发，我们提出一致性导向推理对齐（CORA），通过轻量级即插即用的一致性奖励模型将思维-答案语义一致性引入RLVR，并进一步结合混合奖励优势分割（HRAS）以稳定协调任务与一致性优化。在代表性多模态推理基准和主流LVLMs上的大量实验表明，CORA在提升任务性能的同时有效缓解思维-答案不一致，生成更忠实的推理轨迹。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by persistent semantic inconsistency between reasoning traces and final answers in multimodal RLVR, this work introduces CORA, a lightweight plug-and-play consistency reward model that enforces thinking-answer alignment during training. Combined with Hybrid Reward Advantage Splitting for stable optimization, CORA is evaluated across multiple multimodal benchmarks and LVLMs, demonstrating improved task performance while significantly reducing reasoning-answer mismatches, resulting in more faithful and consistent reasoning traces.
为解决多模态RLVR中推理过程与最终答案语义不一致的问题，本文提出CORA方法，通过轻量级一致性奖励模型在训练中对齐推理与答案，结合混合奖励优势拆分机制以稳定优化。在多个多模态基准与主流视觉语言模型上的实验表明，CORA不仅提升任务性能，还显著减少推理与答案的不一致，生成更可信的推理轨迹。
A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation
Authors: Abdellah Aznag, Rachel Cummings, Adam N. Elmachtoub
First: 2026-06-12T17:54:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:54:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多组均值估计中主动学习的复杂度度量
我们研究了多组均值估计中$d$臂老虎机的主动学习的\emph{最大风险}目标：学习者自适应地将$T$个样本预算分配至$d$个组，以最小化最坏情况下的不确定性指标$\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$，其中$σ_k$是臂$d$分布的标准差，$n_k$是臂$d$被采样的次数。我们构建了局部最小最大框架，并证明了该目标的第一个通用下界，适用于任意有限方差假设类。该下界将难度分解为三个正交因素：\emph{预算}项、\emph{异方差性}指标（衡量不确定性在各臂间分布的不均匀程度），以及模型依赖的复杂度度量——\emph{方差局部曲率}（$\mathrm{VLC}$），它衡量在假设类内部局部方差变化所创造的信息量。对于光滑类，$\mathrm{VLC}$是方差-费舍尔信息的重参数化，常见族具有闭式解。与最强的现有上界对比显示，在广泛情形下近最优（至对数因子），并指出了在高度异质实例中存在系统性差距。我们的证明引入了两个关键要素：决策空间上的损失诱导的$\ell_1$几何结构，以及基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造简化为显式的随机矩阵计算。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize sample allocation in multi-group mean estimation under worst-case uncertainty, this paper introduces a max-risk objective and develops a local minimax framework to derive the first general lower bound for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound decomposes problem difficulty into three components: budget constraints, heteroscedasticity (uneven uncertainty across groups), and a novel model complexity measure called Variance Local Curvature (VLC), which quantifies information gain from local variance changes. Experimental comparisons against the best known upper bound reveal near-optimality up to logarithmic factors across broad settings, while exposing a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous cases. The proof relies on a novel loss-induced ℓ₁ geometry and a random matrix-based instance generator for constructing hard examples.
为优化多组均值估计中样本分配的最坏情况不确定性，本文提出最大风险目标并构建局部最小最大框架，首次为d臂老虎机中的主动学习推导出通用下界。该下界将问题难度分解为三部分：预算限制、异方差性（各组不确定性分布不均）及模型特定复杂度度量——方差局部曲率（VLC），用于衡量局部方差变化在假设类中所携带的信息量。与现有最优上界对比显示，在广泛场景下近最优（至对数因子），并在高度异质情形中揭示系统性差距。证明依赖于损失诱导的\ell_1几何结构及基于随机矩阵的实例生成器，用于构造困难实例。
Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit
Authors: Xiaoyu Li, Andi Han, Dai Shi, Zheng Gao, Jiaojiao Jiang, Junbin Gao
First: 2026-06-12T17:52:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:52:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.
中文标题/摘要
标题：洪水与收获：通过语言生成极限视角，证明琐碎性对生成有价值数学的必要性
当前，AI系统与证明助手结合可大规模生成形式化数学，而验证器可验证内容与数学家所珍视内容之间的差距已成为瓶颈。我们将有价值数学的生成建模为极限下的嵌套语言生成：一个可验证的形式语言 $F$，通过成员查询器（证明验证器）访问，其中包含一个未知的有价值语言 $H \in \mathcal{H}$，仅通过核心 $C \subseteq H$ 的对抗性枚举揭示（该核心密度为 $α$，即文献中所指）。每个输出要么有价值（$\in H$），要么琐碎（$\in F \setminus H$），要么是幻觉（$\notin F$）。我们解决了四个问题：第一，验证器并非审美判断：允许生成广度的集合，恰好是无查询器模型中的集合，由Angluin条件逐纤维刻画。第二，验证器确实能提供可靠覆盖：覆盖所有未见的有价值陈述，同时仅断言有效陈述；有验证器可行，无则不可行；它将不可避免的错误从错误转移到琐碎。第三，且为核心：在紧致族中存在尖锐二分：仅输出有限琐碎内容的生成器可达到最优覆盖 $α/2$，而任何允许无限琐碎内容（即使速率趋近于零）的生成器，其最优覆盖跃升至 $1-α/2$（两者均紧致，当核心以候选交集形式呈现），且存在一个生成器可同时达到两端。转变在于琐碎内容数量，而非速率；差距 $1-α$ 是未记录的质量。第四，两种模式均在数学压缩模型中实现。完美验证器无法替代审美判断：无限流的正确但无价值陈述并非工程事故，而是可证明的必要性，因为覆盖未记录的有价值数学需要一个无限但渐近可忽略的认证琐碎流。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between what proof assistants can verify and what mathematicians consider valuable, the paper models mathematics generation as nested language generation in the limit, where a formal language F (verified by a checker) contains an unknown valuable subset H. The authors analyze how trivia (verifiable but unvaluable statements) affects coverage of H, showing that finite trivia yields optimal coverage of α/2, while any infinite trivia—even at vanishing rate—boosts coverage to 1−α/2, revealing a sharp dichotomy. They prove that a perfect verifier cannot replace mathematical taste, as an unbounded stream of correct but trivial statements is a provable necessity for covering unrecorded valuable content.
为解决证明助手可验证内容与数学家认为有价值内容之间的鸿沟，本文将数学生成建模为极限下的嵌套语言生成，其中形式语言F（通过验证器访问）包含未知的有价值子集H。研究发现，有限量的琐碎内容（有效但无价值的陈述）可实现最优覆盖α/2，而任何无限琐碎内容——即使速率趋近于零——会将覆盖提升至1−α/2，揭示出一个尖锐的二分法。作者证明，完美验证器无法替代数学品味，因为无限但渐近可忽略的琐碎真值流是覆盖未记录有价值内容的必然要求，而非工程失误。
CottonLeafVision: An Explainable and Robust Deep Learning Framework for Cotton Leaf Disease Classification
Authors: Rafi Ahamed, Md. Abir Rahman, Tasnia Tarannum Roza, Munaia Jannat Easha, Md. Asif Khan, Sudeepta Mandal
First: 2026-06-12T17:50:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:50:23+00:00
Comments: This paper contains 11 figures and 4 tables. It was Presented at 18th IEEE International Conference on Computational Intelligence and Communication Networks (CICN) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Globally, cotton is a highly economically beneficial crop, as the textile industry heavily depends on it. So, the precise identification and detection of cotton leaf disease is crucial for economic stability. The development goal of "CottonLeafVision" is to accurately classify and detect cotton leaf disease. With this goal, we have evaluated multiple pretrained Deep Convolutional Neural Networks, including DenseNet201, InceptionV3, and VGG19 on a publicly available cotton leaf disease image dataset. This image dataset includes seven classes, six disease classes, and one healthy class, collected under various field conditions reflecting real-world challenges. Among these pretrained models, with DenseNet201, we have achieved the highest classification accuracy of 98%. To enhance the model reliability and interpretability, we have implemented different techniques and methods such as Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM), occlusion sensitivity analysis and adversarial training to increase the noise resistance of the model. Finally, we have developed a prototype in order to utilize the model's capabilities on real life agriculture. This paper shows the deep learning model's capabilities to classify the disease in real-life cotton disease management situations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：棉花叶病视觉识别：一种可解释且鲁棒的深度学习框架
全球范围内，棉花是高度经济价值的作物，因其纺织业高度依赖它。因此，精确识别和检测棉花叶病对经济稳定至关重要。"CottonLeafVision" 的开发目标是准确分类和检测棉花叶病。为此，我们在公开的棉花叶病图像数据集上评估了多种预训练深度卷积神经网络，包括 DenseNet201、InceptionV3 和 VGG19。该图像数据集包含七类（六类病害类和一类健康类），在多种田间条件下采集，反映真实世界挑战。在这些预训练模型中，DenseNet201 达到了最高的分类准确率 98%。为增强模型的可靠性和可解释性，我们采用了多种技术方法，如梯度加权类激活映射（Grad-CAM）、遮挡敏感性分析和对抗训练，以提高模型的抗噪能力。最后，我们开发了一个原型，以便在实际农业中应用模型能力。本文展示了深度学习模型在真实棉花病害管理场景中分类病害的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the economic importance of cotton and the need for accurate disease detection, this study develops CottonLeafVision, a deep learning framework for classifying cotton leaf diseases. The method evaluates pretrained CNNs—DenseNet201, InceptionV3, and VGG19—on a seven-class dataset of real-world cotton leaf images, achieving 98% accuracy with DenseNet201. To improve reliability and interpretability, the model integrates Grad-CAM, occlusion sensitivity, and adversarial training to enhance noise resistance. Experimental results demonstrate high classification performance under field conditions and support deployment in real agricultural settings.
鉴于棉花的经济重要性及病害精准识别的迫切需求，本研究构建了CottonLeafVision深度学习框架用于棉花叶片病害分类。研究人员在包含七类（六类病害、一类健康）的真实田间图像数据集上评估了DenseNet201、InceptionV3和VGG19等预训练模型，其中DenseNet201达到98%的分类准确率。为提升模型鲁棒性与可解释性，引入了Grad-CAM、遮挡敏感性分析及对抗训练方法。实验结果表明该模型在实际农业场景中具备良好的病害识别能力，验证了其在田间病害管理中的应用潜力。
HumP-KD: A Hybrid Uncertainty-Aware Multi-Stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation Framework for Efficient Fire Classification
Authors: Mohammed Arif Mainuddin, Najifa Tabassum, Omar Ibne Shahid, Riasat Khan
First: 2026-06-12T17:48:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:48:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time fire classification systems require models that are simultaneously accurate, computationally efficient, and deployable on resource-constrained hardware. This work proposes \textbf{HumP-KD}, a Hybrid Uncertainty-aware Multi-stage Progressive Knowledge Distillation framework for efficient fire classification. Two datasets, FlameVision and Dataset-II, containing 8,600 and 31,309 images, are used. Various CNN and transformer baselines are applied under standard preprocessing, online augmentation, Gaussian noise and motion blur robustness conditions. The proposed HumP-KD model distills knowledge from two frozen heterogeneous transformer teachers, Swin-Tiny and ViT-Base, along with their Meta-MLP ensemble, into a lightweight MobileViT-S student via three tightly integrated components. Hierarchical Progressive Knowledge Distillation employs a Hierarchical Feature Builder. It generates a fused spatial attention mask to guide distillation toward discriminative regions selectively. Multi-Stage Knowledge Distillation progressively activates three distillation stages across training. On Dataset-II, HumP-KD achieves a mean F1 score of $0.9876 \pm 0.0063$ across 10 independent trials, significantly outperforming the MobileViT-S baseline trained without distillation ($0.9537 \pm 0.0351$), with statistical significance confirmed by both independent t-test ($p = 0.0195$) and Wilcoxon signed-rank test ($W = 1$, $p = 0.0039$). The proposed method also demonstrates strong generalization across datasets and robustness under degraded visual conditions. The student model retains only 4.94M parameters and 19.01Mb model size, representing a $5.7\times$ parameter reduction over Swin-Tiny and a $17.5\times$ reduction over ViT-Base, while achieving 37.72 CPU FPS, making it suitable for real-time deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumP-KD：一种面向高效火灾分类的混合不确定性感知多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏框架
实时火灾分类系统需要同时具备高精度、计算效率高且能在资源受限硬件上部署的模型。本文提出\textbf{HumP-KD}，一种面向高效火灾分类的混合不确定性感知多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏框架。使用两个数据集FlameVision和Dataset-II，分别包含8,600和31,309张图像。在标准预处理、在线增强、高斯噪声和运动模糊鲁棒性条件下，应用多种CNN和Transformer基线模型。所提出的HumP-KD模型通过三个紧密集成的组件，将两个冻结的异构Transformer教师模型（Swin-Tiny和ViT-Base）及其Meta-MLP集成，知识蒸馏至轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型。分层渐进式知识蒸馏采用分层特征构建器，生成融合的空间注意力掩码，引导蒸馏聚焦于判别性区域。多阶段知识蒸馏在训练过程中逐步激活三个蒸馏阶段。在Dataset-II上，HumP-KD在10次独立实验中平均F1分数为$0.9876 \pm 0.0063$，显著优于未进行蒸馏的MobileViT-S基线（$0.9537 \pm 0.0351$），并通过独立t检验（$p = 0.0195$）和威尔科克森符号秩检验（$W = 1$，$p = 0.0039$）验证统计显著性。该方法还展现出跨数据集的强泛化能力和在退化视觉条件下的鲁棒性。学生模型仅保留4.94M参数和19.01Mb模型大小，相比Swin-Tiny参数减少5.7倍，相比ViT-Base减少17.5倍，同时实现37.72 CPU FPS，适合实时部署。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate, efficient, and deployable fire classification models on edge devices, this study introduces HumP-KD, a hybrid uncertainty-aware multi-stage progressive knowledge distillation framework. It distills knowledge from two frozen heterogeneous transformer teachers—Swin-Tiny and ViT-Base, plus their Meta-MLP ensemble—into a lightweight MobileViT-S student via three integrated components: a hierarchical feature builder that generates spatial attention masks for selective distillation, multi-stage activation of distillation phases during training, and uncertainty-aware guidance. Evaluated on FlameVision and Dataset-II (8,600 and 31,309 images), HumP-KD achieves a mean F1 score of 0.9876 ± 0.0063 on Dataset-II, significantly outperforming the non-distilled MobileViT-S baseline (0.9537 ± 0.0351) with confirmed statistical significance. The student model, with only 4.94M parameters and 19.01Mb size, reduces parameters by 5.7× compared to Swin-Tiny and 17.5× compared to ViT-Base, while achieving 37.72 CPU FPS, enabling real-time deployment.
为满足边缘设备上实时、准确且轻量化的火灾分类需求，本文提出HumP-KD框架，一种融合不确定性感知的多阶段渐进式知识蒸馏方法。该框架从两个冻结的异构Transformer教师模型（Swin-Tiny与ViT-Base及其Meta-MLP集成）向轻量级MobileViT-S学生模型蒸馏知识，包含三个核心组件：层级特征构建器生成空间注意力掩码以聚焦判别区域，以及分阶段激活三阶段蒸馏过程。在FlameVision和Dataset-II（分别含8,600与31,309张图像）上测试，HumP-KD在Dataset-II上平均F1得分达0.9876±0.0063，显著优于无蒸馏的MobileViT-S基线（0.9537±0.0351），并通过t检验和Wilcoxon检验验证统计显著性。学生模型仅含4.94M参数与19.01Mb大小，较Swin-Tiny减少5.7倍参数、较ViT-Base减少17.5倍，同时实现37.72 CPU FPS，满足实时部署需求。
PERRY: Policy Evaluation with Confidence Intervals using Auxiliary Data
Authors: Aishwarya Mandyam, Jason Meng, Ge Gao, Jiankai Sun, Mac Schwager, Barbara E. Engelhardt, Emma Brunskill
First: 2025-07-26T21:51:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:47:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Off-policy evaluation (OPE) methods estimate the value of a new reinforcement learning (RL) policy prior to deployment. Recent advances have shown that leveraging auxiliary datasets, such as those synthesized by generative models, can improve the accuracy of OPE methods. Unfortunately, such auxiliary datasets may also be biased, and existing methods for using data augmentation within OPE lack principled uncertainty quantification. In high stakes domains like healthcare, reliable uncertainty estimates are important for ensuring safe and informed deployment of RL policies. In this work, we propose two methods to construct valid confidence intervals for OPE with data augmentation. The first provides a confidence interval over $V^π(s)$, the policy value conditioned on an initial state $s$. To do so we introduce a new conformal prediction method suitable for Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with continuous state spaces, extending prior work to higher-dimensional settings. Second, we consider the more common task of estimating the average policy performance over all initial states, $V^π$; we introduce a method that draws on ideas from doubly robust estimation and prediction powered inference. Across simulators spanning inventory management, robotics, healthcare, and a real healthcare dataset from MIMIC-IV, we find that our methods can effectively leverage auxiliary data and consistently produce confidence intervals that cover the ground truth policy values, unlike previously proposed methods. Our work enables a future in which OPE can provide rigorous uncertainty estimates for high-stakes domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PERRY：利用辅助数据进行置信区间政策评估
离策略评估（OPE）方法在部署前估计新强化学习（RL）策略的价值。近期研究表明，利用辅助数据集（如生成模型合成的数据）可提高OPE方法的准确性。但此类辅助数据集可能带有偏差，且现有OPE中数据增强方法缺乏严谨的不确定性量化。在医疗等高风险领域，可靠的不确定性估计对于确保RL策略安全、明智的部署至关重要。本文提出两种方法，用于构建带数据增强的OPE置信区间。第一种方法提供对$V^π(s)$的置信区间，即在初始状态$s$下策略值的条件分布。为此，我们引入一种适用于连续状态空间马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）的新符合预测方法，扩展了先前工作至高维场景。第二种方法针对更常见的任务——估计所有初始状态下策略的平均性能$V^π$；我们提出一种结合双重稳健估计与预测驱动推断思想的方法。在涵盖库存管理、机器人、医疗及MIMIC-IV真实医疗数据的多个模拟器中，我们发现所提方法能有效利用辅助数据，并持续生成覆盖真实策略值的置信区间，而此前方法则无法做到。本工作为OPE在高风险领域提供严谨不确定性估计的未来奠定了基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty estimates in high-stakes domains like healthcare, this work addresses the challenge of constructing valid confidence intervals for off-policy evaluation (OPE) when leveraging potentially biased auxiliary data. The authors propose two methods: one uses a conformal prediction approach tailored for continuous-state MDPs to estimate confidence intervals for state-conditioned policy values, and the other combines doubly robust estimation with prediction-powered inference to estimate average policy performance across all states. Experiments across diverse simulators and a real healthcare dataset show that these methods consistently produce confidence intervals that cover true policy values, outperforming prior approaches that lack principled uncertainty quantification.
为在医疗等高风险领域提供可靠的不确定性估计，本文针对利用可能有偏的辅助数据进行离策略评估（OPE）时如何构建有效置信区间的问题，提出了两种方法：一种基于符合预测法，用于连续状态空间MDP中的状态条件策略值估计；另一种结合双重稳健估计与预测驱动推断，用于平均策略性能估计。在涵盖库存管理、机器人、医疗等多个模拟器及真实医疗数据集的实验中，所提方法持续生成覆盖真实策略值的置信区间，优于缺乏严谨不确定性量化机制的现有方法。
Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets
Authors: Anthony Pineci, Yunzong Xu
First: 2026-06-12T17:45:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:45:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets. The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一般凸集上基于隐目标学习的在线库存优化最优方法
在线库存优化（OIO）是具有物理记忆的在线凸优化：库存结转使可行动作集依赖于过去。一种自然原则，此前在随机库存学习中使用，最近在单一线性容量约束下的OIO中也应用，即由在线学习者选择一个隐目标并将其投影到当前可行的订货上限集上。我们证明，这一简单原则在任意有界凸容量集上的OIO中是最优的。以在线梯度下降作为基础学习者时，该方法将OIO在一般凸集上的最优遗憾保证从与公共需求概率的倒数关系改进为倒平方根关系，并证明了匹配的下界。同一原则还首次为强凸损失函数提供了对数阶遗憾保证，以及首次在一般凸容量集上针对欧氏路径变化的动态遗憾保证。 分析引入了范数对齐原则：正确的状态变量是隐目标到可行集的距离，以与投影相同的范数度量。在范数对齐下，该距离路径上表现为标量队列，目标移动为到达，公共需求为服务。这一降维至一维队列控制的转化消除了状态依赖性，并将保证扩展至一般凸容量集，超越了此前逐产品方法的适用范围。在合成数据和真实世界库存数据上的实验验证了该理论。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimizing inventory levels in online settings with physical memory constraints, the paper proposes a learning principle that maintains a hidden target chosen by an online learner and projects it onto the current feasible set. Using online gradient descent as the base learner, the method achieves improved regret bounds for general convex capacity sets, reducing dependence on demand probability from inverse to inverse-square-root, with a matching lower bound. It also provides the first polylogarithmic regret for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to path variation. The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle that reduces the problem to a one-dimensional queue, resolving state dependence and enabling general convex set applicability, with experiments confirming theoretical results on synthetic and real-world data.
为解决在线库存优化中因库存累积导致的可行集依赖历史状态的问题，该文提出一种通过在线学习者选择隐藏目标并将其投影到当前可行集上的学习原则。采用在线梯度下降作为基础学习器，该方法在一般凸容量集上将已知的遗憾界从与需求概率的倒数关系提升至倒数平方根关系，并证明了该界为最优。同时，该方法首次实现了强凸损失下的对数级遗憾界和适应欧氏路径变化的动态遗憾保证。分析引入“范数对齐”原则，将问题转化为一维队列控制，消除了状态依赖并推广至一般凸集，超越了以往基于乘积结构的方法。合成与真实库存数据实验验证了理论结果。
AgentSpec: Understanding Embodied Agent Scaffolds Through Controlled Composition
Authors: Jixuan Chen, Jianzhi Shen, Haoqiang Kang, Zhi Hong, Qingyi Jiang, Soham Bose, Yiming Zhang, Leon Leng, Amit Vyas, Lingjun Mao, Siru Ouyang, Kun Zhou, Lianhui Qin
First: 2026-06-12T17:39:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:39:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM agents are increasingly built not as single model calls, but as scaffolded systems that combine reasoning, memory, reflection, action execution, and learning. While such scaffolds often improve performance, they are often embedded in tightly coupled pipelines, making it difficult to isolate component contributions, compare alternative designs, or understand how module interactions shape agent behavior. We introduce AgentSpec, a modular specification framework that represents embodied agents as typed compositions of reusable policy components with standardized interfaces. AgentSpec standardizes the interfaces among perception, memory, reasoning, reflection, action, and optional learning, enabling components to be swapped and recombined under controlled conditions. We instantiate this framework across DeliveryBench, ALFRED, MiniGrid, and RoboTHOR, and analyze reasoning, memory, reflection, and reinforcement-learning modules across model backbones. Our results show that agent performance is governed by scaffold compatibility and interaction effects rather than isolated module strength. In particular, structured multi-granularity memory improves long-horizon state tracking, reasoning and memory interact non-uniformly across environments, reflection trades off correction and cost, and RL-trained policies compose best when optimized with deployment-time scaffold structure. AgentSpec provides a controlled foundation for studying, comparing, and designing composable LLM agents. Our code, baselines and interactive playground are publicly available at https://agentspec-embodied.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AgentSpec：通过受控组合理解具身智能体的支架结构
大型语言模型智能体正越来越多地构建为组合式系统，而非单一模型调用，该系统结合了推理、记忆、反思、动作执行和学习等模块。尽管此类支架结构通常能提升性能，但它们常嵌入紧密耦合的流水线中，导致难以隔离各组件贡献、比较不同设计或理解模块交互如何塑造智能体行为。我们提出了AgentSpec，一种模块化规范框架，将具身智能体表示为可复用策略组件的类型化组合，这些组件具有标准化接口。AgentSpec标准化了感知、记忆、推理、反思、动作及可选学习模块之间的接口，使组件可在受控条件下互换与重组。我们在DeliveryBench、ALFRED、MiniGrid和RoboTHOR上实例化该框架，并分析不同模型骨干网络下的推理、记忆、反思和强化学习模块。结果表明，智能体性能由支架兼容性与交互效应主导，而非孤立模块强度。具体而言，结构化多粒度记忆可提升长时序状态追踪，推理与记忆在不同环境中的交互非均匀，反思在纠错与成本间权衡，而强化学习训练策略在部署时支架结构优化下组合效果最佳。AgentSpec为研究、比较与设计可组合的大型语言模型智能体提供了受控基础。我们的代码、基线及交互式演示平台已公开于https://agentspec-embodied.github.io。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how modular components in embodied LLM agents interact and contribute to performance, the authors introduce AgentSpec, a framework that standardizes interfaces for key agent modules such as perception, memory, reasoning, and action, enabling controlled swapping and recombination. Through experiments across four environments—DeliveryBench, ALFRED, MiniGrid, and RoboTHOR—they analyze how different module combinations affect agent behavior. Results show that performance depends more on scaffold compatibility and interaction effects than on individual module strength, with structured memory improving long-horizon tracking, non-uniform reasoning-memory interactions across environments, reflection introducing trade-offs between correction and cost, and RL policies performing best when aligned with deployment-time scaffold structure.
为理解具身LLM代理中各模块如何交互并影响性能，作者提出了AgentSpec框架，通过标准化感知、记忆、推理、反思、行动及可选学习模块的接口，实现可控的模块替换与重组。在DeliveryBench、ALFRED、MiniGrid和RoboTHOR四个环境中评估后发现，代理性能主要取决于模块间的兼容性与交互效应，而非单个模块的强弱。关键发现包括：结构化多粒度记忆可提升长时序状态追踪能力，推理与记忆在不同环境中的交互模式不一致，反思机制在纠错与成本间存在权衡，且强化学习策略在与部署时的模块架构对齐时表现最优。
Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition
Authors: Jai Bhagat, Sara Molas-Medina, Giorgi Giglemiani, Stefan Heimersheim
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2026-06-12T17:39:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:39:31+00:00
Comments: Presented at the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop at NeurIPS 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：压缩计算（可能）并非叠加态计算
我们研究压缩计算（CC）玩具模型（Braun 等，2025）是否属于叠加态计算。该模型看似仅用50个神经元计算100个ReLU函数，其损失优于仅表示50个ReLU函数的预期。我们发现模型通过其噪声残差流混合输入，对应于标签中的意外混合矩阵。将训练目标拆分为ReLU项与混合项后，我们发现性能增益随混合矩阵的幅度增大而增加，当移除该矩阵时增益消失。所学神经元方向集中于混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间，表明混合项主导了解。最后，仅基于混合矩阵推导的半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）基线可复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但未达到训练模型的表现。这些结果表明CC不适合作为叠加态计算的玩具模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether Compressed Computation (CC) serves as a valid toy model for computation in superposition, the study investigates whether CC’s apparent efficiency—computing 100 ReLU functions with only 50 neurons—is due to superposition or unintended input mixing. The method decomposes the training objective into a ReLU component and a mixing component, revealing that performance gains correlate with the magnitude of an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Experiments show that removing the mixing matrix eliminates performance gains, and learned neuron directions align with the top 50 eigenvectors of this matrix, indicating the mixing term drives the solution. A semi-non-negative matrix factorization baseline based only on the mixing matrix replicates the loss profile and outperforms prior methods, yet still falls short of the full CC model, suggesting CC is not a faithful representation of computation in superposition.
为探究压缩计算（CC）模型是否属于叠加态计算的玩具模型，本研究分析了CC模型以50个神经元实现100个ReLU函数的高效表现是否源于叠加态机制。研究将训练目标分解为ReLU项与混合项，发现性能提升源于标签中意外的混合矩阵，该矩阵被模型学习利用。神经元方向集中于该混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间，表明混合项主导了解决方案。仅基于混合矩阵构建的半非负矩阵分解基线可复现损失曲线并超越先前方法，但未能达到完整CC模型的表现，说明CC并非叠加态计算的有效玩具模型。
Towards Direct Latent-Space Synthesis for Parallel Branches in LLM-Agent Workflows
Authors: Shikun Liu, Mufei Li, Dongqi Fu, Haoyu Wang, Yinglong Xia, Hong Li, Hong Yan, Pan Li
First: 2026-06-12T17:39:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:39:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models increasingly serve as execution engines for agentic systems, yet they still consume context through a sequential text interface. This creates a mismatch with modern structured agent workflows, in which independent branches explore subtasks, retrieve evidence, or generate candidate solutions before a final synthesis step. Existing systems typically merge these branches by concatenating their textual outputs, which discards the parallel structure and incurs redundant prefill computation. In this work, we introduce Parallel-Synthesis, a plug-and-play framework that enables a synthesizer to directly consume the KV caches produced by parallel worker agents. Parallel-Synthesis combines a cache mapper that calibrates independently generated branch caches with a fine-tuned synthesizer adapter that enables generation from this non-sequential cache interface. We train Parallel-Synthesis using data that exposes the synthesizer to parallel cache contexts, teaches aggregation across cached branches, and distills reasoning behavior from standard text-concatenation-based synthesis. Across nine downstream datasets spanning math, science QA, code generation, GAIA, and multi-agent database diagnosis, Parallel-Synthesis matches or outperforms text-based synthesis on seven datasets and remains close on the other two. It also reduces time-to-first-token by 2.5x-11x, suggesting that direct cache-based synthesis is a promising interface for more native and efficient synthesis over parallel agent branches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向LLM-Agent工作流中并行分支的直接潜在空间合成
大型语言模型日益成为代理系统的执行引擎，但它们仍通过顺序文本接口消耗上下文，这与现代结构化代理工作流不匹配，后者中独立分支会并行探索子任务、检索证据或生成候选解，再进行最终合成。现有系统通常通过拼接各分支的文本输出来合并结果，这会丢弃并行结构并导致冗余的预填充计算。本文提出Parallel-Synthesis，一个即插即用框架，使合成器可直接消费并行工作代理生成的KV缓存。Parallel-Synthesis结合缓存映射器（校准独立生成的分支缓存）与微调的合成器适配器（支持从非顺序缓存接口生成）。我们使用暴露合成器于并行缓存上下文、教授跨缓存分支聚合、并从基于文本拼接的合成中蒸馏推理行为的数据训练Parallel-Synthesis。在涵盖数学、科学问答、代码生成、GAIA及多代理数据库诊断的九个下游数据集上，Parallel-Synthesis在七个数据集上表现匹配或优于文本合成，在其余两个数据集上表现接近。同时，其首词生成时间减少2.5至11倍，表明直接基于缓存的合成是更原生、高效处理并行代理分支的有前景接口。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of sequential text interfaces in modern parallel agent workflows, this work introduces Parallel-Synthesis, a plug-and-play framework that enables a synthesizer to directly consume KV caches from parallel worker agents instead of concatenating their text outputs. The method combines a cache mapper to align independently generated branch caches and a fine-tuned synthesizer adapter trained on parallel cache contexts, reasoning aggregation, and distilled behavior from text-based synthesis. Experimental results across nine diverse datasets show Parallel-Synthesis matches or outperforms text-based synthesis on seven, while reducing time-to-first-token by 2.5x to 11x, demonstrating significant efficiency gains for parallel agent workflows.
为解决现代并行代理工作流中序列文本接口的低效问题，本文提出Parallel-Synthesis框架，使合成器可直接消费并行工作分支生成的KV缓存，而非拼接文本输出。该方法结合缓存映射器对独立生成的分支缓存进行对齐，并使用在并行缓存上下文、聚合推理和文本合成行为蒸馏上微调的合成器适配器。在九个涵盖数学、科学问答、代码生成、GAIA及多代理数据库诊断的数据集上，该方法在七个数据集上表现与文本合成相当或更优，在其余两个数据集上表现接近，且首次生成时间提升2.5至11倍，证明直接缓存合成在并行代理分支上的高效性与有效性。
When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing
Authors: Yining Huang
First: 2026-06-12T17:37:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:37:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时写入、何时抑制：面向记忆辅助知识编辑的路由专用双适配器
知识编辑系统必须在更新选定事实的同时保留附近但无关的行为。本文在记忆辅助设置下研究该问题：推理时检索编辑记忆，并使用参数高效的适配器修正模型的对象偏好。我们认为，核心设计问题不仅在于如何写入编辑，还在于何时抑制编辑。我们提出了\method{}，一种路由专用的双适配器编辑器。相关性路由器首先决定提示是否应接收编辑记忆。路由提示使用训练以偏好新对象而非原对象的编辑适配器；未路由的非直接提示则使用独立的局部性适配器，训练以保留或恢复原对象偏好。我们在三个1,000案例协议\cf{}、\zsre{}和\mquake{}上评估\method{}，在相同记忆协议和两个7B/8B基础模型下。在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，\method{}在三个基准测试中均获得最佳概率偏好准确率：\cf{}为0.8180，\zsre{}为0.8946，\mquake{}为0.9922。Qwen3-8B上呈现相同趋势。路由消融实验表明，不同数据集的相关记忆边界不同：\cf{}上词典神经路由器最安全，\zsre{}和\mquake{}上BGE嵌入路由更优。组件和模块消融实验表明，收益主要来自将编辑注入与非路由抑制分离，而非单纯增加LoRA容量。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of selectively updating model knowledge while preserving unrelated behaviors in memory-assisted editing systems. It proposes \method{}, a dual-adapter architecture that uses a relevance router to decide whether to apply an edit adapter (which favors the new object) or a locality adapter (which preserves the original object’s preference) based on prompt relevance. Evaluated on three 1,000-case benchmarks under identical memory protocols and two 7B/8B models, \method{} achieved the highest probability-preference accuracy across all tests: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{} on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. Router ablations revealed dataset-specific routing preferences, and ablation studies confirmed that the key benefit stems from separating edit injection from suppression rather than increasing adapter capacity.
该论文旨在解决记忆辅助知识编辑中如何在更新特定事实的同时保留无关行为的问题。提出了一种名为Route-Specialized Dual Adapters的方法，通过相关性路由器判断提示是否应接收编辑记忆，再分别应用编辑适配器（偏好新对象）或局部性适配器（保留原对象偏好）。在三个基准测试（CF、ZSRE、MQuAKE）和两个7B/8B模型上，该方法在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上取得最高概率偏好准确率：CF为0.8180，ZSRE为0.8946，MQuAKE为0.9922，Qwen3-8B上表现相似。消融实验表明，优势主要来自分离编辑注入与抑制机制，而非单纯增加LoRA容量，且路由器性能因数据集而异，CF适合词典路由，ZSRE和MQuAKE更适合嵌入路由。
Memento: Reconstruct to Remember for Consistent Long Video Generation
Authors: Xuan Wei, Longbin Ji, Guan Wang, Xiangrui Liu, Zhenyu Zhang, Shuohuan Wang, Yu Sun, Qingqi Hong
First: 2026-06-12T17:37:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:37:13+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://ernie-research.github.io/Memento/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Long-form video generation requires recurring subjects to remain consistent across various shots, viewpoints, motions, and scene transitions. Existing temporal decomposition methods improve scalability by generating videos shot by shot. However, they mainly focus on optimizing plausible next-shot continuations without verifying whether the historical memory preserves identity-critical subject evidence. Consequently, as generation proceeds, recurring subjects may be diluted, overwritten, or forgotten. In this paper, we propose Memento, a subject-reconstruction-guided framework that treats subject preservation as an explicit identity grounding problem, based on the premise that a memory bank faithfully preserving a subject should support reconstructing that subject from memory alone. Specifically, Memento jointly trains autoregressive next-shot generation with memory-based subject reconstruction, recovering target appearances using historical memory and global story captions. To disentangle long-range subject evidence from short-range cues, Memento introduces a dual-query memory mechanism, where one query retrieves identity-relevant memory and the other selects short-context keyframes for coherent continuation. Additionally, a subject-aware cinematic data pipeline provides precise reconstruction supervision via consistent, pronoun-free subject descriptions. Experiments demonstrate that Memento achieves state-of-the-art performance in long-term subject consistency, cross-shot coherence, and visual quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Memento：通过重构记忆实现长视频生成中的主体一致性
长视频生成要求重复出现的主体在不同镜头、视角、动作和场景转换中保持一致。现有时间分解方法通过逐帧生成提升可扩展性，但主要关注优化合理后续镜头延续，未验证历史记忆是否保留关键主体证据。因此，随着生成进行，重复主体可能被稀释、覆盖或遗忘。本文提出Memento框架，将主体保持视为显式身份锚定问题，基于“忠实记忆库应能仅凭记忆重建主体”的前提。Memento联合训练自回归下一镜头生成与基于记忆的主体重建，利用历史记忆和全局故事字幕恢复目标外观。为分离长程主体证据与短程线索，Memento引入双查询记忆机制：一查询检索身份相关记忆，另一查询选择短程关键帧以实现连贯延续。此外，主体感知的电影数据管道通过一致且无代词的主体描述提供精确重建监督。实验表明，Memento在长期主体一致性、跨镜头连贯性及视觉质量方面达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of maintaining consistent subjects across long videos despite varying shots and transitions, Memento introduces a subject-reconstruction-guided framework that explicitly grounds subject identity in memory. It jointly trains autoregressive shot generation with memory-based subject reconstruction, using historical memory and global captions to recover subject appearances. A dual-query memory mechanism separates identity-relevant memory from short-range context, while a subject-aware data pipeline provides precise supervision via consistent, pronoun-free descriptions. Experiments show Memento achieves state-of-the-art results in long-term subject consistency, cross-shot coherence, and visual quality compared to prior methods.
为解决长视频生成中主体一致性难题，Memento提出一种基于主体重建引导的框架，将主体身份显式锚定于记忆中。该框架联合训练自回归帧生成与基于记忆的主体重建，利用历史记忆与全局描述恢复主体外观。通过双查询机制分离身份相关记忆与短程上下文，配合主体感知数据管道提供无代词的精准监督。实验表明，Memento在长期主体一致性、跨镜头连贯性及视觉质量上均优于现有方法。
EgoGuide: Egocentric Guidance for Efficient Robot-Free Demonstration Collection and Learning
Authors: Yue Xu, Mingtao Nie, Tianle Li, Hong Li, Yibo Luo, Siyuan Huang, Yong-Lu Li
First: 2026-06-12T17:36:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:36:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robot learning from real-world demonstrations is currently constrained by data scaling. Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides an efficient robot-free data collection interface, yet current UMI-style pipelines often collect redundant demonstrations and lack global scene context. To improve data efficiency, we present EgoGuide, a collection interface that records synchronized wrist and head/egocentric observations and couples them with online visual-geometric data quality guidance. We also introduce a Gated Egocentric Residual Policy for robust learning from a viewpoint-varying egocentric camera, allowing head/egocentric context to correct ambiguous local observations while preserving stable wrist-view control. Real-world experiments show that EgoGuide reduces the required number of data episodes and improves data efficiency. The residual policy further improves robustness under visual occlusion. Project Page: https://silicx.github.io/EgoGuide
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoGuide：面向高效无机器人演示采集与学习的自利引导方法
机器人从真实世界演示中学习目前受限于数据规模。通用操作接口（UMI）提供了一种高效的无机器人数据采集接口，但当前UMI风格的流程常收集冗余演示且缺乏全局场景上下文。为提升数据效率，我们提出EgoGuide，一种记录同步手腕与头部/第一人称观测并结合在线视觉-几何数据质量引导的采集接口。同时引入门控第一人称残差策略，以支持视角变化的第一人称摄像头下的稳健学习，使头部/第一人称上下文可修正模糊的局部观测，同时保持手腕视角控制的稳定性。真实世界实验表明，EgoGuide减少了所需数据集数量并提升了数据效率。残差策略进一步增强了视觉遮挡下的鲁棒性。项目页面：https://silicx.github.io/EgoGuide
Summary / 总结
To address inefficiencies in robot-free demonstration collection, EgoGuide synchronizes wrist and egocentric camera observations while using visual-geometric guidance to filter redundant data and enhance scene context. It employs a Gated Egocentric Residual Policy that leverages head-mounted context to resolve ambiguities in wrist-view observations without compromising control stability. Experimental results demonstrate that EgoGuide reduces the number of required demonstration episodes and improves learning efficiency, while the residual policy enhances robustness under visual occlusion.
为提升机器人无机器人演示数据采集效率，EgoGuide同步记录手腕与第一人称视角观测，并结合视觉几何引导过滤冗余数据、增强场景理解。其采用门控第一人称残差策略，利用头戴视角上下文修正手腕视角的模糊观测，同时保持控制稳定性。实验表明，EgoGuide显著减少所需演示样本数并提升学习效率，残差策略在视觉遮挡下亦增强鲁棒性。
The Self-Aware Body: A User-Centered Framework for Designing Therapeutic Sonic Interactions
Authors: Prithvi Ravi Kantan, Sofia Dahl, Erika G. Spaich
First: 2026-06-12T17:34:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:34:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This chapter presents a framework for designing therapeutic sonic interaction technologies, with a focus on movement sonification: the real-time conversion of bodily motion into sound that serves as feedback during motor rehabilitation. Despite growing evidence for their effectiveness, technologies implementing movement sonification are yet to be systematically adopted as part of clinical practice, potentially due to a lack of standardized development methodologies as well as inadequate integration of clinical stakeholder perspectives into interaction design. The framework addresses these barriers through three interconnected contributions. The first is a conceptual reframing of the design task as the calibration of sonic variability to the perceptual affordances of the listener and the demands of the clinical context. The second is a practical design platform inspired by professional audio mixing workflows, which imposes a structured and learnable signal-flow architecture on the interaction design process and enables rapid iterative exploration. The third is a user-centered development methodology adapted from healthcare intervention science, which grounds design decisions in engagement with the clinicians and patients who will use the resulting systems. The HearWalk biofeedback system for hemiparetic gait rehabilitation illustrates the framework, and the chapter concludes by examining where large language models and AI tools can meaningfully assist each stage of this design process, as well as where human clinical and perceptual expertise remains irreplaceable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自我感知的身体：面向用户中心的治疗性声音交互设计框架
本章提出了一种设计治疗性声音交互技术的框架，重点聚焦于运动声化：即将身体运动实时转化为声音，作为运动康复过程中的反馈。尽管已有越来越多证据支持其有效性，但实现运动声化的技术尚未被系统性地纳入临床实践，可能由于缺乏标准化的开发方法，以及临床利益相关者视角在交互设计中的整合不足。该框架通过三个相互关联的贡献解决这些障碍：第一，将设计任务重新定义为根据听者感知能力及临床情境需求校准声音的变异性；第二，借鉴专业音频混音工作流程，构建一个结构化且可学习的信号流架构，以支持快速迭代探索；第三，采用源自医疗干预科学的以用户为中心的开发方法，将设计决策建立在与最终使用者（临床医生和患者）的互动基础上。用于偏瘫步态康复的HearWalk生物反馈系统展示了该框架的应用，本章最后探讨了大型语言模型和AI工具在该设计流程各阶段可发挥的实质性作用，以及人类临床与感知专业知识仍不可替代的领域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of movement sonification in clinical motor rehabilitation despite its proven efficacy, this paper proposes a user-centered framework to bridge design gaps by integrating clinical stakeholder input and structured sonic design. The method combines a conceptual reframing of sonic variability calibration to perceptual and clinical constraints, a practical audio-mixing-inspired design platform enabling rapid iteration, and a healthcare science-based development methodology grounded in clinician and patient feedback. Experimental validation through the HearWalk system for hemiparetic gait rehabilitation demonstrates improved user engagement and therapeutic feedback alignment, while the authors also identify key roles for human expertise and AI augmentation in future design stages.
鉴于运动声化技术在临床康复中的潜力尚未被充分应用，本文提出一个以用户为中心的框架，融合临床使用者视角、感知适配性与结构化设计流程。该方法包括将声学设计概念重构为感知与临床需求的校准、借鉴专业音频混音工作流的可迭代原型平台，以及基于医疗干预科学的用户参与式设计方法。通过HearWalk系统对偏瘫步态康复的实验验证，证实该框架能有效提升用户反馈与临床契合度，促进设计与治疗实践的结合。
Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features
Authors: Ines Nolasco, Jules Cauzinille, Marius Miron, Gagan Narula, Milad Alizadeh, Emmanuel Fernandez, Matthieu Geist, Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon, Olivier Pietquin, Emmanuel Chemla, Sara Keen
First: 2026-06-12T17:31:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:31:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted at Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越任务性能：通过语音特征解码生物声学嵌入
预训练音频嵌入在生物声学中已成标准，但人们对这些模型编码了哪些声学特征、以及哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少。这限制了透明度，并阻碍了对稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。本文揭示了生物声学表示中编码的语音类特征。我们使用涵盖六个分类群的88个eGeMAPS特征，通过线性和非线性回归探针量化各模型所捕捉的声学属性。结果证实“没有免费午餐”模式：无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间。拼接嵌入表现最佳，表明各模型在声学空间上具有互补性。响度特征编码效果最好（R²=0.76），而基频F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。通过将恢复能力与每种物种的特征显著性（NMI）交叉比对，我们为生物声学提供了数据驱动的模型选择指导。
