Giving AI a Headache: Acoustic Adversarial Attacks to Computer Vision Applications
Authors: Nicole Villavicencio-Garduño, Maksim Ekin Eren, Milo Prisbrey, Ben Migliori, Michael Teti
Venue: Proc. SPIE 14046, Assurance and Security for AI-enabled Systems 2026, 1404609 (10 Jun 2026)
First: 2026-06-12T17:22:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:22:53+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 7 figures, SPIE Defense + Security
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial Intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to automate a variety of real-world computer vision (CV) applications, such as autonomous vehicle control, facial recognition, and security cameras. Recent research has shown that acoustic vibration can induce real physical motion in cameras, interfering with their internal stabilization mechanisms. Because the motion falls outside the conditions the stabilization system was designed to handle, the system introduces artifacts into the frame, causing AI-based CV models to misclassify, miss targets, or hallucinate objects. Previous work used ultrasonic frequencies (>20 kHz) to perform short-range attacks, which limits them to short distances due to the attenuation exhibited by high frequencies. In this work, we investigate acoustic attacks using lower frequencies in the audible range (<20 kHz), and we further expand our analysis to include how various image and object features are affected by the attacks. Specifically, we performed physical experiments to demonstrate the viability of our attacks on an off-the-shelf object detection model (YOLO11) by resonating a commercially available camera with various frequencies. Based on our results, we provide insights into several factors that make an AI CV system more vulnerable to these attacks, which could help inform the development of future mitigation strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：让AI头疼：针对计算机视觉应用的声学对抗攻击
人工智能（AI）正越来越多地用于自动化各种现实世界的计算机视觉（CV）应用，如自动驾驶车辆控制、人脸识别和安防摄像头。近期研究表明，声波振动可引发相机产生真实物理运动，干扰其内部稳定机制。由于该运动超出了稳定系统设计处理的范围，系统会在画面中引入伪影，导致基于AI的CV模型误分类、漏检目标或产生幻觉。 以往研究使用超声频段（>20 kHz）进行短距离攻击，受限于高频衰减，仅适用于短距离。本研究则探讨使用可听频段（<20 kHz）的低频声学攻击，并进一步分析不同图像和物体特征如何受攻击影响。 具体而言，我们通过物理实验验证了对现成目标检测模型（YOLO11）的攻击可行性，通过共振商用相机不同频率实现。基于实验结果，我们揭示了若干使AI CV系统更易受攻击的因素，可为未来防御策略的制定提供参考。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how audible-frequency acoustic vibrations can disrupt computer vision systems by inducing unintended physical motion in camera stabilization mechanisms, leading to image artifacts that cause AI models to misclassify or hallucinate objects. The authors extend prior work that used ultrasonic frequencies to demonstrate that lower, audible frequencies can also be effective over longer distances, and they analyze how different image features influence vulnerability. Through physical experiments using a commercial YOLO11 object detection model, they show that targeted acoustic resonance can reliably degrade performance, identifying factors such as object size, texture, and camera design that affect susceptibility, offering insights for future system hardening.
该论文研究了可听频段声波如何通过诱导相机稳定机构产生非预期物理运动，干扰计算机视觉系统，导致AI模型误分类或幻觉。作者扩展了此前仅使用超声波进行短距离攻击的研究，转而采用更低的可听频率，实现更远距离攻击。通过针对商用YOLO11目标检测模型的物理实验，他们验证了攻击可行性，并识别出如相机结构、频率和目标特征等关键脆弱因素，为未来防御策略提供参考依据。
HPSv3++: Scaling Reward Models Across the Full Spectrum of Diffusion Model Capabilities
Authors: Yijun Liu, Jie Huang, Zeyue Xue, Yuming Li, Ruizhe He, Haoran Li, Shijia Ge, Siming Fu
First: 2026-06-12T17:22:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:22:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reward models guide text-to-image (T2I) systems toward outputs aligned with human preferences. However, typical reward models such as HPSv3 are trained on pre-annotated data from earlier T2I models, without accounting for quality discriminative shifts arising from evolving model capabilities and reinforcement learning (RL) iterations, limiting their broader applicability. In this work, we propose HPSv3++, a reward model framework that elevates the HPSv3 model for varying T2I model capabilities and their RL iteration changes across the full capability-iteration spectrum. Specifically, we first introduce HPDv3++, a 212K dual-dimension preference dataset annotated for text fidelity and aesthetic quality using a recent high-capability (Qwen-Image) model with human supervision. We then propose a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 employs data-aware orthogonal gradient projection to incorporate diverse aesthetic perception from HPDv3++ while preserving the original effective human preference knowledge in HPSv3. Stage 2 further leverages unlabeled data from T2I models spanning different capability levels and RL iterations, and introduces a joint capability-iterations conditioned signal for the reward model together with a standard deviation-driven unsupervised guidance mechanism, strengthening reward model across the capability-iteration spectrum. HPSv3++ achieves state-of-the-art preference prediction, outperforming HPSv3 9.8% on HPDv3, 5.5% on GenAI-Bench, while achieving 79.1%/88.1% on our proposed HPDv3++. When used for T2I RL training, it consistently improves GenEval scores across diverse T2I models, demonstrating its wide-range capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/HPSv3-PlusPlus.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HPSv3++：在扩散模型能力全谱上扩展奖励模型
奖励模型引导文本到图像（T2I）系统生成符合人类偏好的输出。然而，典型的奖励模型（如HPSv3）通常在早期T2I模型的预标注数据上训练，未考虑因模型能力演进和强化学习（RL）迭代带来的质量判别偏移，限制了其广泛应用。本文提出HPSv3++，一种面向不同T2I模型能力及其RL迭代变化的奖励模型框架。具体而言，我们首先引入HPDv3++，一个21.2万条双维度偏好数据集，使用近期高能力模型（Qwen-Image）结合人类监督标注文本保真度与美学质量。随后提出两阶段训练框架：第一阶段采用数据感知正交梯度投影，融合HPDv3++的多样美学感知，同时保留HPSv3原有的有效人类偏好知识；第二阶段进一步利用不同能力层级和RL迭代的T2I模型无标注数据，引入联合能力-迭代条件信号与标准差驱动的无监督引导机制，强化奖励模型在能力-迭代全谱上的表现。HPSv3++在偏好预测上达到最先进水平，在HPDv3上优于HPSv3 9.8%，在GenAI-Bench上提升5.5%，在我们提出的HPDv3++上达到79.1%/88.1%。用于T2I RL训练时，其在多种T2I模型上持续提升GenEval得分，展现广泛适用性。代码见https://github.com/PlantPotatoOnMoon/HPSv3-PlusPlus。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing reward models like HPSv3 that fail to adapt to evolving text-to-image model capabilities and RL iterations, this work introduces HPSv3++, a scalable reward model framework. It leverages a new 212K dual-dimension preference dataset (HPDv3++) annotated with human supervision using a high-capability model, and employs a two-stage training method: first, data-aware orthogonal gradient projection preserves original preference knowledge while incorporating aesthetic diversity; second, it uses unlabeled data across capability levels and RL iterations with a joint capability-iteration conditioned signal and unsupervised guidance to generalize across the spectrum. HPSv3++ achieves superior preference prediction, outperforming HPSv3 by 9.8% on HPDv3 and 5.5% on GenAI-Bench, and scores 79.1%/88.1% on HPDv3++. When applied to T2I RL training, it consistently boosts GenEval scores across multiple models, demonstrating broad applicability.
为解决现有奖励模型（如HPSv3）无法适应扩散模型能力演进与强化学习迭代变化的问题，本文提出HPSv3++框架，旨在覆盖全谱系扩散模型能力。方法首先构建HPDv3++数据集（21.2万条），由高能力模型Qwen-Image与人类标注，涵盖文本保真度与美学质量双维度。训练分两阶段：第一阶段采用数据感知正交梯度投影，在保留HPSv3原始偏好知识的同时融合新美学感知；第二阶段引入跨能力-迭代的联合条件信号与标准差驱动的无监督引导机制，利用多能力扩散模型的未标注数据增强泛化能力。实验表明，HPSv3++在HPDv3上优于HPSv3 9.8%，在GenAI-Bench上提升5.5%，在HPDv3++上达79.1%/88.1%，并在多种扩散模型的强化学习训练中持续提升GenEval评分。
Low-Burden LLM-Based Preference Learning: Personalizing Assistive Robots from Natural Language Feedback for Users with Paralysis
Authors: Keshav Shankar, Dan Ding, Wei Gao
First: 2026-04-01T23:17:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:20:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE RO-MAN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physically Assistive Robots require personalized behaviors to ensure user safety and comfort. However, traditional preference learning methods, like exhaustive pairwise comparisons, cause substantial physical and cognitive fatigue for users with severe motor impairments. To solve this, we propose a low-burden, offline framework that translates unstructured natural language feedback directly into deterministic robotic control policies. To safely bridge the gap between ambiguous human speech and robotic code, our pipeline uses Large Language Models (LLMs) grounded in the Occupational Therapy Practice Framework. This clinical reasoning decodes subjective user reactions into explicit physical and psychological needs, which are then mapped into transparent decision trees. Before deployment, an automated "LLM-as-a-Judge" verifies the code's structural safety. We validated this system in a simulated meal preparation study with 10 adults with paralysis. Results show our natural language approach significantly reduces user workload compared to traditional baselines. Additionally, occupational therapists confirmed the generated policies are safe and accurately reflect user preferences.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低负担的基于大语言模型的偏好学习：从自然语言反馈个性化瘫痪用户辅助机器人
物理辅助机器人需要个性化行为以确保用户安全与舒适。然而，传统偏好学习方法（如穷举成对比较）对严重运动障碍用户造成显著的体力与认知疲劳。为此，我们提出一种低负担的离线框架，可将非结构化自然语言反馈直接转化为确定性机器人控制策略。为安全弥合模糊人类语言与机器人代码之间的鸿沟，我们的流程基于职业治疗实践框架的大语言模型（LLMs），通过临床推理将用户主观反应解码为明确的生理与心理需求，并映射至透明决策树。部署前，自动化“LLM作为裁判”验证代码结构安全性。我们在模拟的餐食准备实验中验证了该系统，涉及10名瘫痪成人。结果表明，我们的自然语言方法显著降低用户工作负荷，相较传统基线方法。此外，职业治疗师确认生成策略安全且准确反映用户偏好。

为降低瘫痪用户在个性化辅助机器人时的身心负担，该研究提出一种低负担离线框架，直接将自然语言反馈转化为确定性机器人控制策略。系统利用基于职业治疗实践框架的大语言模型，将用户的主观反馈解码为明确的生理与心理需求，并映射为透明决策树。部署前通过“大模型裁判”自动验证代码安全性。在对10名瘫痪成人进行的模拟餐食准备实验中，自然语言方法显著降低了用户工作量，且职业治疗师确认生成策略安全且准确反映用户偏好。
Abstracting Cross-Domain Action Sequences into Interpretable Workflows
Authors: Gaurav Verma, Scott Counts
First: 2026-06-12T17:19:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:19:15+00:00
Comments: preprint; 9 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sequential or time-stamped interaction logs provide objective records of digital application usage, yet their granularity and noise often obscure meaningful insights into people's work. Such insights are essential for improving digital products in ways grounded in real-world user interactions. Prior research has applied deep learning models to cluster user actions into high-level activities, but these approaches are highly sensitive to noise and struggle to generalize across applications. To address this limitation, we introduce WorkflowView, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to abstract low-level action sequences into high-level activities. We establish the effectiveness and generality of our approach across three distinct, challenging sequential tasks and diverse domains: (a) zero-shot task description reconstruction from browser logs (achieving high semantic similarity, $μ_{sim} = 0.91$), (b) few-shot student dropout prediction using MOOC interaction logs (reaching weighted $F_1 = 0.90$ with only five few-shot examples), and (c) anonymized, privacy-preserving analysis of AI tool integration within document workflows in Microsoft Word. Our work demonstrates that LLM-based abstraction is a robust and efficient path forward for transforming low-level behavioral data into high-level, interpretable, and actionable insights. We also discuss practical considerations for deploying LLM-based inferences within logging infrastructures, including computational efficiency and user privacy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将跨领域操作序列抽象为可解释的工作流
顺序或时间戳交互日志提供了数字应用使用情况的客观记录，但其粒度和噪声常掩盖人们对工作的有意义洞察。这些洞察对于基于真实用户交互改进数字产品至关重要。先前研究已应用深度学习模型将用户操作聚类为高层活动，但这些方法对噪声高度敏感，且难以跨应用泛化。为解决此局限，我们引入WorkflowView框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）将低层操作序列抽象为高层活动。我们在三个不同且具有挑战性的序列任务及多样领域中验证了方法的有效性和通用性：(a) 从浏览器日志零样本重建任务描述（语义相似度μ_sim = 0.91），(b) 使用MOOC交互日志少量样本预测学生辍学（仅五个样本即达到加权F1 = 0.90），(c) 在Microsoft Word文档工作流中对AI工具集成进行匿名化、隐私保护分析。我们的工作表明，基于LLM的抽象是将低层行为数据转化为高层、可解释且可操作洞察的稳健高效路径。我们还讨论了在日志基础设施中部署基于LLM推断的实用考量，包括计算效率和用户隐私。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract meaningful, interpretable insights from noisy, granular user interaction logs across diverse digital applications, this work introduces WorkflowView, a framework that leverages large language models to abstract low-level action sequences into high-level activities. Evaluated across three challenging tasks—browser log reconstruction, student dropout prediction from MOOC logs, and privacy-preserving analysis of AI tool usage in Word—the method demonstrates strong generalization and robustness. 为从噪声大、粒度细的数字应用交互日志中提取有意义的洞察，本文提出WorkflowView框架，利用大语言模型将低层级操作序列抽象为可解释的高层活动。在浏览器日志重构、MOOC辍学预测及Word中AI工具匿名使用分析三个不同领域中验证，方法展现出强泛化能力与鲁棒性：任务描述重建语义相似度达0.91，仅用五个样本即实现0.90的加权F1分数，且支持隐私保护分析，证实大语言模型可高效将行为数据转化为可操作洞察，并兼顾部署中的效率与隐私考量。
Knowing When to Quit: A Principled Framework for Dynamic Abstention in LLM Reasoning
Authors: Hen Davidov, Nachshon Cohen, Oren Kalinsky, Yaron Fairstein, Guy Kushilevitz, Ram Yazdi, Patrick Rebeschini
Venue: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning, Seoul, South Korea. PMLR 306, 2026. Copyright 2026 by the author(s)
First: 2026-04-20T15:38:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:15:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLMs utilizing chain-of-thought reasoning often waste substantial compute by producing long, incorrect responses. Abstention can mitigate this by withholding outputs unlikely to be correct. While most abstention methods decide to withhold outputs before or after generation, dynamic mid-generation abstention considers early termination of unpromising reasoning traces at each token position. Prior work has explored empirical variants of this idea, but principled guidance for the abstention rule remains lacking. We present a formal analysis of dynamic abstention for LLMs, modeling abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework. An abstention reward parameter controls the trade-off between compute and information. We show that abstaining when the value function falls below this reward strictly outperforms natural baselines under general conditions. We further derive a principled and efficient method to approximate the value function. Empirical results on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks support our theory and demonstrate improved selective accuracy over existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：何时该停止：大语言模型推理中动态弃权的原理框架
使用思维链推理的大语言模型常因生成冗长且错误的响应而浪费大量计算资源。弃权机制可通过不输出不太可能正确的结果来缓解这一问题。尽管多数弃权方法在生成前或生成后决定是否弃权，动态生成中弃权则考虑在每个词位置提前终止无希望的推理路径。此前研究已探索过该思想的实证变体，但缺乏对弃权规则的原理性指导。我们对大语言模型的动态弃权进行了形式化分析，将其建模为正则化强化学习框架中的显式动作。一个弃权奖励参数控制计算与信息之间的权衡。我们证明，在一般条件下，当价值函数低于该奖励时弃权，其表现严格优于自然基线。我们进一步推导出一种原理性且高效的值函数近似方法。在数学推理与毒性规避任务上的实证结果支持我们的理论，并展示了相比现有方法更高的选择性准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of LLMs generating long, incorrect reasoning traces, this work proposes a principled framework for dynamic mid-generation abstention, where outputs are terminated early if reasoning paths become unpromising. The method models abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework, using a reward parameter to balance compute cost against information gain. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks show that abstaining when the value function falls below this threshold consistently outperforms existing baselines in selective accuracy, with an efficient approximation method enabling practical deployment.
为解决大语言模型在链式推理中生成冗长错误响应导致的计算浪费问题，本文提出一种动态中途终止的主动放弃框架，允许模型在生成过程中根据当前推理轨迹的价值函数决定提前终止。该方法将放弃建模为强化学习中的显式动作，并引入放弃奖励参数以权衡计算成本与信息收益。通过推导高效的价值函数近似方法，实验表明当价值函数低于该奖励阈值时放弃，能在数学推理和毒性规避任务上显著优于现有方法，提升选择性准确率。
Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals
Authors: Christoph Bauschmann, Setareh Maghsudi
First: 2026-06-12T17:11:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:11:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

标题：通过可分离信号实现图结构组合半带臂的非线性奖励关联
在庞大互联数据阵列中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源。学习并利用潜在信号依赖关系可显著提升效率和预测能力，但非线性统计关系的普遍存在加剧了此类任务的复杂性。本文提出新颖的通用自适应策略，配备基于图的因果奖励建模、分析再生核方法及函数过程的泰勒近似。我们建立了理论性能保证，其时间复杂度为亚线性，数据量随时间呈线性增长。分析涵盖对噪声干扰、模型渐进收敛及解空间不匹配等多种不确定性的鲁棒性。该框架仅依赖少量条件或先验估计，即可实现广泛适用性，而多种改进方案可应对特定或扩展场景。为验证实际有效性，我们使用经过基准测试的合成数据及真实世界交通数据集进行数值实验。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently identify optimal structures in large interconnected datasets under nonlinear reward dependencies, this paper introduces adaptive strategies combining graph-based causal reward modeling, reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximations to approximate complex reward functions. Theoretical guarantees show sublinear time complexity and linear scalability with data volume, while the framework remains robust to noise, model convergence issues, and solution space mismatches. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world transportation datasets confirm the method’s practical effectiveness in improving sampling efficiency and predictive accuracy without requiring prior estimates or strong assumptions.
为在大规模互联数据中高效识别最优结构并应对非线性奖励依赖，本文提出结合图结构因果奖励建模、再生核方法与泰勒近似的自适应策略。理论分析表明其时间复杂度呈次线性、数据量呈线性增长，且对噪声、模型收敛与解空间偏差具备鲁棒性。在合成与真实交通数据上的实验验证了该框架在最小假设下具备实用有效性。
Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization
Authors: Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores, My Duong, Juan S. Borrero, Kaixun Hua
First: 2026-06-12T17:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:10:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted at UAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：哪些方向重要？仿射鲁棒优化的稀疏设计
鲁棒机器学习与优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择。我们研究当不确定性由有限字典和预算约束定义时，模型必须覆盖哪些不确定性方向。选择子集形成具有闭式支撑函数的原子不确定性集，从而为仿射目标函数生成可处理的鲁棒规划。我们提出一种基于评估方向（如梯度、对抗扰动或保留数据集观察到的偏移）覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则。我们证明该目标是单调且次模的，支持贪心方法并具有(1-1/e)近似保证，同时存在匹配的硬度下界。我们还提供一个证书，用于界定所选子集的损失上界，并提供一个具有样本外控制能力的半径校准规则。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of selecting a sparse subset of uncertainty directions in affine robust optimization, motivated by the need to balance model coverage and computational tractability under budget constraints. The authors propose a data-driven greedy algorithm that selects directions based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions such as gradients or adversarial perturbations, leveraging the monotonicity and submodularity of the objective to guarantee a (1-1/e) approximation. Experiments show that this method yields robust programs with closed-form support functions and provides a certificate bounding the loss, along with a radius calibration rule that maintains out-of-sample performance.
该论文旨在解决在仿射鲁棒优化中如何从有限字典中稀疏选择不确定性方向的问题，以在预算约束下平衡模型覆盖与计算可行性。作者提出一种基于覆盖目标的数据驱动贪心算法，选择梯度或对抗扰动等评估方向，利用该目标的单调性和子模性，保证(1-1/e)近似比。实验表明，该稀疏选择方法在保持与全覆盖相当性能的同时显著降低计算负担，并提供损失界证书及具有样本外控制能力的半径校准规则。
Listening with Attention: Entropy-Guided Explainability for Transformer-Based Audio Models
Authors: Ravi Ranjan, Utkarsh Grover, Xiaomin Lin, Agoritsa Polyzou
First: 2026-06-12T17:08:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:08:42+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 3 figures, and 9 tables. Accepted in Interspeech 2026 conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) models such as Whisper are highly accurate, but their predictions remain difficult to interpret. Existing explainable AI (XAI) methods often lack faithfulness and precise temporal grounding. We propose Listening with Entropy-guided Attention for Faithful explainability (LEAF-X), a model-intrinsic XAI framework for transformer-based ASR. LEAF-X combines entropy-guided attention weighting, multi-layer attention rollout, and optional causal ablations to identify low-entropy, high-impact heads and layers, producing sparse token-to-frame attributions. Unlike perturbation-based explainers or raw attention maps, LEAF-X exploits the internal structure of encoder-decoder and speech-augmented decoder-only models to generate explanations that better reflect model computation. Results show 32% improved faithfulness, 35-39% stronger locality/sparsity, and the most stable attributions, supporting more transparent and auditable ASR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：专注聆听：基于熵引导的注意力可解释性方法用于基于Transformer的音频模型
基于Transformer的自动语音识别（ASR）模型（如Whisper）准确率高，但其预测结果难以解释。现有可解释人工智能（XAI）方法常缺乏忠实性和精确的时间定位。我们提出了一种名为LEAF-X的模型内生XAI框架，用于基于Transformer的ASR模型。LEAF-X结合熵引导注意力加权、多层注意力展开和可选因果消融，识别低熵、高影响的注意力头和层，生成稀疏的令牌到帧归因。与基于扰动的解释器或原始注意力图不同，LEAF-X利用编码器-解码器及语音增强的解码器-only模型的内部结构，生成更贴合模型计算过程的解释。实验结果表明，LEAF-X在忠实性上提升32%，局部性/稀疏性增强35-39%，且归因最稳定，支持更透明、可审计的ASR。
Summary / 总结

This approach generates sparse token-to-frame attributions that reflect internal model computation rather than relying on perturbations or raw attention. Experimental results demonstrate 32% improved faithfulness, 35-39% stronger locality and sparsity, and the most stable attributions compared to existing methods, enabling more transparent and auditable ASR systems.
为了解决基于Transformer的语音识别模型（如Whisper）预测难以解释、现有可解释AI方法缺乏忠实性和时间定位的问题，作者提出了LEAF-X框架，该框架结合熵引导注意力加权、多层注意力展开和可选因果消融，识别低熵高影响的模型组件，生成稀疏的音素到帧归因。与基于扰动或原始注意力图的解释方法不同，LEAF-X利用编码器-解码器及语音增强的解码器模型内部结构生成更贴近模型计算的解释。实验结果表明，其忠实度提升32%，局部性和稀疏性增强35-39%，且归因最稳定，支持更透明可审计的语音识别系统。
Application of Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning in Libraries: A Systematic Review
Authors: Rajesh Kumar Das, Mohammad Sharif Ul Islam
First: 2021-12-06T07:33:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:08:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As the concept and implementation of cutting-edge technologies like artificial intelligence and machine learning has become relevant, academics, researchers and information professionals involve research in this area. The objective of this systematic literature review is to provide a synthesis of empirical studies exploring application of artificial intelligence and machine learning in libraries. To achieve the objectives of the study, a systematic literature review was conducted based on the original guidelines proposed by Kitchenham et al. (2009). Data was collected from Web of Science, Scopus, LISA and LISTA databases. Following the rigorous/ established selection process, a total of thirty-two articles were finally selected, reviewed and analyzed to summarize on the application of AI and ML domain and techniques which are most often used in libraries. Findings show that the current state of the AI and ML research that is relevant with the LIS domain mainly focuses on theoretical works. However, some researchers also emphasized on implementation projects or case studies. This study will provide a panoramic view of AI and ML in libraries for researchers, practitioners and educators for furthering the more technology-oriented approaches, and anticipating future innovation pathways.

Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes
Authors: Simone Di Gregorio, Anupam Gupta, Stefano Leonardi, Matteo Russo
First: 2026-06-12T17:05:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:05:08+00:00
Comments: Accepted at COLT '26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss. Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$. Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带次线性噪声探针的在线凸优化
我们研究在凸集 $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$ 上的在线凸优化（OCO），其中在每轮 $t$，学习者选择 $x_t\in K$，然后观察一个凸损失函数 $f_t:K\to[0,1]$，目标是相对于事后最优固定决策最小化遗憾。我们引入一个统一的探针模型，概括了两个近期研究方向：专家设置中的次线性最优专家查询，以及OCO中每轮均可获得的成对（比较式）反馈。在我们的框架中，学习者拥有 $k\le T$ 个成对探针预算；在探针轮次中，可查询两点并得知哪一点损失更小。我们的主要结果表明，即使探针预算次线性且带噪声，也能在全反馈OCO场景中显著改善最坏情况遗憾。使用 $k$ 个 $δ$-噪声成对探针，我们得到：$ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $，该结果在 $T$、$k$ 和 $δ$ 上（除 $T$ 的对数因子外）均紧致。特别地，对于噪声参数 $δ\in [0,1]$，当 oracle 响应接近抛硬币（即 $δ$ 接近 $\frac{1}{2}$）时，遗憾保证平滑退化。当将相同技术应用于有限 $K$ 的 $d$ 专家预测设置时，所得速率在所有参数（包括 $d$）上完全紧致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve regret bounds in Online Convex Optimization (OCO) under limited and noisy feedback, this paper introduces a unified probing model that generalizes both sublinear expert queries and pairwise comparison feedback. The learner is allowed a budget of k ≤ T noisy pairwise probes, where each probe reveals which of two queried points incurs smaller loss. The main result demonstrates that even with sublinear and noisy probes, regret can be improved over standard OCO: the regret is bounded by O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T / (k |1-2δ|)}), which is tight up to logarithmic factors in T, k, and δ. As noise δ approaches 1/2, the regret degrades smoothly, reflecting the diminishing value of noisy comparisons. When applied to the finite expert setting, the rates become fully tight across all parameters, including the number of experts d.
为在反馈受限条件下提升在线凸优化（OCO）的后悔界，本文提出一个统一的探针模型，涵盖子线性专家查询与每轮成对比较反馈。学习者可进行 k ≤ T 次带噪声的成对探针，每次探针揭示两点中损失较小者。主要结果表明，即使探针带噪声且数量为子线性，仍可改善后悔：Reg_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), (dT ln T)/(k|1-2δ|)}），在 T、k、δ 上至对数因子紧致。当噪声 δ 接近 1/2 时，后悔界平滑下降，反映探针效用降低。应用于有限专家场景时，所有参数（包括 d）的速率完全紧致。
From Self-Supervised Speech Models to Mixture-of-Experts for Robust Anti-Spoofing
Authors: Hugo Daumain, Driss Matrouf, Khaled Khelif, Mickael Rouvier
First: 2026-06-12T17:04:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:04:32+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures, accepted at Odyssey 2026 (The Speaker and Language Recognition Workshop)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in speech generation have significantly improved the naturalness of synthetic speech, making spoofing detection increasingly challenging. A key limitation of current anti-spoofing systems is their limited robustness to unseen synthesis methods. In this work, we transform a self-supervised speech representation model into a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture to improve generalization. Feed-forward blocks in selected encoder layers are replaced by multiple expert networks controlled by a layer-wise gating mechanism, allowing experts to capture complementary acoustic patterns while preserving the representations learned during self-supervised pretraining. We further analyze the architectural choices affecting the performance of this MoE conversion and investigate the activation behavior of the experts. The proposed approach is evaluated on 14 spoofing datasets and reduces the macro EER from 5.46% to 4.81%, corresponding to 11.9% relative improvement over the baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从自监督语音模型到专家混合架构以实现鲁棒的防欺骗
近年来语音生成技术的进展显著提升了合成语音的自然度，使得欺骗检测愈发困难。当前防欺骗系统的主要局限在于对未见过的合成方法缺乏鲁棒性。本文将自监督语音表征模型转化为专家混合（MoE）架构以提升泛化能力：在选定编码器层中，前馈模块被多个专家网络替代，由逐层门控机制控制，使专家能捕捉互补的声学模式，同时保留自监督预训练期间学到的表征。我们进一步分析了影响该MoE转换性能的架构选择，并研究了专家的激活行为。所提方法在14个欺骗数据集上评估，将宏平均EER从5.46%降至4.81%，相较基线提升11.9%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing difficulty in detecting synthetic speech due to improved naturalness, this work adapts a self-supervised speech model into a Mixture-of-Experts architecture to enhance robustness against unseen spoofing methods. The method replaces selected encoder feed-forward blocks with multiple expert networks, governed by a layer-wise gating mechanism, to capture diverse acoustic patterns while preserving pretraining representations. Evaluated across 14 spoofing datasets, the approach achieves a 11.9% relative improvement in performance, reducing the macro EER from 5.46% to 4.81%.
鉴于合成语音日益逼真导致反欺骗检测难度上升，本文将自监督语音模型转化为门控专家混合架构，以提升对未知合成方法的鲁棒性。方法通过层间门控机制替换部分编码器前馈模块为多个专家网络，保留预训练表示的同时捕捉多样声学模式。在14个欺骗数据集上的实验表明，该方法将宏平均EER从5.46%降至4.81%，相对提升11.9%。
Improving Lunar Topography with Deep Learning Schrödinger Bridges
Authors: Matthew Repasky, Erwan Mazarico, Michael K. Barker, Stefano Bertone, Terence J. Sabaka, Yao Xie
Venue: The Planetary Science Journal 7.6 (2026): 139
First: 2026-06-12T17:04:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:04:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Increasing the resolution of planetary topography models can enable a better understanding of surface processes and geomorphology; however, existing analytical super-resolution methods are expensive and difficult to apply at large scales. Generative models provide the tools to learn complex relationships within data and can be applied at scale due to hardware accelerators and parallelization. We present a diffusion-based Schrödinger Bridge (SB) generative modeling approach for lunar topography super-resolution, connecting the distribution of low-resolution topography to that of high-resolution topography, incorporating physically-constraining optical imagery. Our approach is inspired by existing Shape-from-Shading methods, which improve a priori low-resolution topography by using optical images at the target resolution. We train SBs on a novel dataset of rendered lunar topography, emulating optical imagery from the Lunar Reconnaissance Orbiter Narrow Angle Camera. The result is a flexible approach for topography super-resolution which can provide pixel-level uncertainties in the reconstruction.

提高行星地形模型的分辨率有助于更好地理解地表过程和地貌；然而，现有的分析型超分辨率方法成本高昂且难以大规模应用。生成模型能够学习数据中的复杂关系，并借助硬件加速器和并行化实现大规模应用。我们提出了一种基于扩散的薛定谔桥（SB）生成建模方法，用于月球地形超分辨率，将低分辨率地形分布与高分辨率地形分布相连接，并结合物理约束的光学影像。该方法受现有基于阴影的形状恢复方法启发，通过目标分辨率的光学图像改进先验低分辨率地形。我们在一个新颖的渲染月球地形数据集上训练SB模型，模拟月球勘测轨道器窄角相机的光学影像。该方法提供了一种灵活的地形超分辨率方案，并能输出重建中的像素级不确定性。

Motivated by the need to enhance lunar topography resolution for better surface process analysis, this study proposes a diffusion-based Schrödinger Bridge generative model that maps low-resolution topography distributions to high-resolution ones while incorporating physically-constrained optical imagery. 为提升月球地形分辨率以更好地分析地表过程，该研究提出一种基于扩散的Schrödinger桥生成模型，将低分辨率地形分布映射至高分辨率分布，同时融合物理约束的光学影像。该模型在模拟月球勘测轨道器窄角相机影像的新型渲染数据集上训练，利用硬件加速与并行化实现大规模应用。实验结果表明，该方法能生成高保真地形重建，并提供像素级不确定性估计，优于传统分析方法在分辨率与计算效率方面。
Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables
Authors: Rui Wu, Zongyuan Chen, Hong Xie, Defu Lian, Enhong Chen
First: 2026-06-12T17:02:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:02:53+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Journal of Machine Learning Research (JMLR). 50 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于控制函数工具变量的图扩散残差
控制函数工具变量估计量需要第一阶段残差，而不仅仅是第一阶段预测。高容量的第一阶段可能插值处理变量，导致对结果方程残留的信息过少。我们研究了自适应各向异性工具变量热流（A-IHF），这是一种确定性的图扩散残差提取器，适用于灵活的控制函数。A-IHF将处理变量视为第一阶段特征图上的信号，利用试点扩散检测大处理跳跃，衰减这些跳跃间的传导性，并通过稀疏图解析器计算生成的控制变量。其观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性及图可接受性过滤。分析将误差分解为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，从而得出有限样本界、在潜在分段光滑几何下的图可接受性速率及有限路径选择校准。在54个合成基准单元中，经过调优的图、核、树、提升、序列和神经控制函数基线对比下，受保护的观测A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应均方误差；A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。当图捕捉到分段光滑的第一阶段结构时，性能最佳。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate control-function residuals in instrumental variable estimation, this paper introduces Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a graph-diffusion method that extracts residuals by treating treatment as a signal on a feature graph. A-IHF detects large treatment jumps via pilot diffusion, attenuates graph conductance across those jumps, and computes control functions using a sparse graph resolvent. Experimental results across 54 synthetic benchmarks show that A-IHF achieves the lowest average structural-response MSE among all tested methods, outperforming the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells, with strongest gains when the graph structure aligns with piecewise-smooth first-stage geometry.
为解决工具变量估计中控制函数残差提取的精度问题，本文提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF）方法，将处理变量视为特征图上的信号，通过试点扩散检测大跳跃，抑制跳跃区域的图传导，并利用稀疏图解析子计算控制函数。在54个合成基准实验中，A-IHF获得最低的平均结构响应均方误差，且在32个实验中优于最佳非A-IHF基线，尤其在图结构匹配分段光滑第一阶段几何时表现最优。
Planning with the Views via Scene Self-Exploration
Authors: Kangrui Wang, Linjie Li, Zhengyuan Yang, Shiqi Chen, Zihan Wang, Li Fei-Fei, Jiajun Wu, Leonidas Guibas, Lijuan Wang, Manling Li
First: 2026-05-28T08:15:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:01:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Can VLMs predict how each camera move changes the view, and plan many such moves ahead? We call this capability view planning, requiring (1)understanding how a single action transforms the view, and (2)composing many such transformations across multi-turn plans to identify a target view. We probe both abilities in our proposed ViewSuite, a 3D point-cloud environment on real ScanNet scenes. Across 13 frontier VLMs, a critical planning gap emerges: they possess basic view-action knowledge but fail to compose it across multi-turn plans, with the gap widening as viewpoint distance grows. To close this gap, we propose an iterative framework that alternates self-exploration with view graph distillation. The key insight is that all exploration trajectories, regardless of their outcome, collectively form a view graph that compactly captures how viewpoints connect across a scene. Distilling this graph into diverse supervised tasks reshapes the policy distribution and overcomes the sparse rewards that stall pure RL. This improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 2.5% to 47.8% on interactive view planning, surpassing GPT-5.4 Pro (18.5%) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (21.4%). Self-exploration emerges as a promising path toward VLMs that can actively reason and plan in 3D space. Code and Data are at https://viewsuite.github.io.

为使视觉语言模型能够自主规划相机移动以达成目标视角，该研究构建了基于真实ScanNet场景的3D点云环境ViewSuite，用于评估视图规划能力。研究发现，尽管多数模型能理解单次视角变换，但在多步规划中难以组合这些动作，且距离越远表现越差。为此，作者提出结合自探索与视图图蒸馏的迭代框架，利用探索轨迹中所有视角转换构建紧凑的视图图，并将其蒸馏为多样监督任务，重塑模型策略分布并缓解稀疏奖励问题。该方法显著提升性能：Qwen2.5-VL-7B在交互式视图规划任务中准确率提升至47.8%，超越GPT-5.4 Pro（18.5%）和Gemini 3.1 Pro（21.4%）。
Micro-Swarm Locomotion Optimization in Dynamic Flow using Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Josef Berman, Oren Gal
First: 2026-05-24T12:04:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T17:00:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Coordinating micro-robotic swarms in realistic, time-dependent fluid environments remains a major challenge for biomedical and environmental applications. We present a hybrid CFD-MO-MARL (Computational Fluid Dynamics-Multi Objective-Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning) framework that couples a high-fidelity incompressible Navier--Stokes solver with decentralized proximal policy optimization to learn swarm control policies in oscillatory flow. Sixteen magnetically actuated micro-robots were simulated to navigate a pulsatile arterial waveform within a 2 mm channel while jointly optimizing upstream progression, energy efficiency, and motion smoothness. Conflicting objectives are resolved using Projected Conflicting Gradient (PCGrad) surgery. Without PCGrad, energy and smoothness rewards collapse during training, demonstrating that gradient conflict resolution is essential for stable multi-objective learning. The converged policy achieves progress rewards of 6.5-7.0, energy efficiency of 0.63-0.65, and smoothness of 0.97-0.99, outperforming brute-force baselines by more than 8 reward units on the primary objective. Training reveals three emergent behaviors not encoded in the reward function: hydrodynamic throttling formations that reduce peak flow velocities, a cycle-synchronized ratchet mechanism that exploits flow reversals for upstream movement, and individualized final-approach strategies near the target boundary. These results demonstrate that physically realistic fluid--agent interactions can be integrated directly into multi-objective reinforcement learning, providing a scalable framework for micro-swarm control in biomedical navigation, environmental monitoring, and microfluidic systems.

SED:Lightweight Saliency prediction for Event-based data via Distillation
Authors: Romaric Mazna, Jean Martinet, Michele Magno
First: 2026-06-12T16:59:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:59:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Event-based saliency prediction has gained attention recently, as combining event cameras with saliency estimation can act as an upstream stage that naturally improves the efficiency of downstream eventbased perception at the edge. However, current approaches are either neuromorphic, underperforming on event-based saliency benchmarks, or too heavy for resource-constrained edge applications due to their reliance on transformers or 3D convolutions. Drawing inspiration from efficient convolutional modules, SED and aiming to exploit the temporal information in event data, we propose a lightweight network, trained through knowledge distillation, built on a Depthwise Spatio-Temporal Block (DSTconv) -- a factorization of the 3D depthwise separable convolution. Relative to its teacher, our model reduces the model size from 180 MB to 0.32 MB (562x) and the parameter count from 45M to 81k (554x), while matching or outperforming it on the N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports datasets. Moreover, it generalizes strongly beyond its training distribution, transferring from synthetic to real event data where a model trained from scratch fails.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SED：通过蒸馏实现事件数据轻量级显著性预测
事件显著性预测近年来备受关注，因为将事件相机与显著性估计结合可作为上游阶段，自然提升边缘设备上事件感知的效率。然而，现有方法要么是神经形态的，在事件显著性基准测试中表现不佳，要么因依赖Transformer或3D卷积而过于庞大，难以适用于资源受限的边缘应用。受高效卷积模块启发，我们提出轻量级网络SED，旨在利用事件数据中的时序信息，该网络基于深度时空块（DSTconv）——3D深度可分离卷积的分解形式，并通过知识蒸馏训练。相比其教师模型，我们的模型参数量从180MB降至0.32MB（缩小562倍），参数数量从4500万降至8.1万（缩小554倍），同时在N-DHF1K和N-UCF Sports数据集上表现匹配或超越教师模型。此外，该模型在训练分布之外具有强泛化能力，可从合成事件数据迁移到真实事件数据，而从头训练的模型则无法实现。

It employs a Depthwise Spatio-Temporal Block (DSTconv) that factorizes 3D depthwise separable convolutions for efficiency. Compared to its teacher model, SED reduces size by 562x (from 180 MB to 0.32 MB) and parameters by 554x (from 45M to 81k), while matching or exceeding performance on N-DHF1K and N-UCF Sports benchmarks. Additionally, it demonstrates strong generalization from synthetic to real event data, outperforming models trained from scratch in such settings.
为满足边缘设备上事件相机视觉系统对轻量级显著性预测的需求，作者提出SED模型，通过知识蒸馏从大型教师模型中学习，采用深度时空块（DSTconv）高效捕捉事件数据中的时序信息，避免使用Transformer或3D卷积等重型架构。实验表明，SED模型体积缩小562倍（从180MB降至0.32MB），参数量减少554倍（从45M降至81k），在N-DHF1K和N-UCF Sports数据集上表现与教师模型相当或更优，且在从合成数据迁移到真实事件数据时泛化能力显著优于从头训练的模型。
Sub-Token Routing for KV Cache Compression
Authors: Wei Jiang, Wei Wang
First: 2026-04-23T06:47:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:58:45+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 tables, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer inference often requires a large KV cache, especially for long-context language modeling and multimodal generation. Existing compression methods usually reduce cache cost by selecting, evicting, quantizing, or compressing cached tokens, or by reducing the visual-token sequence before language-model inference. We introduce sub-token routing, a KV-compression method that adds a finer control axis inside retained tokens. It splits each retained value vector into groups and keeps only selected groups, while leaving query and key states unchanged. The method is designed to work after token-level reduction. First, a token-reduction method determines which tokens are retained. Then, sub-token routing compresses the value states inside those retained tokens. Experiments under matched KV budgets show that adding sub-token routing improves token-level reduction performance in both LLM and VLM settings, including Quest on LLaMA-2-7B and Qwen2.5-7B, and FastV/VisionZip across LLaVA and Qwen-VL models. The gains are larger at smaller KV budgets, suggesting that value-group routing is especially useful when further token removal becomes costly. Overall, token-level reduction and sub-token routing provide complementary ways to reduce KV cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于子词路由的KV缓存压缩
Transformer推理通常需要较大的KV缓存，尤其是在长上下文语言建模和多模态生成中。现有压缩方法通常通过选择、驱逐、量化或压缩缓存的token，或在语言模型推理前减少视觉token序列来降低缓存开销。我们引入子词路由，一种KV压缩方法，它在保留的token内部增加更精细的控制轴。该方法将每个保留的值向量划分为若干组，仅保留选定的组，而保持查询和键状态不变。该方法设计为在token级减少之后使用。首先，token减少方法决定保留哪些token；然后，子词路由压缩这些保留token内部的值状态。在匹配的KV预算下实验表明，添加子词路由可提升token级减少在LLM和VLM设置中的性能，包括Quest在LLaMA-2-7B和Qwen2.5-7B上的表现，以及FastV/VisionZip在LLaVA和Qwen-VL模型上的表现。在较小的KV预算下收益更大，表明值组路由在进一步删除token变得昂贵时尤其有用。总体而言，token级减少与子词路由提供了互补的降低KV成本方式。
Summary / 总结
To address the high KV cache cost in transformer inference, especially for long-context and multimodal tasks, the paper proposes sub-token routing—a method that compresses value vectors within retained tokens by splitting them into groups and preserving only selected groups, while leaving query and key states untouched. This technique complements token-level reduction methods, which first determine which tokens to keep. Experiments show that sub-token routing significantly boosts performance under matched KV budgets across LLMs (e.g., LLaMA-2-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) and VLMs (e.g., LLaVA, Qwen-VL), with larger gains observed at smaller KV budgets, indicating its value in scenarios where further token pruning is expensive.
为降低Transformer推理中KV缓存的高成本，特别是在长上下文和多模态任务中，该论文提出了一种名为子词路由的压缩方法，该方法在完成词级压缩后对保留词的值向量进行分组并仅保留部分组，同时保持查询和键状态不变。在匹配的KV预算下，该方法在LLM（如LLaMA-2-7B、Qwen2.5-7B）和VLM（如LLaVA、Qwen-VL）中均显著提升词级压缩效果，且在KV预算较小的情况下收益更明显，表明其在进一步词剪枝成本高昂时尤为有效。
When Good Verifiers Go Bad: Self-Improving VLMs Can Regress on New Tasks
Authors: Jianzhe Lin
First: 2026-06-12T16:55:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:55:30+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Verifier-driven self-DPO is a common recipe for self-improving production visual-language models. In this setup, a frozen verifier scores candidate generations, the top- and bottom-scoring candidates form a preference example, and DPO updates the learner. The deployment-time assumption is monotone: a stronger verifier should yield a stronger student. We show that this assumption can fail because verifier quality is highly task-specific. On a four-rung open-source verifier ladder across MathVista, MMMU, and BLINK, the same verifiers that are above-threshold and improve a Qwen-3-VL-2B student on MathVista become sub-threshold on MMMU, where their task-rubric accuracy drops to 8% to 23%. In this regime, every verifier we tested silently regresses the student, producing drops of 3.4 to 10.9 percentage points below the frozen baseline while the DPO training loss continues to decrease. The regression replicates on a second student, Qwen-2.5-VL-3B. Moreover, within the failure regime, damage is confidence-inverted: the more accurate-but-still-wrong verifier causes larger regression than a near-random verifier, suggesting that progress-gated replay amplifies confidently wrong preference pairs. We give a compact mechanistic explanation via a variance theorem for progress-gated replay and its direction-mismatch failure mode. The deployment message is operational rather than purely diagnostic: before running any verifier-driven loop, teams should measure target-task rubric accuracy, rank verifiers by target-task rubric quality rather than parameter count, and treat diminishing returns in above-threshold regimes as a verifier-side compute budget cap.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当优秀的验证器变差时：自我改进的视觉语言模型可能在新任务上退化
验证器驱动的自我DPO是自我改进生产视觉语言模型的常用方法。在此设置中，一个冻结的验证器对候选生成进行评分，得分最高和最低的候选样本构成偏好示例，DPO更新学习者。部署时的假设是单调的：更强的验证器应产生更强的学生。我们表明，该假设可能失败，因为验证器的质量高度依赖于任务。在MathVista、MMMU和BLINK四个开源验证器层级上，那些在MathVista上高于阈值并能提升Qwen-3-VL-2B学生性能的验证器，在MMMU上却低于阈值，其任务评分准确率降至8%至23%。 在此情况下，我们测试的每个验证器都会静默地使学生退化，导致性能下降3.4至10.9个百分点，而DPO训练损失持续下降。该退化现象在另一个学生Qwen-2.5-VL-3B上也得到复现。此外，在失败范围内，损害呈现置信度反转：准确但仍然错误的验证器比接近随机的验证器导致更大的退化，表明进步门控重放会放大自信错误的偏好对。我们通过进步门控重放的方差定理及其方向不匹配失败模式，给出了简洁的机制性解释。 部署信息具有操作性而非纯诊断性：在运行任何验证器驱动的循环之前，团队应测量目标任务评分准确率，按目标任务评分质量而非参数量对验证器进行排序，并将高于阈值范围内的边际收益递减视为验证器侧的计算预算上限。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the risk of verifier-driven self-improvement in visual-language models, revealing that a verifier effective on one task may regress performance on another, even if it scores well on its own rubric. The authors test a range of verifiers across three benchmarks and find that verifiers deemed 'good' on MathVista can drop student performance by 3.4–10.9 percentage points on MMMU, despite decreasing DPO loss. This regression is consistent across two student models and is exacerbated by verifiers that are accurate but still wrong, suggesting that progress-gated replay amplifies harmful preference pairs. The study concludes that verifiers should be evaluated by task-specific rubric accuracy, not parameter size, and that diminishing returns signal a compute budget limit rather than a failure mode.
该研究针对视觉语言模型中常见的基于验证器的自DPO优化方法，指出强验证器并不总能带来更强的学生模型，因为验证器性能高度依赖具体任务。实验表明，某些在MathVista上表现良好的验证器在MMMU上准确率骤降至8–23%，尽管DPO损失持续下降，学生模型反而退化3.4–10.9个百分点。这种退化在两个不同规模的学生模型上均出现，且准确但错误的验证器造成的退化更严重，暗示进度门控重放机制放大了错误偏好对。研究通过方差定理解释了该失败模式，并建议在部署前应测量验证器在目标任务上的评分准确率，而非仅依据参数规模进行选择。
Efficient Rationale-based Retrieval: On-policy Distillation from Generative Rerankers based on JEPA
Authors: Teng Chen, Sheng Xu, Feixiang Guo, Xiaoyu Wang, Qingqing Gu, Hongyan Li, Luo Ji
First: 2026-04-25T14:45:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:55:18+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 8 figures. ICMR 2026 (https://youtu.be/apDcrzEVwq4)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unlike traditional fact-based retrieval, rationale-based retrieval typically necessitates cross-encoding of query-document pairs using large language models, incurring substantial computational costs. To address this limitation, we propose Rabtriever, which independently encodes queries and documents, while providing comparable cross query-document comprehension capabilities to rerankers. We start from training a LLM-based generative reranker, which puts the document prior to the query and prompts the LLM to generate the relevance score by log probabilities. We then employ it as the teacher of an on-policy distillation framework, with Rabtriever as the student to reconstruct the teacher's contextual-aware query embedding. To achieve this effect, Rabtriever is first initialized from the teacher, with parameters frozen. The Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) paradigm is then adopted, which integrates a lightweight, trainable predictor between LLM layers and heads, projecting the query embedding into a new hidden space, with the document embedding as the latent vector. JEPA then minimizes the distribution difference between this projected embedding and the teacher embedding. To strengthen the sampling efficiency of on-policy distillation, we also add an auxiliary loss on the reverse KL of LLM logits, to reshape the student's logit distribution. Rabtriever optimizes the teacher's quadratic complexity on the document length to linear, verified both theoretically and empirically. Experiments show that Rabtriever outperforms different retriever baselines across diverse rationale-based tasks, including empathetic conversations and robotic manipulations, with minor accuracy degradation from the reranker. Rabtriever also generalizes well on traditional retrieval benchmarks such as MS MARCO and BEIR, with comparable performance to the best retriever baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于理由的高效检索：基于JEPA的生成重排序器的在线策略蒸馏
与传统的基于事实的检索不同，基于理由的检索通常需要使用大语言模型对查询-文档对进行交叉编码，带来高昂的计算成本。为解决这一限制，我们提出了Rabtriever，它独立编码查询和文档，同时提供与重排序器相当的跨查询-文档理解能力。我们首先训练一个基于LLM的生成式重排序器，将文档置于查询之前，并提示LLM通过对数概率生成相关性得分。然后将其作为在线策略蒸馏框架中的教师模型，Rabtriever作为学生模型，重建教师的上下文感知查询嵌入。为实现此效果，Rabtriever首先从教师模型初始化，参数冻结。随后采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）范式，在LLM层与头之间集成一个轻量级可训练预测器，将查询嵌入投影到新的隐藏空间，文档嵌入作为潜在向量。JEPA随后最小化该投影嵌入与教师嵌入之间的分布差异。为增强在线策略蒸馏的采样效率，我们还添加了LLM对数概率的反向KL损失，以重塑学生模型的对数概率分布。Rabtriever将教师模型在文档长度上的二次复杂度优化为线性复杂度，理论与实验证明均成立。实验表明，Rabtriever在多种基于理由的任务（包括共情对话和机器人操作）中优于不同检索器基线，且准确率仅略有下降。Rabtriever在传统检索基准（如MS MARCO和BEIR）上也表现出良好的泛化能力，性能与最佳检索器基线相当。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of rationale-based retrieval relying on cross-encoding via large language models, the paper introduces Rabtriever, an on-policy distillation method that distills a generative reranker into a more efficient retriever. The method initializes Rabtriever from the reranker’s parameters, then employs the JEPA paradigm to project query embeddings into a latent space guided by document embeddings, minimizing distribution divergence from the teacher. An auxiliary reverse KL loss reshapes the student’s logit distribution to improve sampling efficiency. Experimentally, Rabtriever reduces document-length complexity from quadratic to linear, achieves competitive performance on rationale-based tasks like empathetic conversations and robotic manipulation, and generalizes well on traditional benchmarks such as MS MARCO and BEIR with minimal accuracy loss compared to the teacher.
为降低基于理由的检索中依赖大语言模型交叉编码带来的高计算开销，本文提出Rabtriever方法，通过在线策略蒸馏将生成式重排序器知识迁移到更高效的检索器。该方法先冻结重排序器参数初始化学生模型，再采用JEPA范式，在LLM层间引入轻量可训练预测器，将查询嵌入投影至由文档嵌入引导的隐空间，并最小化与教师嵌入的分布差异。同时加入反向KL损失以优化学生模型的logit分布，提升采样效率。理论与实验均验证其文档长度复杂度从二次降至线性。实验表明，Rabtriever在情感对话与机器人操作等基于理由的任务上优于现有检索器，且与教师模型精度损失极小，同时在MS MARCO和BEIR等传统检索基准上表现接近最优检索器。
Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering
Authors: Andy Tang, William Chen, Andrew Wagenmaker, Chelsea Finn, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-11T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:52:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging new tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流反转引导提升机器人通用策略
通用策略可从多样化的机器人数据集中学习广泛技能。为解决或改进具有挑战性的新任务，我们需要从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用适当的动作，尤其当直接命令策略失败时。我们聚焦于流匹配通用策略，提出流反转引导（FRS）方法：该方法接受次优但“合理”的动作，通过反向流策略找到其潜在噪声，并将其映射到邻近的通用动作模式。我们在多种模拟和现实世界操作场景中评估FRS。首先，FRS可将人类或视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供的粗粒度语义引导转化为相应的良好机器人动作，提升零样本控制效果。这些收益可通过行为克隆蒸馏：训练辅助策略输出噪声，使通用策略映射为良好动作——在不到一分钟的训练中，任务成功率提升高达95%。最后，FRS通过语义知识引导强化学习，实现策略改进，优于标准RL在多个任务上的表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to leverage generalist policies' behavioral priors for solving novel tasks where direct policy commands fail, this work introduces Flow Reversal Steering (FRS), a method that takes suboptimal but reasonable actions, reverses them through the flow policy to extract latent noise, and maps them to nearby action modes. Evaluated across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, FRS successfully translates coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models into effective robot actions, boosting zero-shot control. When distilled via behavioral cloning, FRS achieves up to 95% absolute task success rate gains within under a minute of training. Additionally, FRS enables reinforcement learning improvement by bootstrapping with semantic knowledge, outperforming standard RL on several previously stagnant tasks.
为了解决直接命令策略在新任务中失效的问题，本文提出流反转引导（FRS）方法，通过逆向流匹配将次优但合理的动作映射为潜在噪声，再投影到邻近的通用策略动作模式。在模拟与真实操作任务中，FRS能将人类或视觉语言模型提供的粗粒度语义指令转化为有效机器人动作，提升零样本控制能力。通过行为克隆蒸馏，该方法可在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的任务成功率提升。此外，FRS还能借助语义知识引导强化学习，使策略在标准强化学习难以改进的任务上取得性能提升。
Characterizing Cultural Localization in AI-Generated Stories
Authors: Shaily Bhatt, Supriti Vijay, Jeremiah Milbauer, Fernando Diaz
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-06-12T16:51:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:51:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 4th Workshop on Cross-Cultural Considerations in NLP (C3NLP) Co-located with ACL 2026, San Diego, USA (non-archival)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The global use of artificial intelligence has increased interest in assessing the ability to generate culturally localized content, including stories. Cultural localization in stories often occurs through either templated localization -- the use of cultural markers (e.g., names, locations) in a generic narrative -- or holistic localization -- the variation of plots, values, and themes, in addition to cultural markers. We propose a method to measure the degree to which content was generated through templated localization. Specifically, we identify the lexical tokens that distinguish stories across nationalities and measure the similarity of the narratives that remain after removing them. In stories generated by five models on 125 topics for 193 nationalities, our method is able to detect that only a small subset (9-17%) of the vocabulary accounts for the variation across nationalities and that the narratives that remain after removing them contain repeated multi-word sequences, suggesting the presence of a shared culturally-agnostic narrative template. Finally, we characterize the cultural markers for their stereotypicality and offensiveness, finding that markers from 19 countries, mostly located in the Global South, are on average offensive.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能生成故事中的文化本地化特征分析
随着人工智能在全球范围内的应用，评估其生成文化本地化内容（包括故事）的能力变得愈发重要。故事中的文化本地化通常通过两种方式实现：模板化本地化——在通用叙事中使用文化标记（如姓名、地点）；或整体本地化——在文化标记之外，调整情节、价值观和主题。我们提出一种测量内容通过模板化本地化生成程度的方法：识别区分不同国籍故事的词汇标记，并测量去除这些标记后叙事的相似性。在对五种模型针对125个主题、193个国籍生成的故事进行分析时，我们的方法发现仅一小部分词汇（9-17%）解释了国籍间的差异，且去除这些词汇后剩余的叙事包含重复的多词序列，表明存在一个共享的、文化中立的叙事模板。最后，我们对文化标记的刻板性和冒犯性进行分析，发现来自19个主要位于全球南方国家的标记平均具有冒犯性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate AI’s capacity to produce culturally localized stories, this study proposes a method to quantify templated localization by identifying lexical tokens that distinguish stories across nationalities and measuring similarity in the narratives after removing them. Applied to stories generated by five models across 125 topics and 193 nationalities, the method revealed that only 9-17% of vocabulary drives nationality-based variation, with residual narratives containing repeated multi-word sequences indicative of a shared culturally-agnostic template. The study also found that cultural markers from 19 countries, primarily in the Global South, were on average offensive, suggesting potential biases in AI-generated content.
为评估AI模型生成文化本地化故事的能力，本研究提出一种量化模板化本地化的方法：识别区分不同国籍故事的词汇，并在移除这些词汇后测量剩余叙事的相似性。在对193个国籍、125个主题、五种模型生成的故事进行分析后，发现仅9-17%的词汇驱动国籍差异，移除后剩余叙事中出现重复多词序列，表明存在共享的无文化偏倚叙事模板。研究进一步发现，来自19个主要位于全球南方国家的文化标记平均具有冒犯性，暗示AI生成内容可能存在偏见。
ParkourFormer: Integrating Predictive Supervision and Sequence Modeling into Parkour Locomotion
Authors: Yanheng Mai, Wenhao Xu, Zirui Huang, Yifei Fu, Shengwei Dong, Xinjue Wang, Kailun Huang, Yanzhe Xie, Renjing Xu
First: 2026-05-25T12:29:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:51:37+00:00
Comments: Project Homepage: https://mronaldo-gif.github.io/parkourformer.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humanoid parkour requires locomotion policies to coordinate whole-body dynamics across rapidly changing terrains such as stairs, gaps, slopes, and obstacles. Existing reinforcement learning policies are largely reactive, mapping observations directly to actions without explicitly modeling future body states. Such modeling becomes critical in agile locomotion tasks where successful motion execution depends strongly on anticipating upcoming contact transitions and body dynamics. We present ParkourFormer, a Transformer-based sequence modeling framework that reformulates humanoid locomotion as a future-conditioned decision-making problem. The current robot state queries historical sensorimotor trajectories through cross-attention, while a lightweight prediction head forecasts short-horizon future proprioceptive states. The predicted future states, trained with supervised signals, are fused with temporal features to generate actions, enabling the policy to jointly reason over motion history and anticipated future dynamics. We evaluate ParkourFormer on a diverse multi-terrain humanoid parkour benchmark including stairs, gaps, slopes, rough terrain, and obstacle traversal. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid robot show that ParkourFormer achieves a 93.85% average traversal success rate on highly challenging terrains, with improvements of up to 47.12% over strong MLP, MoE-based MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines, while maintaining a single unified policy across all terrain types. These results demonstrate that explicit future-state modeling significantly improves robustness and generalization for agile whole-body locomotion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ParkourFormer：将预测监督与序列建模整合到跑酷运动中
人形机器人跑酷需要运动策略协调全身动力学，以应对楼梯、间隙、斜坡和障碍物等快速变化的地形。现有强化学习策略大多反应式，直接将观测映射到动作，而不显式建模未来身体状态。在敏捷运动任务中，这种建模至关重要，因为成功运动执行高度依赖于预测即将发生的接触转换和身体动力学。我们提出ParkourFormer，一种基于Transformer的序列建模框架，将人形运动重新表述为未来条件下的决策问题。当前机器人状态通过交叉注意力查询历史感觉运动轨迹，同时一个轻量级预测头预测短期未来本体感觉状态。这些经监督信号训练的预测未来状态与时间特征融合生成动作，使策略能够联合推理运动历史与预期未来动力学。我们在包含楼梯、间隙、斜坡、崎岖地形和障碍物穿越的多样化多地形人形跑酷基准上评估ParkourFormer。仿真和真实人形机器人实验表明，ParkourFormer在极具挑战性的地形上平均穿越成功率高达93.85%，相比强MLP、MoE-MLP和基础Transformer基线提升最高达47.12%，同时保持单一统一策略适用于所有地形类型。结果表明，显式未来状态建模显著提升了敏捷全身运动的鲁棒性和泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
To improve humanoid robot locomotion across complex terrains, ParkourFormer introduces a Transformer-based framework that explicitly models future body states to guide motion decisions. It uses cross-attention to query historical sensorimotor data and a lightweight prediction head to forecast short-term proprioceptive states, which are then fused with temporal features to generate actions. Evaluated on diverse parkour terrains including stairs, gaps, and obstacles, the method achieves a 93.85% average success rate, outperforming MLP, MoE-MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines by up to 47.12%, while maintaining a single policy across all environments.
为提升人形机器人在复杂地形（如楼梯、间隙、斜坡和障碍物）上的运动能力，ParkourFormer提出一种基于Transformer的序列建模框架，显式建模未来身体状态。该方法通过交叉注意力机制查询历史传感器运动数据，并用轻量预测头预测短期本体感觉状态，再融合时序特征生成动作。在包含多种地形的公园跑动基准测试中，其平均通过率为93.85%，相比MLP、MoE-MLP和基础Transformer基线最高提升47.12%，且保持单一策略适用于所有地形。
Towards Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models by Refining Textual Embeddings
Authors: Aakriti Agrawal, Gouthaman KV, Rohith Aralikatti, Gauri Jagatap, Jiaxin Yuan, Sarvesh Baskar, Vijay Kamarshi, Andrea Fanelli, Furong Huang
First: 2025-11-07T06:39:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:49:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted at The 64th Annual Meeting of the Association for Computational Linguistics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) remain a persistent challenge, often stemming from inadequate integration of visual information during multimodal reasoning. A key cause is the model's over-reliance on textual priors and underutilization of visual cues, leading to outputs that are linguistically fluent but visually inaccurate. For example, given an image of an empty kitchen countertop, an LVLM might hallucinate a "bowl of fruit" or "cup of coffee", relying on language associations rather than visual evidence. Most LVLMs incorporate visual features by appending them to the input stream of a pre-trained LLM and training on large-scale vision-language datasets. Our systematic analysis reveals that this strategy often leads to over-dependence on textual information due to the inherent bias of LLMs towards language-dominant representations. This imbalance skews attention towards the text over visual content, weakening the model's ability to ground outputs in visual inputs. To address this, we propose a simple yet effective visual feature incorporation method that encourages the model to learn visually-informed textual embeddings distinct from those of the base LLM and promotes a more balanced attention distribution. Experimental results across multiple hallucination benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly reduces hallucinations and fosters more balanced multimodal reasoning. Notably, our approach achieves substantial gains, including +9.33% on MMVP-MLLM, +2.99% on POPE-AOKVQA, up to +3.4% on Merlin, and +3% on the hard-data split of HallusionBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过优化文本嵌入缓解大型视觉-语言模型中的幻觉
大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）中的幻觉问题依然普遍存在，主要源于多模态推理过程中视觉信息整合不足。关键原因在于模型过度依赖文本先验而忽视视觉线索，导致输出语言流畅但视觉不准确。例如，面对空厨房台面图像，LVLM可能凭空生成“水果碗”或“咖啡杯”，依赖语言联想而非视觉证据。多数LVLM通过将视觉特征附加至预训练LLM的输入流，并在大规模视觉-语言数据集上训练。我们的系统性分析表明，该策略因LLM固有的语言主导表征偏见，常导致模型过度依赖文本信息，使注意力偏向文本而削弱对视觉输入的锚定能力。为此，我们提出一种简单有效的视觉特征融合方法，促使模型学习与基础LLM不同的视觉引导文本嵌入，并促进注意力分布更均衡。在多个幻觉基准测试中，实验结果表明该方法显著减少幻觉并提升多模态推理的平衡性。值得注意的是，该方法在MMVP-MLLM上提升9.33%，在POPE-AOKVQA上提升2.99%，在Merlin上最高提升3.4%，在HallusionBench的困难数据子集上提升3%。
Summary / 总结
To address hallucinations in large vision-language models caused by over-reliance on textual priors and underutilization of visual cues, the authors propose refining textual embeddings to encourage visually-informed representations distinct from the base LLM’s language-centric ones. Their method modifies how visual features are integrated into the model’s input stream, promoting balanced attention between text and image modalities. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show significant reductions in hallucinations, with gains of up to +9.33% on MMVP-MLLM, +3.4% on Merlin, and +3% on the hard split of HallusionBench.
为解决大视觉-语言模型因过度依赖文本先验而产生的幻觉问题，作者提出通过优化文本嵌入，促使模型学习与基础语言模型不同的视觉引导型表示。该方法调整视觉特征的输入方式，以平衡文本与视觉的注意力分配。在多个基准测试中，该方法显著降低幻觉率：在MMVP-MLLM上提升9.33%，在POPE-AOKVQA上提升2.99%，在Merlin上提升3.4%，在HallusionBench困难数据集上提升3%。
Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens
Authors: Ali Asaria, Tony Salomone, Deep Gandhi
First: 2026-06-12T16:45:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:45:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.
中文标题/摘要
标题：既非并行也非顺序：DiffusionGemma实际如何提交标记
开源扩散语言模型被宣传为并行、非自回归解码器，但实际部署的模型检查点按何种顺序提交标记几乎从未被测量。我们对基于Gemma 4构建的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型DiffusionGemma 26B进行仪器化，将其采样器的接受步骤挂钩，记录各画布位置何时以何种置信度提交标记。在686个提示、六个模式的探针测试中，我们发现其解码既非并行也非块自回归：它遵循一种部分左到右提交偏倚，其表观强度几乎完全取决于你观察的粒度。顺序在逐标记层面较弱，但随着分析粒度粗化，顺序逐渐增强，因此模型的“块大小”实为测量工具的产物而非架构本身。模型以大批次同时提交标记，批次内顺序大部分真正未定义，而非仅未观测到。该行为具有模式依赖性：结构化JSON几乎任意顺序提交，位置的提交置信度与数学推理正确性相关，但对事实回忆无信号。提交行为激进，在步数预算内后期短时集中完成，而任务准确率与模型自回归的Gemma-4兄弟相当。除上述发现外，我们的核心贡献是方法论层面：诚实地测量解码顺序需处理尾部EOS填充、模式内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性及大提交批次平局，否则可能人为制造出并不存在的解码顺序结果。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how DiffusionGemma 26B, a diffusion-based language model, actually commits tokens during decoding, challenging the assumption that it operates in parallel or block-autoregressive fashion. By instrumenting the model’s sampler to log token commitment positions, timing, and confidence across 686 prompts and six task regimes, the authors find that token commitment exhibits a partial left-to-right bias that varies with measurement granularity—appearing weak at fine resolution but strengthening when coarsened. The model commits tokens in large simultaneous batches, leaving internal batch order largely undefined. Commitment behavior differs by task: structured JSON is committed arbitrarily, while mathematical reasoning correctness correlates with commit confidence, but not factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing early within the step budget, yet accuracy matches its autoregressive counterpart. The key methodological contribution is identifying and correcting for artifacts like trailing-EOS padding, confounding within-regime effects, non-monotonic commitment, block-size sensitivity, and tied batch commits, which can falsely suggest parallel or block-autoregressive behavior.
为填补开源扩散语言模型中令牌提交顺序实证研究的空白，该研究对DiffusionGemma 26B进行仪器化，记录解码过程中每个令牌的提交时间与位置，发现其行为既非完全并行也非块自回归，而是表现出一种部分左到右的提交偏倚，其强度取决于分析粒度——令牌级顺序较弱，而粗粒度分析则增强结构感。模型以大批次同时提交令牌，批次内顺序多未定义而非未观测，且行为随任务类型变化：结构化JSON的提交顺序近乎随机，提交置信度与数学推理正确性相关但与事实记忆无关。尽管如此，其准确率与自回归的Gemma-4相当，且在步数预算内提前完成解码。该研究的核心贡献在于方法论，指出如尾部EOS填充、域内干扰、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性及大批次提交平局等常见问题，若未妥善处理，可能人为制造出并行解码的假象。
StereoGeo: an end-to-end stereo camera calibration method
Authors: Imane Meddour, Andréa Macario Barros, Cédric Gouy-Pailler
First: 2026-06-12T16:44:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:44:59+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure, accepted at the 34th European Signal Processing Conference (EUSIPCO 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In this work, we propose StereoGeo, an end-to-end network-based approach for stereo camera calibration. Our method estimates the focal lengths and gravity directions of the left and right cameras, as well as the relative extrinsic transformation relating them. Existing methods often rely on calibration patterns in structured environments or address only a single camera configuration, being limited to either intrinsic or extrinsic estimation, and depending on a multi-view setups. StereoGeo extends the GeoCalib algorithm, integrating deep neural network feature extraction with a differentiable optimizer. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that StereoGeo achieves competitive performance for intrinsic calibration and provides accurate stereo extrinsic estimation, outperforming existing methods that are limited to monocular settings. The dataset used in this work is partially publicly available at https://github.com/meddourimane/StereoGeo-dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StereoGeo：一种端到端的立体相机标定方法
本文提出StereoGeo，一种基于端到端网络的立体相机标定方法。该方法估计左右相机的焦距和重力方向，以及它们之间的相对外参变换。现有方法常依赖结构化环境中的标定图案，或仅针对单一相机配置，受限于仅能估计内参或外参，且依赖多视角设置。StereoGeo扩展了GeoCalib算法，将深度神经网络特征提取与可微分优化器相结合。在真实世界基准数据上的大量实验表明，StereoGeo在内参标定方面表现优异，并能提供精确的立体外参估计，优于仅限单目设置的现有方法。本研究使用的数据集部分公开于https://github.com/meddourimane/StereoGeo-dataset。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing stereo camera calibration methods that require structured environments or only handle single-camera setups, StereoGeo proposes an end-to-end deep learning approach that jointly estimates intrinsic parameters (focal lengths and gravity directions) and extrinsic transformation between left and right cameras. By extending the GeoCalib algorithm with differentiable neural feature extraction and optimization, the method achieves competitive intrinsic calibration accuracy and superior extrinsic estimation compared to monocular-only baselines, validated on real-world benchmarks.
为克服现有立体相机标定方法依赖标定图案或仅处理单目配置的局限，StereoGeo 提出一种端到端的神经网络方法，联合估计左右相机的焦距、重力方向及相对外参变换。该方法在 GeoCalib 算法基础上，结合深度特征提取与可微优化器，实现无需结构化环境的内参与外参统一标定。实验表明，StereoGeo 在真实场景基准测试中达到领先的内参标定精度，并显著优于仅适用于单目设置的现有方法。
Estimation of Ground Reaction Forces from Kinematic Data during Locomotion
Authors: Gautami Golani, Dong Anh Khoa To, Ananda Sidarta, Arun-Kumar Kaliya-Perumal, Oliver Roberts, Lek Syn Lim, Jim Patton, Domenico Campolo
First: 2026-02-03T06:45:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T16:42:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ground reaction forces (GRFs) provide fundamental insight into human gait mechanics and are widely used to assess joint loading, limb symmetry, balance control, and motor function. Despite their clinical relevance, the use of GRF remains underutilised in clinical workflows due to the practical limitations of force plate systems. In this work, we present a force-plate-free approach for estimating GRFs using only marker-based motion capture data. This kinematics only method to estimate and decompose GRF makes it well suited for widespread clinical depolyment. By using kinematics from sixteen body segments, we estimate the centre of mass (CoM) and compute GRFs, which are subsequently decomposed into individual components through a minimization-based approach. Through this framework, we can identify gait stance phases and provide access to clinically meaningful kinetic measures without a dedicated force plate system. Experimental results demonstrate the viability of CoM and GRF estimation based solely on kinematic data, supporting force-plate-free gait analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于运动学数据的地面反作用力估计
地面反作用力（GRF）为人类步态力学提供了基本洞察，并广泛用于评估关节负荷、肢体对称性、平衡控制和运动功能。尽管其临床相关性高，但由于测力台系统的实际限制，GRF在临床工作流程中仍被低估。本文提出一种无需测力台的方法，仅利用基于标记的运动捕捉数据来估计GRF。该仅依赖运动学的方法可将GRF分解为各分量，非常适合广泛临床部署。通过十六个身体部位的运动学数据，我们估计质心（CoM）并计算GRF，再通过最小化方法将其分解为各分量。通过该框架，可识别步态支撑相，并在无需专用测力台系统的情况下提供具有临床意义的力学指标。实验结果验证了仅基于运动学数据即可实现CoM和GRF估计的可行性，支持无测力台步态分析。
