We further show that under JSON prompts all four variants reach parity on standalone quality, the variants separate on fidelity and text rendering, not on aggregate image-quality scores, and that text legibility, near-zero when the model is prompted with raw strings, reaches 55% OCR exact-match under the JSON captions it expects. We release the INT8 W8A8 and GGUF Q4_K quantized weights on Hugging Face under a gated, non-commercial license.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在8位权重和激活下保持FP8质量上限：面向消费级GPU的Ideogram 4.0 INT8与GGUF后训练量化
我们研究了Ideogram 4.0（一个9.3B参数的流匹配扩散Transformer，采用分类器自由引导，包含单流骨干的两个独立权重副本，由Qwen3-VL文本编码器条件控制）的后训练量化（PTQ），目标为缺乏FP8张量核心的Ampere RTX 3090 GPU。由于Ideogram 4.0在结构化JSON标题上训练，我们使用为Ideogram发布标题规范构建的LLM扩展器生成的schema有效JSON提示进行评估，并通过涵盖人类偏好（HPSv2）、CLIP和PickScore（独立质量）、PP-OCR精确匹配与编辑距离（文本）以及PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS（与FP8参考输出的保真度）的测试集进行评分。在300个提示的基准测试中，配对Bootstrap置信区间显示，INT8 W8A8方案（按通道权重、按令牌动态激活、SmoothQuant及对小高脆弱层集的bf16保护）在CLIP和PickScore上与FP8统计上无显著差异（配对置信区间包含零），HPSv2差距约0.004，且在8位大小下是FP8输出最忠实的复现（LPIPS 0.243 vs 0.277/0.306的半大小4位基线；INT8-Q4_K差距排除零）。GGUF Q4_K量化在相同磁盘大小下达到与已发布NF4基线相同的独立质量，成为质量-内存前沿上的帕累托最优选择。我们进一步表明，在JSON提示下，四种变体在独立质量上达到一致，其差异体现在保真度与文本渲染上，而非综合图像质量评分；当模型被原始字符串提示时，文本可读性接近零，但在预期的JSON标题下可达到55%的OCR精确匹配。我们将在Hugging Face上以受限非商业许可发布INT8 W8A8和GGUF Q4_K量化权重。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy Ideogram 4.0 — a 9.3B diffusion transformer trained on structured JSON captions — on consumer Ampere RTX 3090 GPUs lacking FP8 tensor cores, the authors explore post-training quantization methods. They evaluate INT8 W8A8 and GGUF Q4_K variants under schema-valid JSON prompts generated by an LLM expander, measuring quality via HPSv2, CLIP, PickScore, OCR metrics, and fidelity to FP8 reference outputs. Results show the INT8 W8A8 quantization is statistically indistinguishable from FP8 on CLIP and PickScore, with only a 0.004 HPSv2 gap, and achieves superior fidelity (LPIPS 0.243) compared to 4-bit baselines. The GGUF Q4_K variant matches the published NF4 baseline in standalone quality at equal disk size, forming a Pareto-optimal trade-off. All variants perform similarly on aggregate image quality, but differ in text rendering fidelity and OCR accuracy, which reaches 55% exact-match under expected JSON prompts.
为在缺乏FP8张量核心的消费级Ampere RTX 3090显卡上部署训练于结构化JSON标题的9.3B扩散Transformer模型Ideogram 4.0，作者研究了后训练量化方法。他们使用由LLM生成器生成的符合Schema的JSON提示，评估INT8 W8A8和GGUF Q4_K变体，通过人类偏好、CLIP、PickScore、OCR及与FP8参考输出的保真度等指标进行评分。结果表明，INT8 W8A8量化在CLIP和PickScore上与FP8无统计差异，HPSv2仅下降约0.004，且在4位基线中保真度最高（LPIPS 0.243）。GGUF Q4_K变体在同等磁盘大小下达到与已发布NF4基线相同的质量，构成质量-内存帕累托最优解。所有变体在独立质量上表现相近，但在保真度和文本渲染上分化；在JSON提示下，文本可读性提升至55% OCR精确匹配率。
CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation
Authors: Guanyu Liu, Weiyi Kong, Zeyu Wang, Boer Zhang, Baiqing Li, Peiyu Zhang, Tianyu Shi
First: 2026-06-12T15:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:58:32+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CARE：通过可审计证据审查控制大语言模型生成的实验策略
赋予大语言模型（LLM）对昂贵且不可逆的科学实验的直接控制权，会导致不安全的探索和性能不稳定；但完全抛弃LLM的创造力则会牺牲显著的优化潜力。我们提出了CARE（通过可审计证据审查控制大语言模型生成的实验策略），这是一种用于高通量实验（HTE）优化的可审计控制器。CARE默认采用非LLM的现有优化器作为行动路径，同时利用LLM修订挑战者策略的排序。在每次结果公布前，一个公开证据干预门会比较挑战者与现有方案，仅当选择前可用的证据支持变更时才授权挑战者被选中，并将决策记录在审计日志中。CARE在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准测试中均优于所有评估方法，相对于公开现有方案，Minerva/Olympus的最终最佳性能从80.0提升至88.5，ChemLex从83.9提升至92.1。我们的实验表明，当LLM在可审计控制器下扩展提案空间时，其自我演化更可靠，而非直接选择实验。
Summary / 总结
To address the risks of unsafe and unstable LLM-driven scientific experimentation while preserving optimization potential, CARE introduces an auditable controller that maintains a non-LLM optimizer as default while letting LLMs propose alternative policies. Before each experiment outcome is revealed, a public-evidence gate compares the LLM’s challenger policy against the incumbent, authorizing the switch only if available evidence supports the change, with decisions logged for audit. CARE consistently outperforms other methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, improving final-best performance from 80.0 to 88.5 and from 83.9 to 92.1 relative to public incumbents, demonstrating that LLM self-evolution is more reliable under auditable constraints than direct experiment selection.
为在保障科学实验安全性和稳定性的同时保留LLM的优化潜力，CARE提出一种可审计控制器，以非LLM优化器作为默认策略，由LLM提出替代策略。在每次实验结果揭晓前，一个公开证据门控机制比较挑战策略与当前策略，仅当现有证据支持切换时才批准挑战策略，并将决策记录在审计日志中。实验表明，CARE在Minerva/Olympus和ChemLex基准上均优于其他方法，最终最佳得分分别从80.0提升至88.5、从83.9提升至92.1，证明在可审计框架下LLM自我演化比直接选择实验更可靠。
Persuasion Index: A Theory-Guided Framework for Persuasion Analysis
Authors: Liancheng Gong, Zhiyang Wang, Yiwei Xu, Julia Mendelsohn
First: 2026-06-12T15:58:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:58:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Identifying persuasive rhetorical cues is critical across domains, from detecting information manipulation and improving AI safety to advancing public health communication. We propose Persuasion Index (PI), a taxonomy of 15 dimensions grounded in persuasion theories from psychology and communication, and one transparent implementation using 55 sub-features built from lexicons and rule-based detectors. The taxonomy is modular: individual detectors can be replaced while preserving the theoretical structure. By evaluating PI on four public datasets varying in domain, style, and outcome measures, we show that PI provides a shared feature space for interpreting rhetorical patterns associated with persuasion-related outcomes. Linear models show that PI features carry meaningful predictive signal while remaining computationally lightweight. Dimension-level analyses reveal recurring associations between PI dimensions and persuasion outcomes across datasets, while also highlighting topic- and stance-specific variation. We release PI as an open-source package and web interface for principled and auditable analysis of human and AI-mediated communication.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说服指数：一种基于理论的说服分析框架
识别说服性修辞线索在多个领域至关重要，从检测信息操纵、提升人工智能安全到推动公共卫生传播。我们提出说服指数（PI），该框架基于心理学与传播学中的说服理论，构建了15个维度的分类体系，并提供一个透明的实现方案，包含55个子特征，由词典和基于规则的检测器构建。该分类体系具有模块化特性：可替换单个检测器，同时保持理论结构不变。通过在四个不同领域、风格和结果度量的公开数据集上评估PI，我们证明PI为解释与说服相关结果相关的修辞模式提供了一个共享特征空间。线性模型表明，PI特征具有显著的预测能力，且计算开销低。维度级分析揭示了PI各维度与说服结果在不同数据集间反复出现的关联，同时也突出了主题和立场相关的差异。我们以开源包和网页界面形式发布PI，以支持对人类与AI介导沟通的规范且可审计的分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to systematically analyze persuasive communication across domains like AI safety and public health, the authors introduce Persuasion Index (PI), a theory-driven taxonomy of 15 persuasion dimensions derived from psychology and communication theories, implemented via 55 rule-based and lexicon-based sub-features. PI’s modular design allows individual detectors to be swapped without altering the theoretical framework. Evaluated across four diverse datasets, PI consistently provides a shared feature space for predicting persuasion-related outcomes, with linear models demonstrating strong predictive power while remaining computationally efficient. Dimension-level analyses reveal consistent cross-dataset associations between specific PI dimensions and persuasion outcomes, while also uncovering topic- and stance-dependent variations.
为系统分析跨领域（如AI安全与公共卫生）中的说服性沟通，本文提出Persuasion Index（PI），一个基于心理学与传播学理论的15维说服性分类体系，通过55个基于词典与规则的子特征实现。PI采用模块化设计，允许替换单个检测器而不破坏理论结构。在四个不同领域、风格与评估指标的数据集上评估，PI展现出强大的预测能力，且计算开销低；维度级分析揭示了说服性结果与特定维度在多数据集中的共性关联，同时凸显了主题与立场相关的差异。该框架作为开源工具发布，支持人类与AI媒介沟通的透明、可审计分析。
VISTA: View-Consistent Self-Verified Training for GUI Grounding
Authors: Xinyu Qiu, Yunzhu Zhang, Heng Jia, Shuheng Shen, Changhua Meng, Linchao Zhu
First: 2026-06-12T15:58:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:58:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When applying Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for GUI Grounding, rollouts are sampled from a single screenshot view; groups often become either all failures on difficult instances or all successes on easy ones, yielding no useful relative advantage. We propose VISTA (View-Consistent Self-Verified Training), a GRPO-based training framework that constructs each comparison group from multiple target-preserving views of the same GUI instance.Each view is generated by a crop that keeps the target element visible and remaps its box exactly, so model rollouts are compared across semantically equivalent but geometrically different inputs. To stabilize short coordinate generation without turning reinforcement learning into unconditional imitation, VISTA further adds a self-verified cross-view anchor: an oracle answer optimized with an advantage-weighted loss, excluded from the group baseline and activated only when the model has produced a maximum-reward rollout. Across five GUI-grounding benchmarks and multiple Qwen backbones, VISTA consistently improves grounding accuracy.On ScreenSpot-Pro, it raises Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B from 55.5/52.7/53.7 to 63.4/65.8/67.0. Robustness analyses further show higher worst-view accuracy and lower prediction flip rates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VISTA：用于GUI定位的视图一致自验证训练
在应用组相对策略优化（GRPO）进行GUI定位时，采样自单一截图视图的rollouts往往导致组内结果要么在困难实例上全失败，要么在简单实例上全成功，无法提供有用相对优势。我们提出VISTA（视图一致自验证训练），一种基于GRPO的训练框架，从同一GUI实例的多个目标保持视图中构建每个比较组。每个视图通过裁剪生成，确保目标元素可见并精确重映射其框，从而使模型rollouts在语义等价但几何不同的输入间进行比较。为在不将强化学习转化为无条件模仿的前提下稳定短坐标生成，VISTA进一步引入自验证跨视图锚点：一个通过优势加权损失优化的oracle答案，排除在组基线之外，仅在模型生成最大奖励rollout时激活。在五个GUI定位基准和多个Qwen骨干模型上，VISTA持续提升定位准确率。在ScreenSpot-Pro上，Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B的准确率从55.5/52.7/53.7提升至63.4/65.8/67.0。鲁棒性分析进一步表明其最差视图准确率更高、预测翻转率更低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of GRPO in GUI grounding due to single-view rollouts causing biased comparison groups, VISTA introduces a view-consistent self-verified training framework that constructs comparison groups from multiple target-preserving views of the same GUI instance, each generated via cropping that maintains target visibility and exact bounding box remapping. To stabilize coordinate generation without degenerating into imitation learning, VISTA incorporates a self-verified cross-view anchor—an oracle answer optimized with advantage-weighted loss, activated only when the model achieves maximum reward and excluded from the baseline. Across five benchmarks and multiple Qwen backbones, VISTA consistently boosts grounding accuracy, raising Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B scores on ScreenSpot-Pro from 55.5/52.7/53.7 to 63.4/65.8/67.0, while also improving worst-view accuracy and reducing prediction flip rates.
为解决GRPO在GUI定位中因单视角采样导致比较组信息不足的问题，VISTA提出一种视图一致的自验证训练框架，通过从同一GUI实例的多个目标保留视图中构建比较组，每个视图通过裁剪生成并精确重映射目标元素的边界框。为避免训练不稳定且不退化为无条件模仿，VISTA引入自验证跨视图锚点——一个通过优势加权损失优化的oracle答案，仅在模型生成最大奖励轨迹时激活且不参与基线计算。在五个基准和多个Qwen模型上，VISTA持续提升定位准确率，Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B在ScreenSpot-Pro上从55.5/52.7/53.7提升至63.4/65.8/67.0，并在鲁棒性分析中表现出更高的最差视图准确率和更低的预测翻转率。
A Qualitative Review of GenAI-Based Methods for Data Generation and Augmentation in Industrial Computer Vision Applications
Authors: Paul Koch, Paul Hofmann, Ferdinand Waßelewsky, Adem Karakurt, Andre Sérs, Jörg Krüger
First: 2026-06-12T15:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:57:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Computing Conference 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-driven computer vision applications require a profound database to ensure predictable behaviors and performance. Such predictable behaviors are especially important for industrial applications in gaining trust from users. However, such a database is not readily available in industrial applications, and its acquisition is not trivial either. Active learning methods can be applied to ramp up data within a project deployment to iteratively increase the database, and thus the application predictability. Unfortunately, we observe that this often leads to a loss of user trust in the application, which is difficult to regain once lost. This leads to a "chicken-and-egg" dilemma in which neither the database nor the application is developed. In this work, we review state-of-the-art methods and approaches to further boost the database the initial active data ramp-up phase. Here, we focus on recent advancements in GenAI-based data generation and augmentation methods and review their adaptability on an industrial computer vision classification use case. Although we observe a potential for automatic data ramp-up, we also see a domain miss match in between the source (training environment) and target (industrial use-case) - regarding context defined in natural language and object characteristics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于生成式人工智能的数据生成与增强方法在工业计算机视觉应用中的定性综述
AI驱动的计算机视觉应用需要庞大的数据库以确保可预测的行为和性能，这对于工业应用尤其重要，有助于赢得用户信任。然而，工业应用中此类数据库并不容易获得，其获取也非易事。主动学习方法可用于项目部署初期扩充数据，逐步扩大数据库，从而提升应用的可预测性。但不幸的是，这常导致用户对应用的信任度下降，一旦失去则难以恢复，形成“鸡生蛋、蛋生鸡”的困境，致使数据库和应用均难以发展。本文综述了当前最先进的方法，旨在进一步提升初始阶段的主动数据扩充效果。我们重点关注近期基于生成式人工智能的数据生成与增强方法，并评估其在工业计算机视觉分类场景中的适用性。尽管我们观察到自动数据扩充的潜力，但也发现源（训练环境）与目标（工业应用场景）之间存在领域不匹配——特别是在自然语言定义的上下文和物体特征方面。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of building reliable databases for industrial computer vision applications, where data scarcity hinders model performance and user trust. It reviews recent GenAI-based methods for data generation and augmentation, focusing on their applicability in classification tasks, and finds that while these methods can accelerate initial data ramp-up, they often suffer from domain mismatch between training environments and real-world industrial contexts, particularly in natural language context and object characteristics, which limits their effectiveness in practical deployment.
本文针对工业计算机视觉应用中数据稀缺导致模型性能不足和用户信任缺失的问题，综述了基于生成式人工智能的数据生成与增强方法，重点关注其在工业分类任务中的适用性。尽管这些方法在加速初始数据积累方面具有潜力，但研究发现生成数据与真实工业场景及物体特征存在显著领域不匹配，从而影响模型可靠性与用户信任。
X-OPD: Cross-Modal On-Policy Distillation for Capability Alignment in Speech LLMs
Authors: Di Cao, Dongjie Fu, Hai Yu, Siqi Zheng, Xu Tan, Tao Jin
First: 2026-03-06T06:04:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:53:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted by Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While the shift from cascaded dialogue systems to end-to-end (E2E) speech Large Language Models (LLMs) improves latency and paralinguistic modeling, E2E models often exhibit a significant performance degradation compared to their text-based counterparts. The standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) training methods fail to close this gap. To address this, we propose X-OPD, a novel Cross-Modal On-Policy Distillation framework designed to systematically align the capabilities of Speech LLMs to their text-based counterparts. X-OPD enables the Speech LLM to explore its own distribution via on-policy rollouts, where a text-based teacher model evaluates these trajectories and provides token-level feedback, effectively distilling teacher's capabilities into student's multi-modal representations. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that X-OPD significantly narrows the gap in complex tasks while preserving the model's inherent capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：X-OPD：面向语音大语言模型能力对齐的跨模态在线蒸馏
尽管从级联对话系统转向端到端（E2E）语音大语言模型（LLMs）可提升延迟并增强副语言建模能力，但E2E模型通常在性能上显著落后于其文本型对应模型。标准的监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）训练方法难以弥合这一差距。为此，我们提出X-OPD，一种新颖的跨模态在线蒸馏框架，旨在系统性地将语音LLM的能力对齐至其文本型对应模型。X-OPD使语音LLM通过在线策略滚动探索自身分布，文本型教师模型评估这些轨迹并提供词级反馈，从而将教师能力有效蒸馏至学生模型的多模态表示中。在多个基准测试中的广泛实验表明，X-OPD在复杂任务中显著缩小了性能差距，同时保留了模型固有能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance gap between speech-based and text-based LLMs in end-to-end dialogue systems, the authors propose X-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that aligns speech LLM capabilities with text counterparts. The method enables the speech model to generate its own trajectories via on-policy rollouts, which are then evaluated and refined by a text-based teacher model providing token-level feedback. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show that X-OPD significantly reduces the performance gap in complex tasks while preserving the speech model’s inherent multimodal strengths.
为解决端到端语音大语言模型在复杂任务中性能显著落后于文本模型的问题，作者提出X-OPD方法，通过跨模态在线策略蒸馏，使语音模型在自身分布中生成轨迹，并由文本教师模型提供逐token反馈进行优化，从而将教师能力迁移到学生模型的多模态表示中。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著缩小了性能差距，同时保留了语音模型的固有优势。
SIMMER: Benchmarking Latent Failures in LLM Executable Planning with a World Model
Authors: Xiaoxin Lu, Ranran Haoran Zhang, Rui Zhang
First: 2026-06-12T15:53:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:53:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as planners for autonomous agents in household environments. While existing benchmarks evaluate whether LLM-generated plans execute successfully, they overlook a critical type of failure: latent failures. Unlike immediate failures that trigger instant feedback at execution time and enable timely correction, latent failures do not immediately halt plan execution but silently compromise goal achievement. In severe cases, they cause irreversible harm. To address this gap, we introduce SIMMER, a benchmark for evaluating latent failures in LLM planning through a human-curated symbolic world model grounded in the kitchen domain. SIMMER defines a world model comprising 77 actions, 262 unique objects, and approximately 46,800 possible interactions that are semantically realistic, derived from real-world cooking scripts. It then leverages a state machine executor that validates plans against the world model and detects immediate precondition violations, latent hazards, and irreversible failures. Experiments across six LLMs show that even frontier models achieve at most 17% error-free plans. Moreover, up to 56% of plans contain latent failures, the majority of which lead to irreversible consequences. We further demonstrate that explicit state reasoning via counterfactual foresight simulation can reduce latent failures by up to 72% and irreversible cases by up to 75%, suggesting a promising direction for more robust LLM planners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIMMER：基于世界模型评估大语言模型可执行规划中的潜在失败
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地被部署为家庭环境中自主代理的规划器。现有基准评估LLM生成的计划是否成功执行，但忽略了关键类型的失败：潜在失败。与在执行时立即触发反馈并允许及时纠正的即时失败不同，潜在失败不会立即终止计划执行，而是静默地损害目标达成。在严重情况下，会导致不可逆损害。为解决这一问题，我们引入SIMMER，一个基于人类编纂的厨房领域符号化世界模型的基准，用于评估LLM规划中的潜在失败。SIMMER定义了一个包含77个动作、262个独特对象和约46,800种语义现实的可能交互的世界模型，这些交互源自真实烹饪脚本。随后，它利用状态机执行器验证计划与世界模型的一致性，并检测即时前提违规、潜在危险和不可逆失败。在六种LLM上的实验表明，即使是前沿模型最多也只能实现17%的无错误计划。此外，高达56%的计划包含潜在失败，其中大多数导致不可逆后果。我们进一步证明，通过反事实前瞻模拟进行显式状态推理，可将潜在失败减少高达72%，不可逆案例减少高达75%，表明了构建更稳健LLM规划器的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate latent failures—silent, non-immediate errors in LLM-generated plans that can lead to irreversible harm—the paper introduces SIMMER, a benchmark using a human-curated symbolic world model based on kitchen environments. The method defines a detailed world model with 77 actions, 262 objects, and 46,800 interactions, then employs a state machine executor to detect immediate violations, latent hazards, and irreversible failures. Experiments across six LLMs reveal that even top models produce at most 17% error-free plans, with up to 56% containing latent failures, most resulting in irreversible outcomes. The study further shows that counterfactual foresight simulation can reduce latent failures by 72% and irreversible failures by 75%, highlighting its potential for improving LLM planning robustness.
为评估LLM生成计划中不立即中断执行但会无声损害目标达成的潜在失败，作者提出SIMMER基准，基于厨房环境构建的人工符号化世界模型，包含77个动作、262个对象及约4.68万种现实语义交互。通过状态机执行器验证计划，识别即时违反、潜在危险及不可逆失败。六款LLM实验显示，即使前沿模型最多仅17%计划无错误，56%含潜在失败，多数导致不可逆后果。引入反事实模拟显式状态推理可降低潜在失败72%、不可逆情况75%，表明其提升LLM规划鲁棒性的潜力。
The Journal of Prompt-Engineered (Moral) Philosophy Or: Why AI-Assisted Ethics Research Requires Process Transparency
Authors: Michele Loi
First: 2025-11-10T08:56:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:50:54+00:00
Comments: 21 pages Transparency material documenting LLM usage available at: https://github.com/MicheleLoi/JPEP/tree/main/transparency/Canonical_MD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing AI disclosure mandates in scholarship require that AI assistance be reported but leave transparency philosophically unspecified: they fix the duty without explaining what the duty serves. We argue that ethical inquiry is essentially contested at two independent levels -- about what it is, and about what it demands of the inquirer -- defeating output-only evaluation and welfare-economic dismissal of the transparency question, and, by extension, reproducibility framings imported from the empirical sciences. The transparency duty is grounded instead in agent-integrity: the legibility, before a community of inquiry, of the identity-constituting commitments that the author's mode of philosophising expresses. Because the standards for evaluating such work are not communally settled, the achievable goal for transparency is not evaluation against agreed criteria but tracking -- accumulating the evidentiary record that lets each tradition assess the work on its own terms and makes future normative judgments possible. We develop a documentation-adequacy framework that operationalises Meaningful Human Control through five transparency elements -- declaration, navigation, documentation account, process documentation, and development records -- demonstrated by the paper itself, whose full documentation record is archived at a persistent identifier. The framework is a first iteration subject to revision, not a settled standard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提示工程（道德）哲学期刊或：为何AI辅助伦理研究需要过程透明
现有学术中关于AI披露的规定要求报告AI辅助，但未在哲学层面明确透明性的内涵：它们规定了义务，却未说明该义务的意义。我们认为，伦理探究本质上在两个独立层面存在争议——关于其本质，以及对探究者的具体要求——这否定了仅以输出评估或福利经济学视角否定透明性问题的做法，也否定了从实证科学中引入的可重复性框架。透明义务的基础在于主体完整性：作者哲学思辨方式所体现的身份构成承诺，需在学术共同体面前清晰可辨。由于此类工作的评价标准尚未达成共识，透明性的目标并非依据既定标准进行评估，而是追踪——积累证据记录，使各传统能按自身标准评估该工作，并为未来规范性判断提供可能。我们构建了一个文档充分性框架，通过五个透明要素——声明、导航、文档说明、过程记录与开发记录——实现“有意义的人类控制”，本文自身即为此框架的示范，其完整文档记录已存档于持久标识符。该框架为首次迭代，尚待修订，非最终标准。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that AI-assisted ethics research requires process transparency not merely for reproducibility or evaluation, but to uphold agent-integrity — the ability of authors to demonstrate their philosophical commitments to a scholarly community. The authors propose a documentation-adequacy framework grounded in 'Meaningful Human Control,' comprising five elements: declaration, navigation, documentation account, process documentation, and development records. Applied to the paper itself, this framework enables traditions to assess the work according to their own norms and supports future normative judgments. The framework is presented as a first iteration, not a fixed standard, and its implementation is demonstrated through the paper’s own archived transparency materials.
本文主张，AI辅助的伦理学研究需要过程透明，其目的并非为了可重复性或评估，而是为了维护作者的主体完整性——即其哲学立场在学术共同体中的可理解性。作者提出一个基于‘有意义的人类控制’的文档完备性框架，包含五个要素：声明、导航、文档说明、过程记录与开发记录。该框架应用于本文自身，展示了如何通过透明化使各哲学传统能按其自身标准评估研究，并支持未来规范性判断，而不依赖外部统一的评估标准。
StreamMemBench: Streaming Evaluation of Agent Memory for Future-Oriented Assistance
Authors: Guanming Liu, Yuqi Ren, Hansu Gu, Peng Zhang, Weihang Wang, Jiahao Liu, Ning Gu, Tun Lu
First: 2026-06-12T15:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
A central role of personal-agent memory is to turn stored information and prior interactions into future-oriented assistance. In daily use, useful cues come from what the agent observes and how the user interacts with the agent, and the agent must carry them forward from the current request to similar future tasks. Existing memory benchmarks usually test dialogue recall or task improvement in isolation, leaving the trajectory from streaming observations to later assistance largely untested. We introduce StreamMemBench, a streaming benchmark that constructs a two-step task sequence around each evidence anchor from EgoLife egocentric streams. The initial task tests evidence use, while the follow-up task tests whether feedback and interaction experience are reused. Four metrics diagnose evidence recall, initial evidence use, feedback incorporation, and follow-up reuse. Experiments with eight memory systems across two backbones show that current systems often fail to use observed evidence or turn feedback into reliable follow-up behavior, even when evidence is stored or feedback is incorporated locally. StreamMemBench is publicly available at https://github.com/landian60/StreamMemBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StreamMemBench：面向未来辅助的代理记忆流式评估
个人代理记忆的核心作用是将存储的信息和过往交互转化为面向未来的辅助。在日常使用中，有用的线索来自代理的观察和用户与代理的交互方式，代理必须将这些线索从当前请求延续到相似的未来任务。现有记忆基准测试通常孤立地评估对话回忆或任务改进，而对从流式观察到后续辅助的轨迹测试几乎未被覆盖。我们引入StreamMemBench，这是一个流式基准，围绕EgoLife第一人称流中的每个证据锚点构建两步任务序列。初始任务测试证据使用，后续任务测试反馈和交互经验是否被重用。四个指标诊断证据回忆、初始证据使用、反馈整合和后续重用。在两个骨干架构上对八种记忆系统进行的实验表明，当前系统即使在证据已存储或反馈已局部整合的情况下，也常无法有效使用观察到的证据或将反馈转化为可靠的后续行为。StreamMemBench公开获取地址：https://github.com/landian60/StreamMemBench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate how agents retain and reuse contextual cues from streaming observations for future assistance, this paper introduces StreamMemBench, a streaming benchmark that constructs two-step task sequences around evidence anchors from egocentric video streams. The first task assesses evidence recall and use, while the second evaluates whether feedback and interaction experiences are reused in subsequent tasks. Four metrics measure evidence recall, initial use, feedback incorporation, and follow-up reuse. Experiments across eight memory systems on two backbones reveal that most systems fail to effectively leverage stored evidence or translate feedback into consistent future behavior, even when local storage or incorporation mechanisms are present.
为评估代理如何从流式观察中保留并重用上下文线索以提供未来协助，本文提出StreamMemBench，一个基于第一人称视频流构建的流式基准测试。该方法围绕每个证据锚点设计两步任务序列：第一步测试证据使用，第二步测试反馈与交互经验是否被重用。四个指标分别评估证据召回、初始使用、反馈整合与后续重用。在两个骨干模型上对八种记忆系统进行的实验表明，多数系统即使能存储证据或本地整合反馈，仍难以有效利用这些信息指导后续行为。
EqCollide: Equivariant and Collision-Aware Deformable Objects Neural Simulator
Authors: Qianyi Chen, Tianrun Gao, Chenbo Jiang, Tailin Wu
First: 2025-06-06T06:49:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:48:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Simulating collisions of deformable objects is a fundamental yet challenging task due to the complexity of modeling solid mechanics and multi-body interactions. Existing data-driven methods often suffer from lack of equivariance to physical symmetries, inadequate handling of collisions, and limited scalability. Here we introduce \name, the first end-to-end equivariant neural fields simulator for deformable objects and their collisions. We propose an equivariant encoder to map object geometry and velocity into latent control points. A subsequent equivariant Graph Neural Network-based Neural Ordinary Differential Equation models the interactions among control points via collision-aware message passing. To reconstruct velocity fields, we query a neural field conditioned on control point features, enabling continuous and resolution-independent motion predictions. Experimental results on 2D and 3D scenarios show that \name achieves accurate, stable, and scalable simulations across diverse object configurations. It achieves $24.34\%$ to $57.62\%$ lower rollout MSE, even compared with the best-performing baseline model. Furthermore, \name could generalize to more colliding objects and extended temporal horizons, and stay robust to input transformed with group action. Code is available at: https://github.com/AI4Science-WestlakeU/EqCollide
中文标题/摘要
标题：EqCollide：等变且碰撞感知的可变形物体神经模拟器
模拟可变形物体的碰撞是一项基础但具有挑战性的任务，因为需要建模固体力学和多体交互的复杂性。现有数据驱动方法常因缺乏对物理对称性的等变性、碰撞处理不足及可扩展性有限而受限。本文提出\name，这是首个端到端等变神经场模拟器，用于可变形物体及其碰撞。我们提出等变编码器，将物体几何与速度映射为潜在控制点；随后采用基于等变图神经网络的神经常微分方程，通过碰撞感知的消息传递建模控制点间交互。为重建速度场，我们查询由控制点特征条件化的神经场，实现连续且分辨率无关的运动预测。在2D和3D场景的实验表明，\name在多样物体配置下实现精确、稳定且可扩展的模拟，其 rollout MSE 降低 24.34% 至 57.62%，甚至优于最佳基线模型。此外，\name可泛化至更多碰撞物体与更长时域，并对群作用变换的输入保持鲁棒性。代码见：https://github.com/AI4Science-WestlakeU/EqCollide
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for physically accurate and scalable simulation of deformable object collisions, this work introduces EqCollide, the first end-to-end equivariant neural field simulator. It employs an equivariant encoder to map object geometry and velocity into latent control points, then uses an equivariant Graph Neural Network-based Neural ODE to model interactions via collision-aware message passing. A neural field conditioned on control point features reconstructs velocity fields for continuous motion prediction. Experiments on 2D and 3D scenarios demonstrate that EqCollide achieves significantly lower rollout MSE—24.34% to 57.62% improvement over the best baseline—while maintaining stability and scalability across diverse object configurations and temporal horizons, and showing robustness under group-transformed inputs.
为实现对可变形物体碰撞的物理准确且可扩展的模拟，本文提出EqCollide，首个端到端的等变神经场模拟器。该方法通过等变编码器将物体几何与速度映射为潜在控制点，再利用基于等变图神经网络的神经常微分方程建模碰撞感知的消息传递交互。通过条件神经场重建速度场，实现连续且分辨率无关的运动预测。在2D和3D场景实验中，EqCollide相较最优基线模型降低24.34%至57.62%的 rollout MSE，同时保持稳定、可扩展性及对物理变换的鲁棒性。
Regional Climate Model Emulation with Diffusion Approaches: What is the Added Value of Generative Machine Learning?
Authors: Mikel N. Legasa, Antoine Doury, Achille Gellens, Redouane Lguensat, Clara Naldesi, Soulivanh Thao, Mathieu Vrac
First: 2026-06-12T15:46:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:46:35+00:00
Comments: Submitted to Journal of Advances in Modeling Earth Systems (JAMES)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Emulators provide a cost-effective alternative to regional climate models (RCMs) by capturing their dynamical downscaling function. They link large-scale predictors simulated by global climate models (GCMs) to RCM-simulated high-resolution fields of the target variable, here precipitation. Machine learning methods, typically deep learning, are cheaper than running RCMs in computation time and energy. Among them, generative models are appealing because they can simulate ensembles of local high-resolution fields consistent with the predictors. This ensemble, which we call the uncertainty envelope, remains to be properly assessed for added value. Here, we make three contributions. First, we introduce ParamDiffusion, a new two-stage diffusion-based framework, and compare it with a state-of-the-art diffusion approach. Second, we expand standard validation through a comprehensive framework aligned with climate-science needs, examining specific precipitation events, including extremes. Third, within this framework, we assess the added value of diffusion approaches relative to deterministic methods. We intercompare four deep-learning models: a deterministic model designed to capture the precipitation tail; a parametric probabilistic model based on it; a recently proposed diffusion approach; and ParamDiffusion, which couples the parametric model with a diffusion model. Our results show that diffusion-based approaches reproduce climatological precipitation statistics with high skill, including distributional tails and spatially compounded extremes, while generating spatially detailed fields. However, none of the assessed models consistently accounts for the most extreme RCM-simulated events within its uncertainty envelope. Diffusion models are therefore promising for probabilistic RCM emulation, but progress is still required before they can reliably represent high-impact precipitation extremes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用扩散方法进行区域气候模型仿真：生成式机器学习的附加价值是什么？
仿真器通过捕捉区域气候模型（RCM）的动力学降尺度功能，提供了一种成本效益更高的替代方案。它们将全球气候模型（GCM）模拟的大尺度预测变量与RCM模拟的目标变量（此处为降水）的高分辨率场相联系。机器学习方法（通常为深度学习）在计算时间和能耗上比运行RCM更便宜。其中，生成式模型因其能模拟与预测变量一致的局部高分辨率场集合而具有吸引力。该集合（我们称之为不确定性包络）的附加价值仍需进一步评估。本文有三项贡献：首先，我们引入了新的两阶段扩散框架ParamDiffusion，并与当前最先进的扩散方法进行比较；其次，我们通过一个全面的框架扩展了标准验证，以满足气候科学需求，考察特定降水事件（包括极端事件）；第三，在此框架下，我们评估了扩散方法相对于确定性方法的附加价值。我们比较了四种深度学习模型：一种旨在捕捉降水尾部的确定性模型；一种基于该模型的参数化概率模型；一种近期提出的扩散方法；以及ParamDiffusion，它将参数化模型与扩散模型相结合。结果表明，基于扩散的方法在再现气候学降水统计方面表现出高技能，包括分布尾部和空间复合极端事件，同时生成空间细节丰富的场。然而，所评估的模型均未能始终如一地在不确定性包络内涵盖RCM模拟的最极端事件。因此，扩散模型在概率性RCM仿真方面具有前景，但在可靠表征高影响降水极端事件方面仍需进一步进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for computationally efficient alternatives to regional climate models (RCMs), this study explores diffusion-based generative machine learning for probabilistic emulation of high-resolution precipitation fields. It introduces ParamDiffusion, a two-stage diffusion framework, and compares it with other deep-learning models—including a deterministic tail-capturing model, a parametric probabilistic model, and a state-of-the-art diffusion method—using a climate-science-aligned validation framework that includes extreme precipitation events. Results show that diffusion models effectively reproduce climatological precipitation statistics and spatially detailed fields, including distributional tails and compound extremes, but none consistently captures the most extreme RCM-simulated events within their uncertainty envelopes, indicating potential for probabilistic emulation while highlighting remaining limitations for high-impact extremes.
为降低区域气候模型（RCM）的计算成本，本研究提出ParamDiffusion这一两阶段扩散框架，用于从全球气候模型预测变量生成高分辨率降水场。研究将扩散模型与确定性及概率性深度学习模型对比，采用符合气候科学需求的验证框架，包含极端降水事件。结果表明，扩散模型在重现气候统计量和空间复合极端事件方面表现优异，但无一模型能稳定涵盖RCM模拟的最极端事件于其不确定性包络内，说明其在概率性模拟中具潜力，但尚需改进以可靠表征高影响降水极端事件。
Trimodal Glioma Representation Alignment via Volumetric Contrastive Learning
Authors: Denise Marini, Eleonora Grassucci, Danilo Comminiello
First: 2026-06-12T15:45:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:45:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Glioma grading and survival prediction require the integration of heterogeneous information collected at different spatial and biological scales. Histopathology describes tissue morphology, mRNA expression captures molecular activity, and magnetic resonance imaging provides a non-invasive view of tumor extent and radiological heterogeneity. Existing glioma prognosis models often combine only two of these sources, while their alignment objectives remain mostly pairwise. This paper introduces GLORIA, a novel trimodal framework for GLioma Omics - Radiology - hIstopathology Alignment. GLORIA processes whole-slide image regions, gene-expression profiles, and 3D MRI volumes through modality-specific encoders, projects them into a shared latent space, and aligns them with a Gramian contrastive loss that measures the volume spanned by the three modality embeddings. The aligned representations are fused through a cross-modal gating module and optimized jointly for three-class glioma grading and overall survival prediction. We evaluate GLORIA on a matched TCGA-GBM/LGG and BraTS21 cohort, comprising 132 patients with all three modalities. On the shared trimodal test set, GLORIA improves over the bimodal WSI-mRNA baseline in all the metrics considered.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过体积对比学习实现三模态胶质瘤表征对齐
胶质瘤分级与生存预测需要整合在不同空间和生物学尺度上收集的异质性信息。组织病理学描述组织形态，mRNA表达捕捉分子活动，磁共振成像提供肿瘤范围和影像异质性的无创视图。现有胶质瘤预后模型通常仅结合其中两种来源，其对齐目标多为成对进行。本文提出GLORIA，一种用于胶质瘤组学-影像-病理学对齐的新型三模态框架。GLORIA通过模态特异性编码器处理全切片图像区域、基因表达谱和3D MRI体积，将其投影至共享潜在空间，并利用Gramian对比损失对三模态嵌入所张成的体积进行对齐。对齐后的表征通过跨模态门控模块融合，并联合优化用于三类胶质瘤分级和总体生存预测。我们在包含132名三模态数据患者的TCGA-GBM/LGG与BraTS21匹配队列上评估GLORIA，在共享三模态测试集上，GLORIA在所有评估指标上均优于双模态WSI-mRNA基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to integrate histopathology, mRNA expression, and MRI data for accurate glioma prognosis, this study proposes GLORIA, a trimodal framework that aligns three heterogeneous data types using volumetric contrastive learning. It encodes whole-slide images, gene expression profiles, and 3D MRI volumes into a shared latent space via modality-specific encoders, then aligns them using a Gramian contrastive loss that measures the volume spanned by the embeddings. The aligned representations are fused through a cross-modal gating module and jointly optimized for glioma grading and survival prediction. Evaluated on 132 patients with matched tri-modal data, GLORIA outperforms bimodal baselines across all metrics.
为提升胶质瘤预后预测的准确性，本研究提出GLORIA框架，通过体素级对比学习对组织病理图像、mRNA表达谱和3D MRI数据进行三模态对齐。该框架使用模态专属编码器分别处理三类数据，将其映射至共享潜在空间，并通过Gramian对比损失衡量三类嵌入所张成的体积以实现对齐。融合后的表示通过跨模态门控模块联合优化，用于三分类胶质瘤分级与生存期预测。在包含132名三模态匹配患者的测试集上，GLORIA在所有评估指标上均优于双模态基线模型。
CANN-EUCLID: unsupervised constitutive artificial neural network model discovery from full-field data
Authors: Benjamin Alheit, Siddhant Kumar, Mathias Peirlinck
First: 2026-06-12T15:44:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:44:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) provide interpretable material model discovery, but have so far been used in stress-supervised settings based on apparent stress-strain data from homogeneous tests. Because each test samples only a narrow loading path and provides homogenized rather than local stress information, robust discovery typically requires multiple loading modes to constrain the multidimensional response. This is challenging for soft biological tissues, where repeated testing, damage, and sample variability limit reliable information from a single specimen. Here, we combine CANNs with the stress-unsupervised full-field discovery framework EUCLID to identify sparse hyperelastic laws directly from displacement fields and reaction forces in one heterogeneity-inducing loading case. CANN-EUCLID minimizes equilibrium imbalance with sparsity-promoting regularization selecting compact active terms, without local stress measurements or a prescribed law. We evaluate the approach on isotropic and anisotropic benchmarks with prescribed ground-truth laws. When the ground truth is representable by the chosen CANN basis, our method recovers the correct terms with near-exact accuracy, including exponential terms with embedded parameters. When it is not contained in the basis, the method retains shared terms and approximates missing contributions using available basis functions. Generalization depends strongly on sampled deformation states: exponential strain-stiffening terms can be recovered accurately when sufficiently probed, but can produce large extrapolation errors when the stiffening regime lies outside the sampled domain. Forward FE validation simulations show that the discovered behavior accurately replicates the ground truth. These results establish stress-unsupervised CANN discovery as a promising framework for interpretable full-field constitutive model identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CANN-EUCLID：从全场数据中无监督发现本构人工神经网络模型
本构人工神经网络（CANNs）可提供可解释的材料模型发现，但此前仅用于基于均匀试验所得表观应力-应变数据的应力监督场景。由于每次试验仅采样狭窄的加载路径且提供的是均质而非局部应力信息，稳健发现通常需多种加载模式以约束多维响应。这对软生物组织而言颇具挑战，因重复测试、损伤及样本差异限制了单个样本提供可靠信息。本文将CANNs与无监督全场发现框架EUCLID结合，仅通过一个诱导异质性的加载案例中的位移场和反作用力，直接识别稀疏的超弹性定律。CANN-EUCLID通过最小化平衡不平衡并结合稀疏性正则化选择紧凑的活跃项，无需局部应力测量或预设定律。我们在具有预设真实定律的各向同性和各向异性基准上评估该方法。当真实定律可由所选CANN基函数表示时，该方法可近似精确恢复正确项，包括含嵌入参数的指数项；当真实定律不在基函数范围内时，方法保留共享项并利用现有基函数近似缺失贡献。泛化能力高度依赖采样变形状态：当充分采样时，指数应变硬化项可被准确恢复，但若硬化区域超出采样域，则会产生较大外推误差。正向有限元验证模拟表明，所发现行为能准确复现真实定律。这些结果确立了无监督应力CANN发现作为可解释全场本构模型识别的有前景框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable material models without stress supervision, especially for soft biological tissues with limited testing capacity, this work combines constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) with the EUCLID framework to discover sparse hyperelastic laws from full-field displacement and reaction force data in a single loading case. The method minimizes equilibrium imbalance with sparsity-promoting regularization to select compact active terms, avoiding local stress measurements or predefined constitutive forms. Experimental results on isotropic and anisotropic benchmarks show that when the ground truth is within the CANN basis, the model recovers terms with near-exact accuracy, including exponential functions with embedded parameters; when not, it retains shared terms and approximates missing contributions. Generalization is highly dependent on deformation sampling, with accurate recovery of strain-stiffening terms only when sufficiently probed, and large extrapolation errors when stiffening occurs outside the sampled domain. Forward finite element validation confirms the discovered models accurately replicate ground truth behavior.
为解决软生物组织因难以重复测试而无法获取充分应力数据的问题，本研究提出CANN-EUCLID方法，结合构型人工神经网络与EUCLID框架，从单次异质加载下的位移场和反作用力中无监督发现稀疏的超弹性本构律。该方法通过最小化平衡不平衡并施加稀疏正则化，选择紧凑的活跃项，无需局部应力测量或预设本构形式。在各向同性和各向异性基准测试中，当真实本构可被所选CANN基表示时，方法能精确恢复含嵌入参数的指数项；若不可表示，则保留共享项并用现有基函数近似缺失贡献。结果表明，恢复精度高度依赖采样形变范围：若指数形变硬化区域被充分采样，可准确恢复；若超出采样域则产生较大外推误差。正向有限元验证显示，发现的本构行为能精确复现真实本构。
NEST3D: A High-Resolution Multimodal Dataset of Sociable Weaver Tree Nests
Authors: Constanza A. Molina Catricheo, Simon Boeder, Ting-Jia Guo, Giacomo May, Clément Berthelot, Devis Tuia, Friedrich Fedor Reinhard, Fabio Remondino, Benjamin Risse
First: 2026-06-12T15:39:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:39:18+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures. Dataset available at https://huggingface.co/NEST3D
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Sociable weaver nests function as complex ecological structures offering thermoregulatory microhabitats and sustaining diverse species; however, datasets used in prior studies lack fine-grained 3D structural detail. Producing usable and accurate 3D weaver nest data is challenging due to their irregular geometry and integration with complex host vegetation. We bridge this gap with an open-access, 1.4 TB multimodal drone dataset of 104 nest-bearing trees, comprising 27,945 RGB images, 111,780 multispectral images, approximately 781 million 3D points, and expert-annotated semantic segmentation labels. We benchmark semantic segmentation using KPConv, RandLA-Net, and Point Transformer V3, with PT-v3 achieving an mIoU of 86.35% on the test set. While the results demonstrate strong performance for transformer-based and point-wise methods, they also highlight architecture-dependent challenges, particularly for convolution-based approaches such as KPConv. By uniquely combining spectral, spatial, and structural information, the presented dataset advances 3D reconstruction, segmentation, and classification algorithms, enabling ecological applications from nest volume estimation to species conservation, and serves as a demanding benchmark that exposes architecture-dependent performance under extreme class imbalance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NEST3D：社会织巢鸟树巢的高分辨率多模态数据集
社会织巢鸟的巢穴作为复杂的生态结构，提供热调节微生境并支持多种物种；但以往研究使用的数据集缺乏精细的三维结构细节。由于其不规则几何形状及与复杂宿主植被的融合，生成可用且准确的三维织巢鸟巢数据颇具挑战。我们通过一个开放获取的1.4TB多模态无人机数据集填补这一空白，该数据集包含104棵筑巢树，涵盖27,945张RGB图像、111,780张多光谱图像、约7.81亿个三维点以及专家标注的语义分割标签。我们使用KPConv、RandLA-Net和Point Transformer V3对语义分割进行基准测试，其中PT-v3在测试集上达到86.35%的mIoU。结果表明，基于Transformer和点级方法表现优异，但也凸显了架构依赖性挑战，特别是卷积方法（如KPConv）的局限性。该数据集通过独特结合光谱、空间与结构信息，推动了三维重建、分割与分类算法的发展，支持从巢穴体积估算到物种保护的生态应用，并作为一项严苛基准，揭示了在极端类别不平衡下架构性能的差异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-resolution 3D data to study sociable weaver nests as complex ecological structures, this work introduces NEST3D, a multimodal drone dataset capturing 104 nest-bearing trees with 27,945 RGB images, 111,780 multispectral images, and 781 million 3D points, all annotated with semantic segmentation. The dataset addresses prior limitations in structural detail and vegetation integration. Experimental benchmarks using KPConv, RandLA-Net, and Point Transformer V3 show PT-v3 achieving 86.35% mIoU on the test set, demonstrating strong performance for transformer and point-based methods while revealing challenges for convolutional architectures under extreme class imbalance, thus advancing 3D ecological analysis and providing a rigorous benchmark.
为研究社会织巢鸟巢穴作为复杂生态结构所需的高分辨率三维数据，作者构建了NEST3D数据集，包含104棵带巢树木的27,945张RGB图像、111,780张多光谱图像及约7.81亿个3D点，并附带专家标注的语义分割标签。在评估KPConv、RandLA-Net和Point Transformer V3等模型时，PT-v3在测试集上达到86.35%的mIoU，而基于卷积的方法在极端类别不平衡下表现不佳。该数据集融合光谱、空间与结构信息，支持精确生态分析，同时作为对3D视觉模型架构依赖性的严苛基准。
ORCA: A Platform for Open-Source Dexterity Research
Authors: Francesco Capuano, Maximilian Eberlein, Fabrice Bourquin, Clemens Claudio Christoph
First: 2026-06-12T15:38:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:38:34+00:00
Comments: 15 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotics manipulation research increasingly focuses on two-finger parallel grippers for their effectiveness, affordability, and ease of teleoperation. Grippers are nonetheless limited by their form factor, often requiring bimanual setups even for simple reorientation tasks. Anthropomorphic hands are a more natural platform for dexterous robot learning -- closer to the human hand, and capable of learning from human video -- yet they remain hard to use in learning research: even where open and accessible hand hardware exists, the software for control, simulation, teleoperation, and retargeting is scattered in one-off code bases, and largely disconnected from the robot-learning ecosystem. In this work, we introduce the \orca~learning stack, an open-source research stack for dexterity as a first-class robot learning domain. Our \orca~stack unifies low-level control, simulation, teleoperation from a range of consumer platforms, and hand retargeting, behind a single interface, and integrates natively with popular robot-learning frameworks such as \lerobot, so dexterous hand researchers can leverage the same data, training, and evaluation pipelines used for non-dexterous robot learning. We demonstrate a complete end-to-end workflow, collecting expert demonstrations of an in-hand reorientation task by teleoperation with a consumer-grade VR headset, training an autonomous policy with \lerobot, and evaluating the learned policy in a fully reproducible and observable setup. We open-source the entire stack as a shared, reproducible foundation for dexterous-manipulation research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORCA：开源灵巧性研究平台
机器人操作研究日益聚焦于双指平行夹爪，因其高效、经济且易于遥操作。然而夹爪受限于其结构，即使执行简单重定向任务也常需双臂协同。仿人手作为灵巧机器人学习的更自然平台——更接近人类手部，且能从人类视频中学习——却在研究中难以使用：即便存在开源的仿人手硬件，其控制、仿真、遥操作和重定向软件分散于独立代码库，且与机器人学习生态体系脱节。本文提出ORCA学习栈，作为灵巧性研究的开源第一类机器人学习领域平台。ORCA栈统一了底层控制、多种消费级平台的仿真与遥操作、手部重定向功能，提供单一接口，并原生集成如Lerobot等流行机器人学习框架，使灵巧手研究者可复用非灵巧机器人学习的数据、训练与评估流程。我们演示了端到端工作流：通过消费级VR头显遥操作收集专家在手内重定向任务演示，用Lerobot训练自主策略，并在完全可复现且可观测的环境中评估所学策略。我们开源整个栈，作为灵巧操作研究的共享、可复现基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized, open-source tools to advance dexterous robot manipulation research, this work introduces ORCA, a unified software stack that integrates low-level control, simulation, teleoperation, and hand retargeting for anthropomorphic robotic hands. The method unifies disparate components — including support for consumer VR teleoperation and compatibility with popular robot learning frameworks like LERobot — into a single interface, enabling seamless data collection, training, and evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate a complete end-to-end workflow: expert demonstrations of in-hand reorientation tasks were collected via VR teleoperation, trained into an autonomous policy using LERobot, and evaluated in a fully reproducible setup, showcasing the stack’s effectiveness for dexterous manipulation research.
为推动灵巧机器人操作研究，本文提出ORCA平台，整合了低层控制、仿真、多平台远程操作及手部重定向功能，统一接口并无缝对接LERobot等主流机器人学习框架，使研究人员可复用现有数据、训练与评估流程。实验结果展示完整端到端流程：通过消费级VR设备采集专家手部重定向操作演示，使用LERobot训练自主策略，并在可复现环境中评估策略效果，验证了ORCA作为灵巧操作研究共享基础的有效性。
Free Heavy-Tailed Lunch for Muon: A Theoretical Justification of Empirical Success
Authors: Florian Hübler, Thomas Pethick, Suvrit Sra
First: 2026-06-12T15:37:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:37:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-Euclidean optimisation methods with matrix-valued updates, such as Muon and Scion, have recently shown strong empirical performance for training Transformer models, yet their theoretical advantages over Euclidean methods remain poorly understood. We address this gap in the heavy-tailed non-convex regime, where stochastic gradients have bounded $p$-th central moments, $p \in (1,2]$. We show that certain non-Euclidean methods achieve optimal sample complexity under stronger stationarity measures, while Euclidean methods incur additional dimension-dependent costs. As a consequence, for $m \times n$ matrices, Muon finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point in nuclear norm within $\mathcal{O}\left(\min\{m, n\} \frac{Δ_1 L}{\varepsilon^2} \left(\frac σ\varepsilon \right)^{\frac p {p-1}}\right)$ samples, absorbing heavy-tailed noise without extra dimension dependence, unlike Euclidean methods. We further prove this sample complexity, including its dimension dependence, is optimal for all first-order methods under nuclear-norm stationarity. Experiments on large language models support our theory. Surprisingly, our results suggest that other Schatten geometries beyond the spectral geometry of Muon can perform competitively in certain settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为缪子算法提供自由重尾午餐：对经验成功的理论论证
近期，矩阵值更新的非欧几里得优化方法（如缪子和Scion）在训练Transformer模型时表现出强大的实证性能，但其相对于欧几里得方法的理论优势尚不明确。我们填补了这一空白，研究了重尾非凸场景下的理论优势，其中随机梯度的p阶中心矩有界，p∈(1,2]。我们证明，在更强的平稳性度量下，某些非欧几里得方法可实现最优样本复杂度，而欧几里得方法则需承担额外的维度相关成本。因此，对于m×n矩阵，缪子算法可在核范数下找到ε-平稳点，所需样本数为O(min{m,n}·Δ₁L/ε²·(σ/ε)^(p/(p-1)))，无需额外维度依赖即可吸收重尾噪声，而欧几里得方法则无法做到。我们进一步证明，该样本复杂度（包括其维度依赖性）在核范数平稳性下对所有一阶方法均为最优。在大型语言模型上的实验支持了我们的理论。令人惊讶的是，我们的结果表明，在某些场景下，除缪子所基于的谱几何外，其他Schatten几何也可能具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of non-Euclidean optimizers like Muon in training Transformers despite limited theoretical understanding, this work analyzes their performance in heavy-tailed non-convex settings where stochastic gradients have bounded p-th central moments (p ∈ (1,2]). It demonstrates that Muon achieves optimal sample complexity under nuclear-norm stationarity, scaling with min{m,n} and avoiding dimension-dependent penalties, while Euclidean methods incur extra dimension costs. Theoretical bounds show Muon finds an ε-stationary point in nuclear norm within O(min{m,n} Δ₁L / ε² (σ/ε)^{p/(p−1)}) samples, and this complexity is proven optimal for all first-order methods under the same stationarity measure. Experiments on large language models validate the theory, and the results indicate that other Schatten geometries may also perform well in specific scenarios.
为了解释Muon等非欧几里得优化器在训练Transformer模型中表现优异但理论基础薄弱的问题，本文在具有有界p阶中心矩（p∈(1,2]）的重尾非凸场景下，证明Muon在核范数平稳性条件下可达到最优样本复杂度，其复杂度与min{m,n}成正比且不随维度增加而恶化，而欧氏方法则需承担额外维度代价。理论分析表明该复杂度对所有一阶方法在核范数平稳性下均最优，实验在大语言模型上验证了该结论，且发现其他Schatten几何也可能在特定场景下表现优异。
Visual Quality Score Assessment of Large White Goods in Remanufacture with Multi-View Deformable-DETR
Authors: Paul Koch, Vivek Chavan
First: 2026-06-12T15:35:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:35:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted to GCSM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Remanufacturing large white goods is essential for a circular economy, yet visual quality assessment remains a manual bottleneck for training and pricing. Conventional detection methods require extensive annotation and struggle with small defects in high-resolution multi-view data. We present a multi-view framework based on Deformable-DETR for automated quality scoring that aggregates information across redundant views to extract fine-grained features. To enhance robustness with limited labels, we employ self-supervised pretraining followed by supervised fine-tuning on expert-annotated scores. Additionally, a linear projection over frozen feature maps identifies regions of interest to explain model decisions. Evaluated on an industrial multi-view dataset, our approach delivers precise quality assessments while reducing reliance on manual annotation and per-part customization, enabling scalable and transparent inspection for remanufacturing lines.

大件白色家电的再制造对循环经济至关重要，但视觉质量评估仍依赖人工，成为训练与定价的瓶颈。传统检测方法需大量标注且难以处理高分辨率多视角数据中的微小缺陷。本文提出基于可变形DETR的多视角框架，自动评分并跨冗余视角聚合信息以提取细粒度特征。为在标注有限情况下提升鲁棒性，采用自监督预训练后结合专家标注评分进行监督微调。此外，通过冻结特征图上的线性投影识别感兴趣区域以解释模型决策。在工业多视角数据集上的评估表明，该方法可实现精准质量评估，减少对人工标注和部件定制的依赖，支持再制造产线的规模化与透明化检测。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to automate visual quality assessment in remanufacturing large white goods to support circular economy goals, this work introduces a multi-view Deformable-DETR framework that aggregates fine-grained features across redundant camera views to detect and score defects. To address limited labeled data, it combines self-supervised pretraining with supervised fine-tuning using expert annotations. Experimental results on an industrial multi-view dataset show the method achieves precise quality scoring with reduced manual annotation dependency and no per-part customization, enabling scalable and interpretable inspection for remanufacturing lines.
为解决大型白色家电再制造中视觉质量评估依赖人工的瓶颈问题，本文提出一种基于多视角Deformable-DETR的框架，通过聚合冗余视角中的细粒度特征实现缺陷检测与评分。该方法采用自监督预训练结合专家标注数据的监督微调，提升在标注数据有限情况下的鲁棒性。在工业多视角数据集上的实验表明，该方法可实现精准的质量评分，减少人工标注依赖和部件定制需求，支持再制造产线的规模化与可解释性检测。
Rethinking Global Average Pooling: Your Classifier Is Secretly a Multi-Instance Learner
Authors: Aray Karjauv
First: 2026-06-12T15:35:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:35:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern image classifiers widely adopt global average pooling (GAP) followed by a linear classification head. This linearity ensures that the image-level logits equal the average of logits obtained by applying the classification head pointwise to the feature grid prior to GAP. Consequently, standard classifiers may inherently retain spatial class evidence that remains recoverable even when the image-level prediction is incorrect. This structure naturally suggests a multiple-instance learning (MIL) interpretation, where an image is viewed as a bag of spatial instances. Within this formulation, we demonstrate that standard classifiers trained with a single label per image can still learn the intended classification task in multi-object scenes. We further exploit this property to decompose image-level logits into a prediction grid, providing a post-hoc diagnostic to extract spatial class evidence that GAP otherwise obscures. Our systematic evaluation reveals that off-the-shelf models consistently recover the ground-truth class within foreground regions. The MIL interpretation further suggests that common classifier failures reflect known limitations of mean aggregation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考全局平均池化：你的分类器本质上是一个多实例学习器
现代图像分类器广泛采用全局平均池化（GAP）后接线性分类头。这种线性结构确保图像级logits等于在GAP前对特征网格逐点应用分类头得到的logits的平均值。因此，标准分类器可能隐含保留了即使图像级预测错误仍可恢复的空间类别证据。该结构自然暗示了多实例学习（MIL）的解释，即图像被视为空间实例的集合。在此框架下，我们证明标准分类器即使在单标签图像上训练，仍能在多物体场景中学习预期的分类任务。我们进一步利用这一特性将图像级logits分解为预测网格，提供事后诊断工具以提取GAP掩盖的空间类别证据。系统评估表明，现成模型在前景区域始终能恢复真实类别。MIL解释进一步表明，常见分类器失败反映了均值聚合的已知局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that global average pooling (GAP) in image classifiers inherently preserves spatial class evidence despite producing image-level predictions, this work reinterprets standard classifiers as multi-instance learners, where each image is treated as a bag of spatial instances. The authors demonstrate that even with single-label supervision, these models can learn to classify multi-object scenes correctly, and they propose a post-hoc method to decompose image logits into a spatial prediction grid, revealing where true class evidence lies. Experimental results show that off-the-shelf models consistently recover ground-truth classes within foreground regions, supporting the view that classifier failures stem from the limitations of mean aggregation rather than model incapability.
该研究受全球平均池化（GAP）在图像分类器中隐含保留空间类别证据的启发，将标准分类器重新诠释为多实例学习器，其中每张图像被视为一组空间实例。作者证明，即使在单标签监督下，这些模型仍能正确分类多物体场景，并利用该多实例结构将图像级logits分解为空间预测网格，从而实现对被GAP掩盖的类别证据的后验提取。实验结果表明，现成模型在前景区域始终能恢复真实类别，说明分类器失败源于均值聚合的固有局限而非模型能力不足。
Tackling GNARLy Problems: Graph Neural Algorithmic Reasoning Reimagined through Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Alex Schutz, Victor-Alexandru Darvariu, Efimia Panagiotaki, Bruno Lacerda, Nick Hawes
First: 2025-09-23T12:49:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:31:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural algorithmic reasoning (NAR) is a paradigm that trains neural networks to execute classic algorithms by supervised learning. Despite its successes, important limitations remain: inability to construct valid solutions without post-processing and to reason about multiple correct ones, poor performance on combinatorial NP-hard problems, and inapplicability to problems for which strong algorithms are not yet known. To address these limitations, we reframe the problem of learning algorithm trajectories as a Markov decision process, which imposes structure on the solution construction procedure and unlocks the powerful tools of imitation and reinforcement learning (RL). We propose the GNARL framework, encompassing the methodology to translate problem formulations from NAR to RL and a learning architecture suitable for a wide range of graph-based problems. We achieve very high graph accuracy results on several CLRS-30 problems, performance matching or exceeding much narrower NAR approaches for NP-hard problems and, remarkably, applicability even when lacking an expert algorithm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：攻克棘手问题：通过强化学习重新构想图神经算法推理
神经算法推理（NAR）是一种通过监督学习训练神经网络执行经典算法的范式。尽管取得成功，但仍存在重要局限：无法在后处理前构造有效解、难以推理多个正确解、在组合NP难问题上表现不佳，且无法应用于尚无强算法的问题。为解决这些局限，我们将学习算法轨迹的问题重新框架为马尔可夫决策过程，从而赋予解构造过程结构并解锁模仿学习与强化学习（RL）的强大工具。我们提出GNARL框架，涵盖从NAR到RL的问题表述转换方法及适用于多种图问题的学习架构。我们在多个CLRS-30问题上实现了极高的图准确性，在NP难问题上表现匹配或超越狭窄的NAR方法，且令人惊讶的是，即使缺乏专家算法也能适用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of neural algorithmic reasoning—such as reliance on post-processing, poor handling of NP-hard problems, and lack of applicability to unsolved algorithmic domains—the paper redefines algorithm learning as a Markov decision process to enable structured, reinforcement learning-driven solution construction. The GNARL framework translates graph-based problems into RL tasks and employs a tailored architecture to learn algorithmic trajectories. Experimental results show high accuracy on CLRS-30 problems, performance matching or surpassing narrow NAR methods on NP-hard problems, and notable success even in the absence of known expert algorithms.
为解决神经算法推理在无后处理、NP难问题表现差及缺乏专家算法时无法应用等局限，作者将算法学习重构为马尔可夫决策过程，引入强化学习。GNARL框架将图类问题转化为RL任务并设计适配架构以构建解。实验表明，GNARL在CLRS-30问题上准确率高，对NP难问题表现不逊于窄域NAR方法，且在无专家算法时仍能有效应用。
TRACE: Trajectory-Routed Causal Memory for Delayed-Evidence Visuomotor Imitation
Authors: Zihao Li, Ranpeng Qiu, Yincong Chen, Guoqiang Ren, Weiming Zhi
First: 2026-06-12T15:30:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:30:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robots under autonomous operation may require decisions based on evidence that is no longer visible. We study \emph{delayed-evidence} tasks, where an early cue disappears before a later decision point, so visually similar observations can require different actions. In these settings, the current observation is not a sufficient state for control. We introduce TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence (TRACE), a memory framework for visuomotor imitation policies. TRACE stores task-relevant visual and robot-state evidence, such as object identity, target choice, or route-dependent state, in a fixed-size latent memory that remains bounded over long episodes. Instead of indexing memory by raw time or manually provided task labels, TRACE uses \emph{path signatures}: compact, order-sensitive features of the executed robot-state trajectory. These signatures do not store the visual cue itself; rather, they provide trajectory-conditioned keys for writing and retrieving the evidence stored when the cue was visible. When the robot later reaches an ambiguous observation, the policy conditions on TRACE memory to recover the missing context and choose the correct branch. TRACE attaches through lightweight adapters to policies, without changing the policy backbone, action head, or imitation objective. Across real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks with visually ambiguous branch points, TRACE improves branch selection and task success over alternative baselines, including short-history and recurrent memory. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/trace
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRACE：用于延迟证据视觉运动模仿的轨迹路由因果记忆
在自主运行中，机器人可能需要基于已不可见的证据做出决策。我们研究“延迟证据”任务，其中早期提示在后期决策点前消失，导致视觉上相似的观察可能需要不同的动作。在这种情况下，当前观察不足以作为控制状态。我们提出TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence（TRACE），一种用于视觉运动模仿策略的记忆框架。TRACE在固定大小的潜在记忆中存储任务相关的视觉和机器人状态证据（如物体身份、目标选择或路径依赖状态），并在长时序任务中保持记忆有界。TRACE不按原始时间或人工提供的任务标签索引记忆，而是使用“路径签名”——执行机器人状态轨迹的紧凑、顺序敏感特征。这些签名不存储视觉提示本身，而是提供轨迹条件下的键值，用于写入和检索提示可见时存储的证据。当机器人后续遇到模糊观察时，策略根据TRACE记忆恢复缺失上下文并选择正确分支。TRACE通过轻量级适配器连接到策略，无需修改策略主干、动作头或模仿目标。在具有视觉模糊分支点的真实长时序操作任务中，TRACE在分支选择和任务成功率方面优于其他基线方法（包括短历史和循环记忆方法）。项目页面：https://jeong-zju.github.io/trace
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of robots making decisions based on visual cues that disappear before action, this work introduces TRACE, a memory framework for visuomotor imitation that stores task-relevant visual and state evidence in a fixed-size latent memory. Instead of relying on time or manual labels, TRACE uses path signatures—compact, order-sensitive features of robot trajectories—to conditionally index and retrieve evidence when cues are no longer visible. Experiments on real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks with ambiguous visual branches show that TRACE significantly improves branch selection accuracy and task success compared to baselines using short-history or recurrent memory, without modifying the policy architecture or imitation objective.
为解决机器人在视觉线索消失后仍需做出正确决策的问题，本文提出TRACE框架，通过固定大小的潜在记忆存储任务相关的视觉与机器人状态证据。该框架不依赖时间戳或人工标签，而是利用机器人轨迹的路径签名（顺序敏感的紧凑特征）作为索引，实现线索消失后对上下文的恢复与正确动作选择。在具有视觉相似但语义不同的分支点的真实长时程操作任务中，TRACE显著优于短历史与循环记忆基线，提升了分支选择准确率与任务成功率。
From Sorting Algorithms to Scalable Kernels: Bayesian Optimization in High-Dimensional Permutation Spaces
Authors: Zikai Xie, Linjiang Chen
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-07-17T16:12:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:30:09+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, published on ICLR-26
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian Optimization (BO) is a powerful tool for black-box optimization, but its application to high-dimensional permutation spaces is severely limited by the challenge of defining scalable representations. The current state-of-the-art BO approach for permutation spaces relies on an exhaustive $Ω(n^2)$ pairwise comparison, inducing a dense representation that is impractical for large-scale permutations. To break this barrier, we introduce a novel framework for generating efficient permutation representations via kernel functions derived from sorting algorithms. Within this framework, the Mallows kernel can be viewed as a special instance derived from enumeration sort. Further, we introduce the \textbf{Merge Kernel} , which leverages the divide-and-conquer structure of merge sort to produce a compact, $Θ(n\log n)$ to achieve the lowest possible complexity with no information loss and effectively capture permutation structure. Our central thesis is that the Merge Kernel performs competitively with the Mallows kernel in low-dimensional settings, but significantly outperforms it in both optimization performance and computational efficiency as the dimension $n$ grows. Extensive evaluations on various permutation optimization benchmarks confirm our hypothesis, demonstrating that the Merge Kernel provides a scalable and more effective solution for Bayesian optimization in high-dimensional permutation spaces, thereby unlocking the potential for tackling previously intractable problems such as large-scale feature ordering and combinatorial neural architecture search.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从排序算法到可扩展核函数：高维排列空间中的贝叶斯优化
贝叶斯优化（BO）是黑箱优化的强大工具，但在高维排列空间中的应用受限于可扩展表示的定义难题。当前最先进的排列空间BO方法依赖于耗时的Ω(n²)成对比较，产生不适用于大规模排列的密集表示。为突破这一瓶颈，我们提出一种通过排序算法导出的核函数生成高效排列表示的新框架。在此框架中，Mallows核可视为枚举排序的特例。进一步，我们引入了**合并核**，利用归并排序的分治结构，以Θ(n log n)复杂度实现最低计算开销且无信息损失，有效捕捉排列结构。我们的核心观点是：合并核在低维场景下与Mallows核表现相当，但随着维度n增大，在优化性能和计算效率上显著优于后者。在多个排列优化基准上的广泛评估验证了我们的假设，表明合并核为高维排列空间中的贝叶斯优化提供了可扩展且更有效的解决方案，从而解锁了处理大规模特征排序和组合神经架构搜索等此前难以解决的问题的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current Bayesian Optimization methods in high-dimensional permutation spaces, this work proposes a novel kernel-based framework that leverages sorting algorithms to construct scalable permutation representations. The authors introduce the Merge Kernel, derived from merge sort’s divide-and-conquer structure, which achieves Θ(n log n) complexity while preserving full permutation structure—outperforming the traditional Mallows kernel in both optimization performance and computational efficiency as dimension n increases. Experimental results across multiple permutation optimization benchmarks confirm that the Merge Kernel enables effective and scalable Bayesian optimization, making previously intractable problems like large-scale feature ordering and combinatorial neural architecture search feasible.
为解决现有贝叶斯优化方法在高维排列空间中效率低下问题，本文提出一种基于排序算法的核函数框架，通过构建紧凑排列表示以实现可扩展优化。核心方法是引入“合并核”，其源于归并排序的分治结构，复杂度为Θ(n log n)，在保持完整排列结构的同时避免了马洛斯核的Ω(n²)密集表示。实验结果表明，合并核在低维场景下与马洛斯核表现相当，但在高维时显著优于后者，无论优化精度还是计算效率均大幅提升，从而为大规模特征排序与组合神经架构搜索等此前难以处理的问题提供了可行方案。
Vanishing Depth: Training Generalized Depth Adapters with Sinusoidal Depth Preprocessing for Pretrained RGB Encoders
Authors: Paul Koch, Jörg Krüger
First: 2025-03-25T15:19:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:29:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IntelliSys 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generalized metric depth understanding is critical for precise vision-guided robotics, which current state-of-the-art (SOTA) vision-encoders do not support. To address this, we propose a self-supervised training approach that extends pretrained RGB encoders with a depth adapter to incorporate and align metric depth into a combined latent space without interfering with the pretrained RGB feature extraction. In combination with our sinusoidal depth encoding, the depth adapter enables generalized and robust depth density and distribution invariant feature extraction. Our depth adapters improve a wide set of generalized RGB baselines across a spectrum of relevant RGBD downstream tasks in segmentation, pose estimation, and depth completion -- without the necessity of finetuning. Most importantly, we achieve 56.05 mIoU in the SUN-RGBD segmentation, while outperforming SOTA depth-aware and multi-modal encoders in our experiments. When no depth is present, one can activate our depth adapter with an empty map, use single pixel depth clues, or monocular depth estimation to include the depth aware feature extraction into subsequent downstream tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：消失的深度：通过正弦深度预处理训练通用深度适配器以增强预训练RGB编码器
通用度量深度理解对精确的视觉引导机器人至关重要，而当前最先进的视觉编码器尚不支持。为此，我们提出一种自监督训练方法，通过为预训练RGB编码器添加深度适配器，使其在不干扰预训练RGB特征提取的前提下，将度量深度纳入联合潜在空间并进行对齐。结合我们的正弦深度编码，深度适配器实现了对深度密度和分布不变的通用且鲁棒的特征提取。我们的深度适配器在多种RGBD下游任务（如分割、姿态估计和深度补全）中显著提升了广泛通用RGB基线模型的性能，且无需微调。最重要的是，在SUN-RGBD分割任务中，我们达到了56.05 mIoU的性能，优于现有最先进的深度感知和多模态编码器。当无深度信息时，可通过空地图激活深度适配器，或利用单像素深度线索或单目深度估计，将深度感知特征提取引入后续下游任务。
Summary / 总结
To enable pretrained RGB encoders to understand metric depth without disrupting their original visual features, the authors introduce a self-supervised depth adapter trained with sinusoidal depth preprocessing. This adapter integrates depth information into the encoder’s latent space while preserving RGB capabilities, allowing robust performance across depth-invariant tasks. Experiments show the adapter significantly boosts performance on RGBD tasks like segmentation and depth completion without finetuning, achieving 56.05 mIoU on SUN-RGBD segmentation — outperforming existing depth-aware and multi-modal models. The adapter can also operate with minimal depth input, such as single-pixel cues or monocular estimates, making it flexible for real-world deployment.
为使预训练RGB编码器无需微调即可处理度量深度，作者提出一种结合正弦深度预处理的自监督深度适配器，该适配器将深度信息融入编码器的潜在空间而不干扰原有视觉特征。该方法在分割、姿态估计和深度补全等RGBD下游任务中表现稳定，显著优于现有深度感知模型。尤其在SUN-RGBD分割任务中达到56.05 mIoU，超越当前最先进多模态编码器，且在无深度输入时可通过空图或单目深度估计激活深度感知特征提取。
Multi-fidelity aerodynamic data fusion by autoencoder transfer learning
Authors: Javier Nieto-Centenero, Esther Andrés, Rodrigo Castellanos
First: 2025-12-15T08:06:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:27:21+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate aerodynamic prediction often relies on high-fidelity simulations; however, their prohibitive computational costs severely limit their applicability in data-driven modeling. This limitation motivates the development of multi-fidelity strategies that leverage inexpensive low-fidelity information without compromising accuracy. Addressing this challenge, this work presents a multi-fidelity deep learning framework that combines autoencoder-based transfer learning with a newly developed Multi-Split Conformal Prediction (MSCP) strategy to achieve uncertainty-aware aerodynamic data fusion under extreme data scarcity. The methodology leverages abundant Low-Fidelity (LF) data to learn a compact latent physics representation, which acts as a frozen knowledge base for a decoder that is subsequently fine-tuned using scarce HF samples. Tested on surface-pressure distributions for NACA airfoils (2D) and a transonic wing (3D) databases, the model successfully corrects LF deviations and achieves high-accuracy pressure predictions using minimal HF training data. Furthermore, the MSCP framework produces robust, actionable uncertainty bands with pointwise coverage exceeding 95%. By combining extreme data efficiency with uncertainty quantification, this work offers a scalable and reliable solution for aerodynamic regression in data-scarce environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于自编码器迁移学习的多保真度气动数据融合
精确的气动预测通常依赖高保真度仿真，但其高昂的计算成本严重限制了其在数据驱动建模中的应用。为此，本研究提出一种结合自编码器迁移学习与新开发的多分割一致性预测（MSCP）策略的多保真度深度学习框架，以在极端数据稀缺条件下实现具有不确定性感知的气动数据融合。该方法利用丰富的低保真度（LF）数据学习紧凑的物理隐含表征，作为解码器的冻结知识库，随后使用稀缺的高保真度（HF）样本进行微调。在NACA翼型（2D）和跨音速机翼（3D）数据库的表面压力分布测试中，模型成功修正了LF偏差，并在极少量HF训练数据下实现了高精度压力预测。此外，MSCP框架生成了稳健且可操作的不确定性区间，点覆盖率达95%以上。该方法结合了极端数据效率与不确定性量化，为数据稀缺环境下的气动回归提供了一种可扩展且可靠的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the high computational cost of high-fidelity (HF) aerodynamic simulations, this study proposes a deep learning framework that fuses low-fidelity (LF) and HF data using autoencoder transfer learning. The method first trains an autoencoder on abundant LF data to extract a compact latent physics representation, then fine-tunes a decoder using scarce HF samples. Evaluated on 2D NACA airfoil and 3D transonic wing datasets, the model corrects LF prediction errors and achieves high-accuracy pressure predictions with minimal HF data. Additionally, the Multi-Split Conformal Prediction (MSCP) strategy generates uncertainty bands with pointwise coverage exceeding 95%, enabling reliable predictions under extreme data scarcity.
为解决高保真气动模拟计算成本过高的问题，本文提出一种融合低保真（LF）与高保真（HF）数据的多保真深度学习框架。该方法利用自编码器从大量LF数据中提取紧凑的物理潜变量表征，并将其冻结作为解码器的先验知识，随后用少量HF样本进行微调。结合多分割置信预测（MSCP）策略，模型实现不确定性感知的预测。在2D NACA翼型和3D跨音速机翼数据集上测试表明，该模型能有效修正LF偏差，仅用极少HF样本即获得高精度压力预测，且MSCP生成的点态置信区间覆盖率超过95%。
MAD: Manifold Attracted Diffusion
Authors: Dennis Elbrächter, Giovanni S. Alberti, Matteo Santacesaria
Venue: Forty-third International Conference on Machine Learning, 2026
First: 2025-09-29T12:40:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:24:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Score-based diffusion models are a highly effective method for generating samples from a distribution of images. We consider scenarios where the training data comes from a noisy version of the target distribution, and present an efficiently implementable modification of the inference procedure to generate noiseless samples. Our approach is motivated by the manifold hypothesis, according to which meaningful data is concentrated around some low-dimensional manifold of a high-dimensional ambient space. The central idea is that noise manifests as low magnitude variation in off-manifold directions in contrast to the relevant variation of the desired distribution which is mostly confined to on-manifold directions. We introduce the notion of an extended score and show that, in a simplified setting, it can be used to reduce small variations to zero, while leaving large variations mostly unchanged. We describe how its approximation can be computed efficiently from an approximation to the standard score and demonstrate its efficacy on toy problems, synthetic data, and real data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAD：流形吸引扩散
基于分数的扩散模型是生成图像分布样本的一种高效方法。我们考虑训练数据来自目标分布的噪声版本的情形，并提出一种可高效实现的推理过程修改方法，以生成无噪声样本。我们的方法受流形假设启发，即有意义的数据集中在高维空间中的低维流形上。核心思想是：噪声表现为流形外方向的小幅变化，而目标分布的相关变化主要局限于流形内方向。我们引入扩展分数的概念，并证明在简化情形下，它可将小幅变化归零，同时基本保持大幅变化不变。我们描述了如何从标准分数的近似值高效计算扩展分数的近似值，并在玩具问题、合成数据和真实数据上验证了其有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the manifold hypothesis that meaningful data lies on a low-dimensional manifold within high-dimensional space, this paper proposes MAD (Manifold Attracted Diffusion), a modification of score-based diffusion models to generate noiseless samples from noisy training data. The method introduces an extended score that targets off-manifold noise variations, suppressing small perturbations while preserving large, meaningful variations aligned with the manifold. By approximating this extended score efficiently from the standard score, MAD achieves improved sample quality on toy problems, synthetic datasets, and real-world data without requiring additional training.
基于数据集中在低维流形上的假设，本文提出MAD（流形吸引扩散），对基于分数的扩散模型进行改进，以从含噪训练数据中生成无噪样本。该方法引入扩展分数，专门抑制流形外的小幅扰动，同时保留流形内主要变化，从而有效去噪而不破坏目标分布。在玩具问题、合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，MAD在含噪数据场景下显著优于标准扩散模型，生成样本质量更高。
MCR-VQGAN: A Scalable and Cost-Effective Tau PET Synthesis Approach for Alzheimer's Disease Imaging
Authors: Jin Young Kim, Jeremy Hudson, Jeongchul Kim, Qing Lyu, Christopher T. Whitlow
First: 2025-12-17T20:22:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T15:20:42+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in IEEE Access. 14 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tau positron emission tomography (PET) is a critical diagnostic modality for Alzheimer's disease (AD), but its widespread clinical adoption is hindered by radiation exposure, limited availability, high clinical workload, and substantial financial costs. To address these limitations, we propose the Multi-scale CBAM Residual Vector Quantized Generative Adversarial Network (MCR-VQGAN) to synthesize high-fidelity tau PET images from structural T1-weighted MRI. MCR-VQGAN advances the standard VQGAN architecture through three enhancements: multi-scale convolutions, ResNet blocks, and Convolutional Block Attention Modules (CBAM), which collectively improve the capture of local and global features. Using 222 paired T1-weighted MRI and tau PET scans from the ADNI database, we trained and compared MCR-VQGAN against cGAN, WGAN-GP, CycleGAN, and baseline VQGAN. MCR-VQGAN achieved superior image synthesis performance across all metrics (MSE = 0.0056 +/- 0.0061, PSNR = 30.65 +/- 4.47 dB, SSIM = 0.9263 +/- 0.0469). A CNN-based AD classifier trained on real tau PET achieved comparable accuracy on real (63.64%) and synthetic (65.91%) images, indicating that diagnostically relevant features are preserved. Regional SUVR-equivalent analysis across Braak-defined ROIs further indicated strong agreement between real and synthetic tau PET (Pearson r = 0.78-0.88; ICC = 0.71-0.84), with the strongest agreement in Braak V/VI (ICC = 0.838). Together, these results suggest that MCR-VQGAN offers a promising and scalable surrogate for conventional tau PET imaging, potentially improving the accessibility of tau biomarkers for AD research and clinical workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MCR-VQGAN：一种用于阿尔茨海默病成像的可扩展且经济高效的Tau PET合成方法
Tau正电子发射断层扫描（PET）是阿尔茨海默病（AD）的关键诊断手段，但其临床广泛应用受限于辐射暴露、设备稀缺、临床工作量大和高昂费用。为解决这些问题，我们提出多尺度CBAM残差矢量量化生成对抗网络（MCR-VQGAN），可从结构T1加权MRI合成高保真度Tau PET图像。MCR-VQGAN在标准VQGAN架构基础上进行了三项改进：多尺度卷积、ResNet模块和卷积块注意力模块（CBAM），共同提升局部与全局特征的捕捉能力。基于ADNI数据库的222对T1加权MRI与Tau PET扫描数据，我们训练并比较了MCR-VQGAN与cGAN、WGAN-GP、CycleGAN及基础VQGAN。MCR-VQGAN在所有评估指标上均表现最优（MSE=0.0056±0.0061，PSNR=30.65±4.47 dB，SSIM=0.9263±0.0469）。基于真实Tau PET训练的CNN分类器在真实图像（63.64%）和合成图像（65.91%）上均取得相近准确率，表明诊断相关特征得以保留。在Braak定义的脑区ROI中进行区域SUVR等效分析进一步显示，真实与合成Tau PET高度一致（Pearson r=0.78-0.88；ICC=0.71-0.84），其中Braak V/VI区域一致性最强（ICC=0.838）。综合结果表明，MCR-VQGAN有望成为传统Tau PET成像的可行替代方案，提升AD研究与临床工作流程中Tau生物标志物的可及性。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the clinical and financial barriers of tau PET imaging for Alzheimer's disease, researchers developed MCR-VQGAN, a generative model that synthesizes high-fidelity tau PET images from T1-weighted MRI scans using multi-scale convolutions, ResNet blocks, and CBAM modules to enhance feature capture. Evaluated against cGAN, WGAN-GP, CycleGAN, and baseline VQGAN on 222 ADNI pairs, MCR-VQGAN outperformed all competitors in image quality metrics (MSE=0.0056, PSNR=30.65 dB, SSIM=0.9263) and maintained diagnostic utility, as a CNN classifier achieved 65.91% accuracy on synthetic images versus 63.64% on real ones. Regional analysis across Braak stages showed strong correlation (Pearson r=0.78–0.88, ICC=0.71–0.84), with highest agreement in Braak V/VI (ICC=0.838), demonstrating MCR-VQGAN’s potential as a scalable, cost-effective surrogate for tau PET.
为解决tau PET影像在阿尔茨海默病诊断中因辐射、稀缺、成本高和工作量大而难以普及的问题，作者提出MCR-VQGAN模型，该模型在标准VQGAN基础上引入多尺度卷积、ResNet模块和CBAM注意力机制以增强局部与全局特征提取能力。在ADNI数据库222对T1加权MRI与tau PET数据上训练后，MCR-VQGAN在图像合成指标上全面优于cGAN、WGAN-GP、CycleGAN及基础VQGAN（MSE 0.0056，PSNR 30.65 dB，SSIM 0.9263）。基于真实tau PET训练的CNN分类器在真实与合成图像上准确率分别为63.64%和65.91%，表明诊断相关特征得以保留；在Braak分区的区域SUVR分析中，真实与合成图像高度一致（Pearson相关系数0.78–0.88，ICC 0.71–0.84），其中Braak V/VI区域一致性最高（ICC 0.838），证明MCR-VQGAN可作为可靠且可扩展的tau PET替代方案。
